### [1] [Spectral densification and macroscopic phase delay of gravitational echoes from exotic compact objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29273)
*Corentin Guigot*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究奇异致密天体（Exotic Compact Objects, ECOs）产生的引力波回波信号，证明光子球处的波色散会导致回波频谱从恒定自由光谱范围（Free Spectral Range）偏离，呈现双曲型频谱加密（hyperbolic spectral densification）。通过基于Riccati方程和宏观阻抗映射的分析框架，提取高精细度共振谱，并发现波主导区域（ℓ∈[2,10]）中偏离半经典极限的行为遵循L^(-3/2)反幂律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有回波现象学模型通常假设恒定自由光谱范围，依赖于几何光学近似，忽略了光子球处的波动色散效应。该假设可能导致对真实回波频谱的系统性误解，因此需要更精确的分析框架来刻画这一偏差。

Method: 作者采用基于Riccati方程和宏观阻抗映射（macroscopic impedance mapping）的分析框架，无需半经典近似即可提取高精细度共振谱。通过该框架刻画了从eikonal几何渐近极限（ℓ≫1）到波隧穿主导的四极模（ℓ=2）的结构转变。

Result: 在波主导区域（ℓ∈[2,10]）内，偏离半经典极限的宏观偏差遵循L^(-3/2)反幂律。此外，当膜相位移动与频率无关时，这种宏观频谱加密可将外部时空的结构色散与边界微观物理解耦开来。

Conclusion: 光子球处的波色散效应导致回波频谱出现双曲型加密，而非传统几何光学近似的恒定自由光谱范围。该效应在低角动量模中尤为显著，且可作为探针隔离外部时空的结构特性，为引力波回波观测提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave echoes from Exotic Compact Objects (ECOs) provide an observable probe for horizon-scale physics. Standard phenomenological models for these signals typically assume a constant Free Spectral Range, relying on the geometric optics approximation. In this work, we demonstrate that wave dispersion at the photon sphere induces a systematic deviation from this assumption, manifesting instead as a hyperbolic spectral densification. By employing an analytical framework based on the Riccati equation and macroscopic impedance mapping, we extract the spectrum of these high-finesse resonances without semi-classical approximations. We characterize the structural transition from the eikonal geometric asymptote ($\ell \gg 1$) down to the wave-tunneling dominated quadrupolar mode ($\ell=2$). In this wave-dominated regime ($\ell \in [2, 10]$), the macroscopic deviation from the semi-classical limit is governed by a phenomenological $\mathcal{L}^{-3/2}$ inverse power law. Finally, we show that this macroscopic densification isolates the structural dispersion of the external spacetime, decoupled from the boundary microphysics, provided the membrane phase shift is frequency-independent.

</details>

### [2] [Covariance-Driven Momentum Rectification at Liquid-Vapor Interfaces Near Wetting Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28938)
*Nelson Bolívar,Gabriel Abellán,Ivaylo Vasilev*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个守恒连续介质模型，以局部响应场与零均值切向驱动之间的协方差为核心对象，建立显式动量账本，推导出液-气界面上由马兰戈尼效应（Marangoni effect）驱动的相选择性漂移定律U∝εhΘsinφ，并系统分析了润湿敏感性、相变、波动、热输运等通道对整流动量传递（rectified momentum transfer）的贡献与约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零均值强迫（zero-mean forcing）可以在介质具有空间结构响应且相关对称性被破坏时产生定向输运，但现有理论缺乏统一的守恒框架来评估各种动量携带通道（如表面张力梯度、润湿动力学、汽相交换、毛细波和声波、电离子屏蔽、热噪声、边界柔顺性）的贡献。

Method: 发展了一个守恒连续介质模型，核心对象是局部响应场M与零均值切向驱动f之间的协方差〈Mf〉−〈M〉〈f〉，将其嵌入显式动量账本（momentum ledger）中。在最小弥散界面（diffuse-interface）实现中，推导出U∝εhΘsinφ的相选择性漂移定律；通过一阶旋节线（spinodal）条件、临界润湿和体相临界性分析增益因子行为；将附加的动量储库作为补偿通道处理，通过符号反转、标度律和预算闭合加以隔离。

Result: 推导得到了马兰戈尼驱动的液-气界面在结构化壁面上方的相选择性漂流定律U∝εhΘsinφ，对称性恢复时该定律精确为零。润湿敏感性表现为有界增益因子：一阶旋节线条件提供有效放大区间，临界润湿使通道饱和（界面解离移除短程驱动），体相临界性则随界面消失而抑制该通道。简化数值计算验证了相选择规则、有界旋节线增益、动量预算闭合、横向手性及网格收敛性。

Conclusion: 该工作既不提出新的微观力，也不给出装置级声明，而是提供一种对称性约束的核算方案（symmetry-constrained accounting scheme），用于水和基界面系统中整流动量传递的系统性分析。其价值在于将各动量携带通道统一纳入可隔离、可闭合的守恒框架中。

Abstract: Zero-mean forcing can generate directed transport when a medium responds in a spatially structured way and the relevant symmetries are broken. Liquid-vapor interfaces are a useful setting for this problem because surface-tension gradients, wetting dynamics, vapor exchange, capillary and acoustic waves, electro-ionic screening, thermal noise, and boundary compliance can all carry momentum. We develop a conservative continuum model in which the central object is the covariance between a local response field and a zero-mean tangential drive, $\langle Mf\rangle-\langle M\rangle\langle f\rangle$, embedded in an explicit momentum ledger. In a minimal diffuse-interface realization, this covariance gives the phase-selective drift law $U\proptoε_hΘ\sin\varphi$ for a Marangoni-driven liquid-vapor interface above a structured wall, with exact nulls when the symmetry is restored. Wetting susceptibility then acts as a bounded gain factor: first-order spinodal conditions provide the useful amplification regime, critical wetting saturates because interfacial unbinding removes the short-range drive, and bulk criticality suppresses the channel as the interface disappears. Additional reservoirs - phase change, waves, thermal transport, electro-ionic coupling, compliance, and fluctuations - are treated as compensating channels to be isolated by sign reversals, scaling laws, and budget closure. The result is neither a new microscopic force nor an apparatus-level claim, but a symmetry-constrained accounting scheme for rectified momentum transfer in water-based interfacial systems. Reduced numerical calculations illustrate the phase-selection rules, bounded spinodal gain, momentum-budget closure, transverse chirality, and grid convergence.

</details>


### [3] [Coalescence-induced alignment of anisotropic particles in drying sessile droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29201)
*Johannes Schöttner,Qingguang Xie,Jens Harting*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过介观模拟（mesoscale simulation）探究了液滴打印中相邻液滴聚并（coalescence）与蒸发如何共同决定各向异性颗粒在固着液滴中的取向和再分布，并提出了一个基于曲率和体积不对称性的几何标度律，将模拟结果归约为一条主曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性颗粒（anisotropic particles）的取向强烈决定印刷材料的功能性质，但已有研究大多集中于单一蒸发液滴中的颗粒取向。在实际液滴打印过程中，相邻液滴会发生聚并并产生快速毛细流动（capillary flow），从而显著改变最终形貌，这一机制尚未被系统研究。

Method: 使用介观模拟方法，系统考察了具有不同接触角（contact angle）和体积的固着液滴在聚并及后续蒸发过程中的颗粒取向和再分布行为，并基于曲率与体积不对称性推导了几何标度关系。

Result: 聚并早期，所有接触角与体积不对称性组合下，沿聚并方向的平均向列序（mean nematic order）均增大；后期则依液滴几何结构或继续增大或减小。模拟结果可归约为一条主曲线，识别出有效几何不对称参数控制聚并末期的平均向列序。蒸发阶段，小接触角下接触线钉扎时间更长，更好保持取向；大接触角促进接触线移动，削弱取向但产生更强的浓度梯度。

Conclusion: 液滴聚并与蒸发共同决定了最终沉积物中颗粒的取向结构，其中接触角是关键调控参数：小接触角利于保持聚并诱导的取向，大接触角则在削弱取向的同时增强浓度梯度。所提出的几何标度律为预测和调控打印材料的功能性质提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Alignment of anisotropic particles strongly governs the functional properties of printed materials, yet most studies have focused on particle alignment in single evaporating droplets. In droplet- based printing, however, neighboring droplets can coalesce, generating rapid capillary flows that redistribute material and markedly affect the final morphology. Here, we use mesoscale simulations to investigate how droplet coalescence and subsequent evaporation jointly determine alignment and redistribution in sessile droplets with different contact angles and volumes. During the early stages of coalescence, the mean nematic order along the coalescence direction increases for all combinations of contact-angle and volume asymmetries of the droplets. At later times, the mean nematic order either continues to increase or decreases, depending on the droplet geometry. We derive a geometric scaling based on curvature and volume asymmetry and show that the simulation results collapse onto a master curve, identifying an effective geometric asymmetry parameter that governs the mean nematic order at the end of coalescence. During evaporation, the contact angle strongly influences how the coalescence-induced orientational structure is transferred to the final deposit. For small contact angles, the contact line remains pinned for a longer duration, better preserving alignment. In contrast, larger contact angles promote contact-line motion, which weakens alignment, as reflected by a reduced mean nematic order, while simultaneously generating stronger concentration gradients in the final deposit.

</details>


### [4] [Weak-electrolyte diffusiophoresis for rigid colloids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29230)
*Subrata Majhi,Huanshu Tan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文建立了化学惰性刚性球形胶体在单价弱电解质中因中性溶质可逆解离为离子而产生的扩散泳（diffusiophoresis）模型。在快速反应极限下求解耦合的Nernst-Planck、Poisson与Stokes方程，发现在Debye-Hückel极限中迁移率等于完全解离响应的二分之一加有限双电层修正；超出该极限后出现支路选择性响应，且该反应-极化耦合无法仅通过调整完全解离模型中的体相离子强度来重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散泳模型多针对完全解离电解质，而许多实际体系（如弱电解质）中存在中性溶质的可逆解离-缔合反应，其对胶体迁移行为的影响尚未被系统建模。

Method: 发展一个带固定表面电荷的惰性刚性球形胶体在单价弱电解质中的扩散泳模型，设中性物种浓度施加弱远场梯度；在快速反应极限下利用局部质量作用定律与体相电中性确定远场离子梯度，利用体相零电流条件确定扩散势梯度。对任意双电层厚度求解耦合的Nernst-Planck、Poisson和Stokes方程，在梯度强度上线性化，同时保留非线性Poisson-Boltzmann平衡。

Result: 在Debye-Hückel极限下，迁移率等于匹配的完全解离响应的二分之一加一个由中性离子耦合引起的有限双电层修正项，该修正项在Hückel与Smoluchowski极限下均消失。超出该极限的代表性体系数值解显示，随表面电位幅值增大出现支路选择性响应：当反离子比共离子慢时，解离-缔合削弱了阻滞性的浓差极化层，使迁移率可超过完全解离值；当反离子较快时，响应保持接近由质量作用定律设定的二分之一标度。

Conclusion: 弱电解质的可逆解离反应对扩散泳迁移率产生显著的支路选择性影响，且这种反应-极化耦合无法仅通过调节完全解离模型中的体相离子强度（即Debye长度）来复现。

Abstract: We develop a model for the diffusiophoresis of a chemically inert, rigid spherical colloid with fixed surface charge in a monovalent weak electrolyte, in which a neutral solute reversibly dissociates into ions. A weak far-field gradient is imposed in the neutral-species concentration. In the fast-reaction limit, local mass action and bulk electroneutrality determine the far-field ionic gradients, while the bulk zero-current condition determines the diffusion-potential gradient. We solve the coupled Nernst-Planck, Poisson and Stokes equations for arbitrary double-layer thickness, linearising in the gradient strength while retaining the nonlinear Poisson-Boltzmann equilibrium. In the Debye-Hückel limit, the mobility consists of one half of the matched fully dissociated response and a finite-double-layer correction due to neutral-ion coupling; the correction vanishes in both the Hückel and Smoluchowski limits. Beyond this limit, numerical solutions for the representative systems reveal a branch-selective response as the surface potential magnitude increases. When the counterion is slower than the co-ion, dissociation-association weakens a retarding concentration-polarisation layer, allowing the mobility to exceed the fully dissociated value. When the counterion is faster, the response remains close to the one-half scaling set by mass action. This reaction-polarisation coupling cannot be reproduced by adjusting only the bulk ionic strength, and hence the Debye length, in a fully dissociated model.

</details>


### [5] [Mechanical isotropy of heterogeneous octahedral materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29259)
*Jehoon Moon,Seunghwan Lee,Gisoo Lee,Jeongun Lee,Hansohl Cho*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文证明了异质八面体材料（heterogeneous octahedral materials）在临界组分体积分数下可实现完全弹性各向同性（elastic isotropy），且该临界点几乎与组分刚度比无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 八面体网络是多种架构材料（architected materials）的基本构筑单元，但异质八面体材料的弹性各向同性行为尚未被系统阐明，作者旨在揭示其调控机制。

Method: 通过解析与数值手段研究异质八面体材料在组分体积分数变化下的弹性各向异性比（anisotropy ratio）a，结合微结构分析和3D打印原型实验验证。

Result: 各向异性比a在临界组分体积分数处从a>1过渡至a<1，源于主导变形模式的改变；该行为与基于简立方（SC）和体心立方（BCC）复合晶格构建的异质材料具有高度相似的几何与连通性特征。

Conclusion: 调节八面体材料中组分体积分数可调控弹性各向异性，与SC-BCC材料中调节BCC-SC组分比机制一致，为设计力学各向同性架构材料提供了新途径，并通过3D打印原型验证了其力学各向同性。

Abstract: An octahedral network has been widely used as a fundamental building block for diverse architected materials. Here, we demonstrate that heterogeneous octahedral materials can achieve complete elastic isotropy at a critical constituent volume fraction, nearly independent of the constituent stiffness ratio. The anisotropy ratio, a, transitions from a > 1 to a < 1 at the critical constituent volume fraction, due to a change in the dominant deformation modes. Microstructural analysis reveals that the geometry and connectivity of the heterogeneous octahedral materials are very similar to those of heterogeneous materials constructed on combined simple cubic (SC) and body-centered cubic (BCC) lattices exhibiting opposite elastic anisotropy. More importantly, we demonstrate that varying the constituent volume fractions in the octahedral materials governs elastic anisotropy, similarly to tuning the BCC-to-SC composition ratio in the SC-BCC materials widely employed for designing mechanically isotropic architected materials. The mechanical isotropy of the heterogeneous octahedral materials is further assessed using 3D-printed prototypes.

</details>


### [6] [Phoretic flow in a three-dimensional wedge geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29262)
*Abdallah Daddi-Moussa-Ider,Semyon Yakubovich,Maciej Lisicki*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个理论框架，用于分析三维楔形几何中化学驱动（chemically driven）的泳动流（phoretic flows），在扩散主导（diffusion-dominated）条件下利用Fourier-Kontorovich-Lebedev谱表示求解扩散场和Stokes流场，为楔形受限微流控系统中的泳动泵提供解析基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解化学诱导表面输运如何产生受限几何中的流体运动，对于微观泵送装置和主动微流控系统（active microfluidic systems）的理性设计至关重要。

Method: 采用Fourier-Kontorovich-Lebedev谱表示构建扩散与流体动力学问题，利用楔形的平动不变性和径向结构；为浓度场推导反射及混合反射-吸收边界的格林函数（Green's functions），并通过Papkovich-Neuber表示构建三维Stokes流以获得速度场的显式谱解。

Result: 格林函数在可通约（commensurate）楔角的条件下可化简为有限镜像和形式或闭式表达式；滑移速度（slip velocity）用于构三维Stokes流，得到速度场的显式谱解。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了楔形受限环境中泳动泵输运的格林函数框架，为有限微流控系统中化学驱动输运的数值模拟提供了解析基准。

Abstract: Understanding how chemically induced surface transport generates fluid motion in confined geometries is essential for the rational design of microscale pumping devices and active microfluidic systems. Here we develop a theoretical framework for chemically driven phoretic flows in a three-dimensional wedge geometry in the diffusion-dominated regime. We formulate both the diffusion and hydrodynamic problems using a Fourier-Kontorovich-Lebedev spectral representation, exploiting the translational invariance and radial structure of the wedge. Green's functions for the concentration field are derived for reflecting and mixed reflecting-absorbing boundaries, reducing to finite image-like sums or closed-form expressions for commensurate wedge angles. The resulting slip velocity is then used to construct the three-dimensional Stokes flow through the Papkovich-Neuber representation, yielding explicit spectral solutions for the velocity field. These results establish a Green's-function framework for phoretic pumping in wedge-shaped confinement and provide analytical benchmarks for numerical simulations of chemically driven transport in confined microfluidic systems.

</details>


### [7] [Substrate contact angle governs microgel shape, stiffness and deposition pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29477)
*M. Friederike Schulte,Sebastian S. Meyer,Timon Kratzenberg,Simon Schog,Jesco M. Schönfelder,Silke Klein-Kormelink,Tim Blinzer,Matthias Karg,Walter Richtering,Marcel Rey*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文发现基底润湿性（substrate wettability）会在干燥前机械性预处理软微凝胶（soft microgel），亲水与疏水基底分别产生不同形态和刚度分布的单微凝胶状态，这些状态进而决定Langmuir-Blodgett沉积单层在干燥过程中的重排行为与最终结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软微凝胶广泛用作二维组装的柔性构筑单元（building block），但固态基底在界面沉积后常被视为被动支撑物。本研究旨在揭示基底润湿性对微凝胶机械状态的主动调控作用，并建立从界面自组装到转移干燥后最终结构之间的缺失联系。

Method: 采用液内力-体积原子力显微镜（in-liquid force-volume atomic force microscopy），系统测量同一批次微凝胶在不同接触角基底上的水合形态与刚度分布，并结合Langmuir-Blodgett沉积方法考察单层在干燥过程中的响应。

Result: 结果显示：疏水基底诱导微凝胶铺展、扁平化并内部硬化；亲水基底则保留更高、更软、接触面积更小的微凝胶。在亲水基底上，干燥过程中弱固定微凝胶受浸没-毛细力（immersion-capillary force）驱动重排，产生冠-冠与核-核接触态及表观等结构转变；在疏水基底上，扁平硬化微凝胶被更强固定，抑制毛细驱动的重排并保留转移后的界面组装结构。

Conclusion: 基底控制的微凝胶力学被确立为连接界面自组装与转移干燥后最终结构之间的缺失环节，为二维软物质组装的基底调控提供了新的物理机制。

Abstract: Soft microgels are widely used as deformable building blocks for two-dimensional assemblies, yet solid substrates are often treated as passive supports after interfacial deposition. Here, we show that substrate wettability mechanically preconditions soft microgels before drying. Using in-liquid force-volume atomic force microscopy, we find that the same microgels adopt markedly different hydrated shapes and stiffness profiles depending on substrate contact angle: hydrophobic substrates induce spreading, flattening, and internal stiffening, whereas hydrophilic substrates preserve taller, softer microgels with smaller contact areas. These single-microgel states can explain how Langmuir-Blodgett-deposited monolayers respond during drying. On hydrophilic substrates, the observed assemblies are consistent with soft and weakly immobilized microgels rearranging under immersion-capillary forces, producing distinct corona-corona and core-core contact states and an apparent isostructural transition. On hydrophobic substrates, the flattened and stiffened microgels are more strongly immobilized, likely suppressing capillary-driven rearrangements and largely preserving the transferred interfacial assembly structure. These findings establish substrate-controlled microgel mechanics as the missing link between interfacial self-assembly and the final structures observed after transfer and drying.

</details>


### [8] [Elastodynamics from Eulerian Poisson-bracket formalism: application to chiral odd solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29521)
*Cheng-Tai Lee,Tomer Markovich*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文发展了欧拉（Eulerian）泊松括号（Poisson-bracket, PB）形式体系在弹性系统中的应用方法，该系统通常以拉格朗日（Lagrangian）空间中的势能来描述。研究表明，欧拉表述会产生拉格朗日表述中不存在的额外非线性项，这些非线性项源于坐标变换下的粗粒化体积变化及相邻粗粒化体积间的粒子流动，在非线性效应重要时必须保留。作者以手性活性固体为例，从欧拉PB形式体系中直接恢复了奇弹性模量（odd elastic modulus），证明了该方法能捕捉驱动活性固体中涌现的非线性弹性行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松括号形式体系虽已在多种宏观体系中广泛应用，但传统上弹性固体采用拉格朗日表述、流体采用欧拉表述。然而，对于真实空间中天然定义的物理现象（如黏弹性响应、移动界面、场致结构变化），需要发展弹性体系的欧拉表述，并厘清其与拉格朗日版本的一致性。

Method: 作者系统建立了将欧拉PB形式体系应用于势能通常写于拉格朗日空间的弹性系统的方法，并比较其与拉格朗日对应版本的异同；通过分析坐标变换下粗粒化体积的变化和粒子在相邻粗粒化体积间的流动来源，识别出欧拉表述特有的额外非线性项。以有限尺寸粒子的手性活性固体作为示例体系进行验证。

Result: 欧拉表述会生成拉格朗日表述中不存在的额外非线性项，其来源包括坐标变换引起的粗粒化体积变化以及粒子跨越粗粒化体积边界的流动。在手性活性固体中，活性力矩驱动粒子内部旋转并产生几何非线性，这些非线性导致了奇弹性模量的出现，该模量在应力-应变响应中非互易地耦合两种不同的剪切模式。

Conclusion: 直接从欧拉PB形式体系恢复奇弹性模量，证明了该形式体系能够捕捉驱动活性固体中涌现的非线性弹性行为。这一方法的优势在于其应力天然在真实空间中测量，适合描述欧拉视角下自然定义的固体现象。

Abstract: The Poisson-bracket (PB) formalism is widely used to derive dynamics of coarse-grained (CG) fields to capture large-scale physics, extending the role of PBs in classical particle mechanics to macroscopic fields. It has been applied to fluctuations in critical phenomena, hydrodynamics of liquid crystals, liquid crystal elastomers, tissues, and the emergence of odd viscosity from spinning particles. The PB formalism can be formulated in either the Lagrangian framework, using reference space, or the Eulerian framework, using real space. Conventionally, the Lagrangian formulation is used for elastic solids, and the Eulerian one for fluids. However, growing interest in Eulerian descriptions of solids has emerged for phenomena naturally defined in real space, such as viscoelastic responses, moving interfaces, and field-induced structural changes in particles. Here we develop a systematic formulation for applying the Eulerian PB formalism to elastic systems with potentials typically written in Lagrangian space, and clarify its consistency with the Lagrangian counterpart. We show that the Eulerian formulation generates additional nonlinearities absent in the Lagrangian framework. Such nonlinearities originate from CG volume changes under coordinate transformation and from particle flow across neighboring CG volumes. They must be retained when nonlinear effects are important. To illustrate, we study chiral active solids of finite-sized particles, where active torques drive internal particle rotations and generate geometric nonlinearities. These nonlinearities give rise to the odd elastic modulus, which non-reciprocally couples two different shear modes in stress-strain response. By recovering this modulus directly from the Eulerian PB formalism, we demonstrate its ability to capture emergent nonlinear elastic behavior in driven active solids, whose stresses are naturally measured in real space.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超选择性多价结合能够实现尖锐的受体密度判别，但其对数响应的微观测量含义尚不明确，需要建立选择性与微观受体统计之间的定量关系。

Method: 推导了选择性的精确响应分解，将其拆分为受体态重加权（receptor-state reweighting）与直接占据响应（direct occupation response）两项；在稀薄淬灭泊松极限下证明直接项消失，选择性等于结合粒子下方平均受体过量，并可通过单密度下的共配准受体-粒子图像测量；在Beyond Poisson统计下以Fano因子归一化过量，并通过格点与离格（off-lattice）模拟验证。

Result: 在Beyond Poisson统计下，线性计数响应中受体过量需以Fano因子归一化；分布形状变化需要完整的计数分辨响应；格点模拟验证了不同受体统计下的关系，离格模拟表明粒子拥挤效应通过直接项进入响应。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了将多价选择性与吸附所优选取样的局部受体涨落相联系的涨落-响应框架，为多价靶向和感知中的受体密度判别提供了微观理论基础。

Abstract: Superselective multivalent binding enables sharp receptor-density discrimination in targeting and sensing, but what its logarithmic response measures microscopically remains unclear. Here, we derive an exact response decomposition of the selectivity into receptor-state reweighting and a direct occupation response. In the dilute quenched Poisson limit, the direct term vanishes and selectivity is exactly the mean receptor excess beneath bound particles, which can be measured at a single density from a sufficiently large co-registered receptor-particle image. Beyond Poisson statistics, the excess is normalized by the Fano factor for a linear count response, while changes in distribution shape require the full count-resolved response. Lattice simulations verify these relations across distinct receptor statistics, while off-lattice simulations show that particle crowding enters through the direct term. These results establish a fluctuation-response framework linking multivalent selectivity to the local receptor fluctuations preferentially sampled by adsorption.

</details>


### [10] [Structure, Diffusion, and Relaxation in a Charge-Neutral ProTalpha-Histone H1 Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29639)
*Aniket Bhattacharya*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过单珠-残余粗粒化（single-bead-per-residue coarse-grained）模型，在2至12 bar压力范围内研究由50个Prothymosin alpha（ProTalpha）和40个Histone H1分子组成的近电荷中性凝聚体。研究发现链尺寸对压力不敏感，扩散系数随压力单调下降（0.22至0.06 nm²/ns），链弛豫符合Rouse标度（ProTalpha）或因其球状结构域偏离（Histone H1），且瞬态静电接触寿命远短于Rouse弛豫时间，表明系统处于快速交换（fast-exchange）区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由带相反电荷的内在无序蛋白（intrinsically disordered proteins, IDPs）形成的凝聚体是理解瞬态静电相互作用如何调控生物分子组装结构与动力学的模型系统。本文旨在探究压力这一外界变量对近电荷中性蛋白质凝聚体链构象、扩散及弛豫行为的影响。

Method: 采用单珠-残余粗粒化模型，将HPS疏水性（hydropathy scale, HPS）模型用于无序区域建模，并结合Go模型（Go model）描述Histone H1的球状结构域；在NPT系综条件下于2至12 bar压力范围内进行模拟。

Result: 链尺寸参数（回转半径Rg、末端距Ree及其比值R）对压力不敏感；Histone H1因球状核心加无序尾的结构而R值系统性大于ProTalpha。平移扩散系数随压力从约0.22降至0.06 nm²/ns，并伴随与均值相当的链间异质性。链弛豫呈拉伸指数形式，β随压力减小；ProTalpha弛豫时间（约12至40 ns）符合Rouse标度，而Histone H1因内部分子约束而偏离。ProTalpha-Histone H1接触寿命（约0.43至0.56 ns）远短于Rouse弛豫时间。

Conclusion: 该体系处于快速交换区域，瞬态静电接触以重整化链摩擦（renormalize chain friction）的方式起作用而非作为永久交联点，这与各压力下观察到的中等拉伸指数β（约0.55至0.70）相一致，表明瞬态静电相互作用主导了凝聚体的动力学行为。

Abstract: Condensates formed by oppositely charged intrinsically disordered proteins provide model systems for understanding how transient electrostatic interactions govern structure and dynamics in biomolecular assemblies. Here we investigate a nearly charge-neutral condensate composed of 50 Prothymosin alpha (ProTalpha) and 40 Histone H1 molecules using a single-bead-per-residue coarse-grained model combining the HPS hydropathy model for disordered regions with a Go model for the globular domain of Histone H1 under NPT conditions at pressures from 2 to 12 bar. We find that chain dimensions, including the radius of gyration (Rg), end-to-end distance (Ree), and their ratio R, are insensitive to pressure, indicating that chain conformations remain largely unchanged over the pressure range studied. Histone H1 exhibits systematically larger values of R than ProTalpha because of its globular-core plus disordered-tail architecture. Translational diffusion coefficients decrease monotonically with pressure, from approximately 0.22 to 0.06 nm^2/ns, with substantial chain-to-chain heterogeneity comparable to the mean diffusion coefficient. Chain relaxation follows a stretched exponential with beta less than 1 that decreases with pressure. ProTalpha relaxation times of approximately 12 to 40 ns obey Rouse scaling, whereas Histone H1 deviates because of the internal constraint imposed by its globular domain. ProTalpha-Histone H1 contact lifetimes of approximately 0.43 to 0.56 ns are much shorter than the Rouse relaxation time, placing the system firmly in the fast-exchange regime where transient electrostatic contacts renormalize chain friction rather than acting as permanent cross-links, consistent with the moderate stretching exponent beta of approximately 0.55 to 0.70 observed across all pressures.

</details>


### [11] [Non-reciprocal torques guide self-assembly of active particles into clusters with controllable function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29651)
*Till Welker,Yukino Fujiya,Holger Stark*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究展示非互易转向力矩（non-reciprocal turn-towards torques）无需吸引力即可将活性粒子组装成团簇，并建立粒子取向与团簇构型之间的关联。通过对称性决定团簇静止、旋转或平移等功能，并且可以通过调节力矩强度来偏置选择所需功能；引入随机重置（stochastic resetting）策略可显著加速自组装过程，实现可控功能的胶体微机器（colloidal micromachines）的快速构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸引性作用力可以将活性粒子组装成胶体微机器，但无法固定粒子的取向，从而限制了机器功能的控制精确性。作者希望寻找一种不依赖吸引力、同时能建立粒子取向与团簇构型关联的组装机制，实现功能可控的活性粒子机器。

Method: 提出非互易转向力矩这一机制，使粒子在无吸引力条件下自组装成团簇，并将粒子取向与团簇构型耦合起来；利用对称性分析确定团簇的静态、旋转或平移行为；针对实际应用，引入随机重置策略，通过短暂关闭推进和力矩来规避慢路径，加速自组装过程。

Result: 在小系统中，粒子数目唯一决定稳定构型及其功能；在大系统中，存在多种稳定构型对应不同功能，调节力矩强度可在不同功能间进行偏置，例如实现运行-翻滚运动（run-and-tumble motion）。随机重置显著加速了自组装进程。

Conclusion: 非互易力矩能够快速将活性粒子组装成功能可控的胶体微机器，且该组装过程可按需开关，实现"即时"（just-in-time）自组装，为微观机器的设计与制造提供了新途径。

Abstract: Self-assembly of constituents determines structure formation in the microscopic world. Attractive forces can assemble active particles into colloidal machines, but they do not fix the particles' orientations, which limits control over the machine's function. We demonstrate that non-reciprocal turn-towards torques not only assemble active particles into clusters, without requiring attractive forces, but also link particle orientations to the cluster configuration. Symmetry then dictates whether the cluster is static, rotates, or translates. In small systems, the particle number uniquely determines the stable configuration and function. In larger systems, there are multiple stable configurations with distinct functions, and tuning the torque strength allows us to bias towards the desired function, such as a run-and-tumble motion. Because the interactions driving assembly can be switched on and off, the clusters self-assemble when needed. For such a "just-in-time" self-assembly to be practical, fast assembly is necessary. We show that stochastic resetting, implemented by briefly turning off propulsion and torque, significantly speeds up self-assembly by avoiding slow pathways. Together, our findings demonstrate that non-reciprocal torques can rapidly assemble active particles into colloidal micromachines with controllable function.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究一种由拓扑缺陷（topological defects）系统描述的结构玻璃（structural glasses）模型，在平均场近似（mean-field approximation）下考察其平衡行为，发现三种可能的平衡情景，其中仅一种表现出强玻璃形成能力（strong glass-forming ability），并可据此进行模型参数的现象学选择，进而研究淬火（quenching）过程中的动力学。研究还确定了存在一个温度上限，超过该温度后淬火进入玻璃态将不可能实现。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型的核心关注对象是由拓扑缺陷系统描述的结构玻璃，作者旨在探讨这一拓扑缺陷方法的适用范围（scope），并希望揭示其平衡行为特征及其对后续淬火动力学研究的意义。

Method: 在平均场近似框架下分析模型的平衡行为，通过对平衡情景的分类来现象学地选择模型参数，随后利用所选参数研究淬火过程中的动力学行为。

Result: 分析揭示了三种可能的平衡情景，其中仅有一种能够展现出强玻璃形成能力；此外，研究识别出存在一个温度上限，超过该上限后淬火进入玻璃态将变得不可能。

Conclusion: 该拓扑缺陷模型能够根据平衡行为进行参数选择，从而为淬火动力学研究提供可操作的框架；但必须注意温度上限的存在，这界定了玻璃态可通过淬火实现的温度范围。

Abstract: The model primarily concerns structural glasses described by systems of topological defects. We explore the scope of this approach and investigate equilibrium behavior within the mean-field approximation. Our analysis reveals three possible equilibrium scenarios, only one of which exhibits a strong glass-forming ability. These scenarios allow for the phenomenological selection of model parameters based on equilibrium behavior, which can then be used to study dynamics during quenching. We identify an upper temperature limit beyond which quenching into the glassy state becomes impossible.

</details>


### [13] [Curvature-amplified angular localization of radially localized states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29174)
*Hiroyuki Shima*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了常曲率表面上的连续薛定谔粒子在径向随机势和较弱角度依赖微扰作用下的行为。通过精确角动量分解将问题简化为独立的一维随机径向通道，发现径向安德森局域化（Anderson localization）与圆对称概率分布之间存在竞争。在双曲面上，壳破裂半径（shell-breaking radius）以$2a\ln(1/\varepsilon)+O(1)$的形式随扰动振幅对数发散，而在平坦空间中则是代数增长。该物理图像通过转移矩阵、有限环计算和直接极坐标网格模拟得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解曲率对安德森局域化（Anderson localization）的影响，特别是考察在非平坦几何（如双曲面）上无序系统中金属-绝缘体转变（metal–insulator transition）是否发生，以及几何曲率如何放大弱无序的边界效应。

Method: 采用角动量分解（angular-momentum decomposition）将旋转对称问题简化为独立的一维随机径向通道；利用正径向李雅普诺夫指数（Lyapunov exponent）区分径向局域化与圆对称分布；将窄径向模式投影为一维无序环模型，分别通过转移矩阵（transfer-matrix）和有限环（finite-ring）计算以及直接极坐标网格（polar-grid）模拟，在六个独立径向无序实现上无偏追踪简并角动量子空间，验证单模投影的有效性。

Result: 环局域化长度（localization length）随扰动振幅的平方反比增长。壳破裂半径在平坦空间呈代数增长，而在曲率半径为$a$的双曲面（hyperbolic surface）上以$2a\ln(1/\varepsilon)+O(1)$形式对数增长。转移矩阵和有限环计算确定了交叉系数；极坐标直接模拟验证了单模投影近似的有效性。

Conclusion: 弱无序极限与平坦曲率极限不可交换，表明该各向异性无序系综在双曲面上呈现一种曲率放大的交叉行为（curvature-amplified crossover），而非双曲面上普遍存在的金属-绝缘体转变（generic metal–insulator transition）。

Abstract: We study a continuum Schrödinger particle on a surface of constant curvature subject to a radial random potential and a weaker angle-dependent perturbation. Exact angular-momentum decomposition reduces the rotationally symmetric problem to independent one-dimensional random radial channels. A positive radial Lyapunov exponent distinguishes radial Anderson localization from the circular probability profile imposed by symmetry. Projection of a narrow radial mode produces a disordered one-dimensional ring. For angular disorder that is stationary in physical arc length, the ring localization length grows as the inverse square of the perturbation amplitude. Equating this length with the geodesic circumference yields a shell-breaking radius that grows algebraically in flat space but as $2a\ln(1/\varepsilon)+O(1)$ on a hyperbolic surface of curvature radius $a$ and angular-disorder amplitude $\varepsilon$. Transfer-matrix and finite-ring calculations determine the crossover coefficient. Direct polar-grid calculations at the same angular energy as the ring model use unbiased tracking of the degenerate angular-momentum subspace across six independent radial-disorder realizations and quantify the validity of the single-mode projection. The noncommuting weak-disorder and flat-curvature limits establish a curvature-amplified crossover for this deliberately anisotropic disorder ensemble, rather than a generic metal--insulator transition on the hyperbolic plane.

</details>


### [14] [Boundary Kerr Signatures of the Interband-Coherence Hall Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29424)
*Ivan Iorsh,Mikhail Titov*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在弱掺杂闪锌矿半导体中识别出一种由光致带间相干（interband coherence）而非非平衡带占据所携带的霍尔（Hall）响应：纵向直流电场驱使近带隙光产生的导带—价带相干产生横向通量，并通过边界转换产生反对称、边缘局域、螺旋度奇异的克尔信号，该效应无需自旋轨道耦合或带内自旋、谷、轨道积累。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有霍尔效应研究主要基于非平衡带占据或自旋/谷/轨道自由度，而带间相干导致的霍尔响应在真实空间中尚未被直接识别。作者旨在揭示一种由光致带间相干驱动的霍尔效应，并将其发展为探测多带量子动力学（multiband quantum kinetics）的直接手段。

Abstract: We identify a Hall response carried by optically induced interband coherence rather than by a non-equilibrium band population. The response is enhanced by electron--hole asymmetry and by strong interband mixing, making narrow-gap semiconductors especially favorable to observe the effect.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Exponential Hopfield networks store a number of patterns that grows exponentially with the number of neurons, and in their classical formulation they are auto-associative: they complete a corrupted copy of a memory into the memory itself. Many of the tasks one wants such a network to perform are instead hetero-associative, mapping a cue to a different target. We introduce and analyse an exponential neural network of $L$ layers of $N$ binary neurons, each layer carrying its own dataset, whose energy is an exponential of the product of the per-layer Mattis overlaps, so that it is minimised precisely when every layer retrieves the pattern of the same index; the stored association must be a surjective function of the cue, and we show why nothing else can be stored at all. A cavity/signal-to-noise analysis, made exact at leading order by a large-deviation evaluation of the noise, shows that the aligned hetero-associative state is a fixed point of the zero-temperature dynamics up to a number of stored patterns $P_c\sim e^{Nρ_L}$, exponential in the layer size, with an explicit rate $ρ_L$ that grows like $L\log 2$; enlarging the basins of attraction lowers the rate but never destroys its exponential character. Comparing the theory with structured data we find that the exponential capacity and the predicted basins survive correlated, many-to-one patterns: the network is a near-perfect content-addressable memory. The same closed forms describe, without refitting, a synthetic manifold, real T-cell-receptor/epitope triples and natural-language intent data, so the mechanism is domain-universal. Generalisation to unseen cues, though significantly above chance, stays below memorisation, and it is the geometry of the encoding, rather than the data domain, that sets how far above chance it reaches. TL;DR: 该研究展示了无释放（release-free）声子晶体腔（phononic crystal cavity）与高阻抗微波谐振器的强机电耦合，其机电耦合速率 g_em/(2π) ≈ 30 MHz，共振时协同度（cooperativity）高达 C ≈ 180，并在毫开尔文温度下于硅和蓝宝石衬底上实现品质因数（quality factor）超过 10^4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子晶体腔器件通常需要悬挂结构以抑制声子辐射损耗，但悬挂会限制热锚定（thermal anchoring）并导致过量噪声。无释放声子晶体有望解决这一挑战，但此前尚未证明其与强机电相互作用的兼容性，因此亟需验证该体系的可行性。

Method: 采用无释放声子晶体腔，将其与高阻抗微波谐振器构成机电耦合系统，在硅和蓝宝石两种衬底上制备铌酸锂（lithium niobate）声子晶体，并在毫开尔文温度下测量其机电耦合速率、损耗率和品质因数。

Result: 实现机电耦合速率 g_em/(2π) ≈ 30 MHz，超过机械损耗率和微波损耗率，共振时的协同度高达 C ≈ 180；在毫开尔文温度和硅、蓝宝石两种衬底上，声子晶体品质因数均超过 10^4。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了无释放声子晶体作为紧凑、可扩展的微波与吉赫兹（gigahertz）声音之间接口的可行性，可用于新兴的传感、通信和计算系统。

Abstract: Phonons hold promise for storing and transferring quantum information, including in mechanically-mediated quantum interconnects between superconducting qubits and light. Phononic crystal cavities confine gigahertz sound to micron-scale volumes well matched to near-infrared light. So far, these devices have typically been suspended to suppress phononic radiation loss into the substrate, but suspension limits thermal anchoring leading to excess noise. Release-free phononic crystals have emerged as a way to address this challenge -- but had yet to be shown compatible with strong electromechanical interactions. Here, we demonstrate a release-free phononic crystal cavity strongly coupled to a high-impedance microwave resonator, with an electromechanical coupling rate $g_\mathrm{em}/(2π) \approx 30\,\text{MHz}$ that exceeds both the mechanical and microwave loss rates, leading to a cooperativity up to $\mathcal{C} \approx 180$ on resonance. In addition, our lithium niobate phononic crystals reach quality factors above $10^4$ at millikelvin temperature on both silicon and sapphire substrates. Our results establish release-free phononic crystals as compact, scalable interfaces between microwaves and gigahertz sound for emerging sensing, communication, and computing systems.

</details>

### [23] [Learning transferable event representations for charmed baryon physics at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29088)
*Kaixuan Huang,Yangu Li,Junpeng Zhao,Peilian Li,Peirong Li,Xiaorui Lyu,Yunxuan Song,Shengsen Sun,Yangheng Zheng*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于粒子Transformer（Particle Transformer）的框架，用于在BESIII实验中学习魅重子物理的可迁移事件表示，通过大规模预训练加微调策略，在Λ_c^+介子的分类和动量方向回归任务中显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高能物理中，深度学习已成为重要工具，但学习可迁移的事件表示（transferable event representations）以提升模型在不同物理过程中的泛化能力仍具有重要价值。针对BESIII实验中的魅重子物理，需要一种可扩展且高效的表示学习框架。

Method: 构建基于粒子Transformer（Particle Transformer）的框架，通过大规模蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟样本进行预训练，再在下游分析任务中进行微调（fine-tuning）。以Λ_c^+产生与衰变作为基准，分别开发了事件分类和动量方向回归两个预训练模型。

Result: 分类模型在信号效率90.0%时拒绝了97.0%的背景事件。在12个Λ_c^+衰变道基准中，基于预训练模型的微调性能与从头训练相当或更优，尤其在低统计量区域改善明显。回归任务中预训练模型提升了动量方向预测精度，在代表性半轻子衰变Λ_c^+→pK^-e^+ν_e的微调后获得进一步改进。

Conclusion: 该方法为BESIII实验的广泛物理课题提供了可扩展的解决方案，并可推广至其他高能物理实验。

Abstract: Deep learning has become an essential tool in high-energy physics, where the ability to learn transferable event representations can significantly improve model generalization across related physics processes. In this work, we present a Particle Transformer-based framework for learning such representations for charmed baryon physics in the BESIII experiment. The framework is implemented through large-scale pre-training on Monte Carlo simulation samples and subsequent fine-tuning for downstream analyses. Using the production and decays of the charmed baryon $Λ_c^+$ as a benchmark, we develop pre-trained models for both event classification and momentum-direction regression. The classification model learns discriminative event representations for the dominant physics categories, rejecting 97.0\% of background events at a signal efficiency of 90.0\%. Across 12 benchmark $Λ_c^+$ decay channels, fine-tuning from the pre-trained model achieves performance comparable or better than training from scratch, with particularly clear improvements in low-statistics regimes. For the regression task, the pre-trained model improves the momentum-direction prediction across the same benchmark channels. Further improvement is obtained after fine-tuning in the representative semileptonic decay $Λ_c^+ \to p K^- e^+ ν_e$. This strategy provides a scalable solution for a wide range of physics cases at BESIII and can be extended to other high energy experiments.

</details>

Abstract: In recent decades, a multitude of modified gravity theories have been proposed to address a variety of cosmological and astrophysical problems. While many of these theories remain viable, observational constraints on their parameters are increasingly stringent. Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs), in particular, have emerged as powerful probes of cosmology and fundamental physics. This study investigates the implications of a generic modified gravity theory for gravitational lensing by FRBs. By analyzing the dataset of CHIME/FRBs, we constrain the fraction of dark matter composed of primordial black holes within this theoretical framework. Furthermore, we demonstrate that modified gravity introduces a screening effect on gravitational lensing, analogous to the scattering effect of plasma on light rays.

</details>

Method: 提出了首个涵盖从暴涨时期深处到当今宇宙结构的全历史宇宙模拟方法，并将其应用于轴子-U(1)暴涨模型，以追踪非线性相互作用如何在早期宇宙中产生可观测的结构印记。

Conclusion: 该从头模拟方法为将非线性暴涨动力学的信号映射到不同宇宙时期可观测的宇宙结构上，提供了一种新颖的理论框架。

Abstract: The cosmic web preserves a record of the physics that shaped the universe in its earliest moments, the period of exponential expansion known as cosmic inflation. However, if inflation involves significant nonlinear interactions, there are no direct theoretical predictions for the resulting cosmic web. We present the first simulation of the entire history of the universe, from deep in the inflationary epoch to the present-day cosmic structure. Applying this to axion-U(1) inflation, we find that early-universe interactions enhance small-scale structure at high redshift, imprinting the matter power spectrum and the mass function of dark matter halos with signatures of a modified primordial curvature power spectrum and non-Gaussianity. These signatures make high-redshift galaxy surveys, 21-cm observations, and line-intensity mapping promising probes of inflationary physics. More broadly, this ab initio approach provides a novel framework for mapping the signatures of nonlinear inflationary dynamics onto observable cosmic structures across cosmic time.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study oscillon formation and gravitational wave production in a generalized exponential plateau inflationary potential. Using Floquet analysis, we identify parametric instability bands consistent with oscillon formation, and solve numerically for the oscillon profile, finding quasi-breather solutions with lifetimes $τ_{\rm osc} \cdot m_{\rm eff} \sim 10^3$ --- $6\times10^4$. Applying the poltergeist mechanism, we compute the induced gravitational wave spectrum and find a peak at $f_{\rm peak} \approx 2.5\times10^{10}$~Hz with amplitude $Ω_{\rm GW,0}\,h^2 \sim 10^{-9}$--$10^{-8}$ for the benchmark parameter $β_{\rm pot} = 5\times10^{-6}$. This is far below the region forbidden by big bang nucleosynthesis. For $β_{\rm pot} = 5\times10^{-5}$ the signal exceeds the big bang nucleosynthesis bound for all considered oscillon energy fractions, constraining the parameter space of the model. The signal falls in the GHz regime, potentially accessible to future resonant cavity experiments.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个定量框架，将哈勃张力（Hubble tension）视为各向同性FLRW模型对宇宙学数据压缩的一致性检验，在均匀但各向异性膨胀的Bianchi I型背景下进行。作者通过弱剪切、轴对称计算得到了剪切历史与低红移光度距离四极矩（luminosity-distance quadrupole）之间的映射关系，并证明了最小纯剪切模型无法解决哈勃张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈勃张力通常被表述为在单个标量参数H₀的两次测量之间的不一致，其中各向同性FLRW模型被假定为有效的。本文旨在将张力重新框架为标量FLRW压缩的一致性检验，探讨在Bianchi I型各向异性膨胀背景下耦合剪切效应是否可能解释这一偏差。

Method: 作者综合了Bianchi I运动学、零测地线（null geodesics）和光学传播的既有结果，提出了一个原创的弱剪切、轴对称计算，将指定的剪切历史映射为低红移光度距离四极矩。计算明确区分了方向依赖的红移—仿射参数映射与Jacobi聚焦效应，并通过解析极盖型玩具窗口传播所得的距离四极矩。早期宇宙边界约束取自前人的大爆炸核合成（BBN）限制，本文的新颖之处在于通过导出的Sachs–Jacobi映射将其传播为光度距离四极矩和星表窗口偏差的极限。

Result: 对于自由衰减剪切，得到距离四极矩展开式A_D(z) = -B_H0 + (2q₀-1)B_H0z/2 + (5-q₀-18q₀²+6j₀)B_H0z²/12 + O(z³, B_H0²)。代表性的BBN限制Ω_σ0 ≤ 10⁻²³意味着|B_H0| ≤ 9.5×10⁻¹²，在z=0.15处距离模数四极矩低于2.4×10⁻¹¹星等。相比之下，1%的方向性偏移需要Ω_σ0 ≈ 2.5×10⁻⁵，而匹配Planck 2018–SH0ES 2022的分离需要Ω_σ0 ≈ 1.8×10⁻³。

Conclusion: 最小纯剪切模型无法解决哈勃张力，因为要在观测上产生足够的方向性偏移所需的剪切参数远超过BBN所允许的范围。然而，该框架提供了一个可证伪的研究纲领，用于利用超新星（SNe）、重子声学振荡（BAO）和标准汽笛（standard sirens）检验晚期宇宙的各向异性。

Abstract: The Hubble tension is usually formulated as a disagreement between two determinations of a single scalar parameter, $H_0$, within an isotropic FLRW model. We develop a quantitative framework treating the tension as a consistency test of the scalar FLRW compression of cosmological data in a homogeneous, anisotropically expanding Bianchi type I background. Beyond synthesizing established results on Bianchi I kinematics, null geodesics, and optical propagation, our original contribution is a worked weak-shear, axisymmetric calculation mapping a specified shear history into a low-redshift luminosity-distance quadrupole. The calculation explicitly separates the direction-dependent redshift--affine-parameter mapping from the Jacobi-focusing contribution, propagating the resulting distance quadrupole through an analytic polar-cap toy window. For freely decaying shear, we obtain $A_D(z) = -B_{H0} + (2q_0-1)B_{H0}z/2 + (5-q_0-18q_0^2+6j_0)B_{H0}z^2/12 + O(z^3, B_{H0}^2)$, where $B_{H0}=(H_{\parallel 0}-H_{\perp 0})/H_0$ and $j_0$ is the mean jerk parameter. A representative BBN limit, $Ω_{σ0} \le 10^{-23}$, implies $\vert{}B_{H0}\vert{} \le 9.5 \times 10^{-12}$ and a distance-modulus quadrupole below $2.4 \times 10^{-11}$ mag at $z=0.15$. The early-Universe bound used is adopted from prior work; the novelty lies in propagating it through the derived Sachs--Jacobi mapping into limits on the luminosity-distance quadrupole and catalogue-window bias. By contrast, a 1% directional shift requires $Ω_{σ0} \approx 2.5 \times 10^{-5}$, while matching the Planck 2018--SH0ES 2022 separation requires $Ω_{σ0} \approx 1.8 \times 10^{-3}$. Thus, the minimal shear-only model cannot resolve the tension, though the framework supplies a falsifiable programme for testing late-time anisotropy with SNe, BAO, and standard sirens.

</details>

### [28] [A user's guide to PINNs in geometric analysis: lessons from the asymptotic Plateau problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28733)
*Tancredi Schettini Gherardini*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文是arXiv:2605.26234v2的方法论伴侣文章，详细阐述了一种基于物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural networks, PINNs）构建双曲空间中趋近给定无穷远结的近极小圆盘的框架。文章聚焦于决定该方法成败的两个关键方面：将问题几何编码进模型架构以确保边界条件精确成立，以及通过两个实现技巧（前向传播二阶jets、编译残差计算图）将训练步骤成本降低约40-50倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文介绍了基于PINNs的机器学习框架，但未充分展开实现细节。作者在DANGER研讨会报告的经验表明，两个方面的实际工程处理对方法是否奏效至关重要，因此撰写本文旨在为微分几何与几何分析研究者提供可复用的方法论参考。

Method: 本文以方法论讨论为主，不综述结果，而是围绕两个决定方法成败的核心方面展开：其一，将问题几何嵌入模型架构，使边界条件与无穷远渐近性质对于所有可学习参数值都精确成立，从而仅需单分量损失函数；其二，精心设计PDE残差的求值方式，包括用前向传播二阶jets替代嵌套逆向模式自动微分，以及一次性编译残差计算图而非每步重建。

Result: 在相同硬件条件下，上述两种实现改动将每个训练步骤的计算成本降低约40至50倍。该方法提供了关于Joel Fine关于H^4中极小曲面与HOMFLY多项式系数之间关系的猜想的数值证据。

Conclusion: 作者希望这些方法学讨论能为希望在自身问题中部署PINNs的微分几何与几何分析研究者提供实用参考。文章强调几何嵌入与实现效率是PINNs方法能否实际工作的决定性因素。

Abstract: This proceedings contribution elaborates on the findings of arXiv:2605.26234v2: a joint work with Marco Usula, where we introduced a machine learning framework based on physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), aimed at constructing near-minimal discs in hyperbolic space asymptotic to a prescribed knot at infinity. We used this method to provide numerical evidence for a conjecture of Joel Fine relating minimal surfaces in $H^{4}$ to the coefficients of the HOMFLY polynomial. This is a methodological companion to that paper, based on a presentation given at the 2026 edition of the workshop "DANGER: Data, Numbers, and Geometry". Rather than reviewing the results, which are presented extensively in the preprint above, we discuss the two aspects of the framework which, in our experience, determined whether the method worked at all. First, the geometry of the problem must be encoded in the architecture of the model, so that the boundary condition and asymptotics at infinity hold exactly for every value of the learnable parameters - leaving us with a single-component loss function; second, the evaluation of the PDE residual must be engineered with care to ensure that complete trainings can be performed in a reasonable time. On the latter point, we describe two implementation techniques which are not spelled out in detail in the original paper: replacing nested reverse-mode automatic differentiation with the forward propagation of second-order jets, and compiling the computational graph of the residual once instead of rebuilding it at every optimisation step. Together, on identical hardware, these two changes reduce the cost of a training step by a factor of roughly forty to fifty. We hope these methodological discussions can be useful for researchers in differential geometry and geometric analysis who wish to deploy PINNs on problems of their own.

</details>

*Tuan Dolmen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在Dong引入的2-凸性（2-convexity）条件下证明了带电荷的彭罗斯不等式（Penrose inequality with charge）：对满足带电主导能量条件和无散电磁场条件的渐近平坦爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦初始数据集，ADM质量满足m ≥ √(|Σ|/16π) + q²√(π/|Σ|)，等号当且仅当数据集等距于次极值Reissner-Nordström时空的标准切片时成立。

Building on Dong's proof of the uncharged case, we use his $\mathbf{P}$-inverse mean curvature flow and its weak formulation, which only depends on $(g,\mathbf{P})$ and hence applies to the charged setting unchanged. The novelty of our work is the modification of the monotonicity formula to account for the additional charge term. Abstract: Elastic curves are the mathematical shapes of thin elastic rods in equilibrium, with deep connections to mechanics, geometry, and computer graphics. Traditionally described as stationary points of bending energy under length and torsion constraints, their rich theory admits many equivalent characterizations. We develop a new one from the viewpoint of geometric mechanics. Our main contribution relies on a lesser-known isoperimetric characterization: a curve is elastic if and only if it is a critical point of the length functional under fixed area and volume vectors. We show that these constraints transform naturally under orientation-preserving rigid body motions, identifying them as momentum variables for these symmetries. This structure suggests a new discrete theory. We show that the low-order integral quantities length, area, and volume vectors are all naturally defined for polygonal curves, leaving the same transformation laws exactly satisfied. The resulting definition of discrete elastic curves in terms of the isoperimetric characterization restricted to discrete polygonal curves is variational, structure-preserving, and requires no auxiliary discretizations of curvature or material frames. Finally, the same structure carries the Marsden--Weinstein form, a canonical (pre-)symplectic structure on the space of curves, to polygonal curves. This yields novel approaches to Hamiltonian dynamics on discrete space curves, including tangent, vortex-filament, and modified Korteweg--de Vries flows.

Abstract: A general implementation of the equation-of-motion (EOM) formalism for correlated many-body states is presented and applied to the description of collective excitations in atomic nuclei. While EOM approaches are traditionally formulated on top of independent-particle reference states, the present work extends the method to correlated reference states generated by modern many-body solvers. This formulation enables a consistent treatment of ground-state correlations and excited-state dynamics within a unified framework. Particular emphasis is placed on collective nuclear excitations employing chiral nuclear Hamiltonians in an ab initio context. The approach is motivated by the renewed interest in EOM techniques across several fields, including quantum chemistry and quantum computing, where they provide efficient and systematically improvable descriptions of excitation spectra. The present results demonstrate that the EOM framework offers a flexible and powerful tool for the microscopic description of nuclear spectroscopy beyond the traditional mean-field paradigm.

Abstract: We present a detailed investigation of scenarios in which dark-photon dark matter is produced from quantum fluctuations during inflation.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对于完全混沌（fully chaotic）系统，在 ∂f/∂Ω > 0 条件下第二定律可被直接导出（玻尔兹曼等先验概率假设为 ∂f/∂Ω > 0 的特例）；而对于局部非混沌系统，∂f/∂Ω ≤ 0 会导致熵自发下降且无需能量惩罚。

Conclusion: 熵增的统计本质在于 f 与 Ω 的正相关性，而非系统的动力学细节；在非混沌区域，常规热力学框架不再适用，熵可以减少而无需任何能量代价。

Abstract: The second law of thermodynamics still lacks a general proof applicable to both classical and quantum systems across broad ranges of time scales, interactions, and degrees of nonequilibrium. In this paper, we show that when the macrostate-level probability $f$ of an isolated system increases monotonically with the number of possible microstates $Ω$ (i.e., $\partial f/\partial Ω> 0$), the second law emerges naturally from the continuity equation of $f$ in phase space; a special case of $\partial f/\partial Ω> 0$ is Boltzmann's assumption of equal a priori equilibrium probabilities. Based on this finding, the second law can be readily derived for fully chaotic systems. The derivation does not rely on dynamical details, highlighting the statistical nature of entropy increase. In contrast, for a locally nonchaotic system, the positive correlation between $f$ and $Ω$ may break down (i.e., $\partial f/\partial Ω\leq 0$). Consequently, the conventional framework of thermodynamics does not apply, and entropy can decrease spontaneously without any energetic penalty.

</details>

Abstract: The symmetric Dyson exclusion process (SDEP) is an exclusion process on the lattice with a long-range logarithmic Coulomb-type interaction. Abstract: Restricted path integral Monte Carlo (RPIMC) sidesteps the fluctuating Fermion sign problem by confining paths within nodal pockets of a trial density matrix, thereby recovering polynomial scaling. Motivated by the observation of the `purity' of quantum mechanics at high temperature (where kinetic energy dominates), we train the density matrix along a temperature (imaginary time) curriculum, learning increasingly large \emph{corrections} to the initial free-particle reference. Abstract: We propose a geometric--analytic framework for equilibrium statistical mechanics on infinite-dimensional Hamiltonian systems. This construction yields entropy and free-energy functionals on weak symplectic Fréchet manifolds and gives existence and uniqueness of exponential-family equilibrium states under explicit admissibility and separation assumptions. These states are stationary under Hamiltonian flows preserving both the reference mean and the equilibrium weight, and satisfy a classical Poisson--KMS identity when the reference mean is Poisson invariant. Under a local exponential regularity assumption, the logarithmic partition functional is smooth and convex, with Hessian given by the covariance form. It is strictly convex modulo thermodynamically null directions and, through Legendre--Fenchel duality, induces a concave entropy on the domain of extensive variables. Abstract: One of the major challenges in axion dark matter haloscope searches is a loss-less tuning mechanism that is able to cover a significant frequency range around the haloscope's central frequency. In this article, we report on the implementation and performance of an axion haloscope dubbed Vertical-cut Optimised Resonant Tunable cavity for dark matter EXploration (VORTEX) centred at $8.5$ GHz with a tuning range of around $800$ MHz. In addition, we test the cavity-mode structure of this cavity type directly via the `bead-pull method' and observe satisfactory agreement with expectations. Abstract: In this paper, we investigate scattering of a scalar field near a Schwarzschild black hole through its $S$-matrix. Abstract: We propose a holographic map from the semiclassical Hilbert space of pure general relativity in $\text{AdS}_{3}$ to that of $\text{CFT}_{2}$. The lengths and angular momenta of external geodesics become conformal weights of primaries in the Hilbert space of the CFT living on the corresponding component of $\partial Σ$. The fixed-area network determines the wave function, which is given by a network of OPE coefficients of primaries whose weights are determined by the corresponding lengths. Abstract: We present new charged and rotating near-horizon geometries in five-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell theory in closed analytic form. The new solutions have the same entropy relations as expected for charged versions of extremal Myers-Perry black holes and for rotating versions of extremal Reissner-Nordström-Tangherlini black holes, but they do not reduce to the Myers-Perry horizon in the vacuum limit. The horizon cross-sections are spherical and carry a Sasakian structure. Abstract: We study the differential Poisson sigma model (DPSM) in the symplectic case and show that its classical reduction defines a distinguished class of A-type models on symplectic targets, not necessarily Kähler. The DPSM is a covariant first-order sigma model whose graded target is the parity-shifted tangent bundle $T[1]M$ of a Poisson manifold $M$. Its graded Poisson tensor encodes a differential Poisson bracket on $C(T[1]M)\congΩ^\bullet(M)$, written covariantly in terms of a connection $Γ$ and its transpose $\widetildeΓ$. In the nondegenerate case, the Jacobi identities force $Γ$ to be flat, while the quartic coupling of the reduced action is given by the curvature of $\widetildeΓ$, induced by the torsion of $Γ$. Thus, the DPSM selects a symplectic class in which the A-model curvature coupling acquires a first-order Poisson origin. We describe this class through examples and obstructions; $\mathbb{CP}^n$ and K3 surfaces are excluded, while affine symplectic targets, symplectic tori, and the Kodaira--Thurston manifold furnish explicit examples. The graded parent geometry on $T[1]M$ equips $Ω^\bullet(M)$ with a differential Poisson bracket and $Ω^\bullet(M)[1]$ with a strict $L_\infty$-algebra structure, equipping the observable complex with a natural chain-level differential Poisson structure that is not manifest in the usual Kähler formulation of the A-model.

</details>

### [75] [An agent-based model of the formation and evolution of common ground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28915)
*Mengbin Ye,Wooseok Jung,Tony J. Mathew,Lorenzo Zino,Yoshihisa Kashima*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究采用基于智能体的模型（agent-based model）来探索宏观层面公共共同基础（common ground）的形成与演化机制，通过蒙特卡罗模拟揭示了不同交互情境和信息条件下系统可能涌现的多种宏观现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共共同基础对群体中的集体行动和协调至关重要，但驱动宏观层面共同基础形成与演化的微观认知和社会过程尚未得到充分理论化和严格探索。

Method: 采用形式化方法构建基于智能体的模型，模拟网络中智能体之间重复的"确立基础"互动，明确区分信息共享互动中的发送者与接收者，并允许接受/拒绝、接收者回应丢失以及发送者对无回应状态的多种解读等关键特征。

Result: 蒙特卡罗模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）显示，不同交互情境和可用共享信息会产生不同的涌现现象，包括全局公共共同基础、分裂为多个不同共同基础的集群，以及共享共同基础完全丧失。

Conclusion: 该工作凸显了利用数学模型研究文化动力学中微观-宏观（micro-macro）链接的潜力，包括识别促进全局公共共同基础涌现的交互方式。

Abstract: The existence of a communal common ground is vital for collective action and coordination in a population, but the micro-level cognitive and social processes that lead to the formation and evolution of common ground at the macro-level are undertheorised and have not been rigorously explored. In this work, we adopt a formal approach and develop an agent-based model that describes repeated grounding attempts between agents interacting on a network, with an explicit distinction between a sender agent and a receiver agent during an interaction involving sharing information. Several key novel features enable us to capture a range of different interaction contexts: we allow for the interaction to result in either acceptance or rejection, the receiver's response may be lost to the sender, and the sender can interpret this lack of response as either acceptance or rejection (or even something in between). A campaign of Monte Carlo simulations reveals how different interaction contexts, as well as the available information for sharing, result in different emergent phenomena, such as a global communal common ground, fragmentation into multiple clusters of differing common ground, and even the total loss of any shared common ground. This work highlights the potential for using mathematical models to study micro-macro links in cultural dynamics, including identifying ways to facilitate interactions to foster the emergence of a global communal common ground.

</details>


### [76] [How null-model constraints affect statistical validation in projected bipartite networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29242)
*Alessandro Catalano,Rosario N. Mantegna*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统性比较了四种用于投影二分网络（projected bipartite network）显著性验证的零模型（null model），以微正则二分配置模型（microcanonical bipartite configuration model）为基准，发现各零模型之间的差异主要由共现统计量（co-occurrence statistic）的期望和方差决定，而非仅由所保留的约束条件决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对零模型的比较主要关注其产出的有效骨干网（validated backbone），而非模型构建背后的统计假设。本文旨在通过分析各零模型诱导的共现统计量分布，揭示放松约束条件的统计后果，从而为投影二分网络验证提供更合理的模型选择依据。

Method: 采用来自比较基因组学、国际贸易和食品科学三个经验性二分系统，系统比较四种零模型：由Curveball算法生成的微正则配置模型、二分配置模型（Bipartite Configuration Model, BiCM）、二分部分配置模型（Bipartite Partial Configuration Model, BiPCM）以及超几何近似（Hypergeometric approximation）。以微正则二分配置模型获得的统计验证连接为参考基准，评估其余三个模型的性能，并推导BiCM期望的稀疏近似。

Result: 放松零模型约束的统计后果不能仅从约束本身理解，必须通过共现统计量诱导的概率分布进行分析；期望与方差的联合行为在很大程度上解释了各零模型有效骨干网的差异。推导的BiCM期望稀疏近似表明其对超几何预测的首阶修正由非投影层的度异质性（degree heterogeneity）控制。尽管超几何模型忽略了这种异质性，但其在各数据集上均能准确重现微正则系综的共现方差。

Conclusion: 零模型不仅应根据其保留的约束条件进行比较，还应依据这些约束对检验统计量分布诱导的统计后果来评估。超几何近似在方差预测上的表现揭示了一种此前未被认识到的统计等价性，提示模型评估应同时关注期望与方差两个维度。

Abstract: Statistical validation of projected bipartite networks depends critically on the null model adopted to describe random co-occurrences. Although several null models have been proposed, their comparison has mainly focused on the validated backbones they produce rather than on the statistical assumptions underlying their construction. Here we compare four widely used null models - the microcanonical configuration model generated by the Curveball algorithm, the Bipartite Configuration Model (BiCM), the Bipartite Partial Configuration Model (BiPCM), and the Hypergeometric approximation - using three empirical bipartite systems from comparative genomics, international trade and food science. We use the statistically validated links obtained from the microcanonical bipartite configuration model as the reference benchmark for assessing performances of the other three models. Our central result is that the statistical consequences of relaxing null-model constraints cannot be understood solely from the constraints themselves but must be analyzed through the probability distribution induced for the co-occurrence statistic. In particular, the combined behaviour of the expectation and variance largely explains the observed differences among the statistically validated backbones. We further derive a leading-order sparse approximation for the BiCM expectation, showing that the first correction to the Hypergeometric prediction is controlled by the degree heterogeneity of the non-projected layer. Surprisingly, despite neglecting this heterogeneity, the Hypergeometric model accurately reproduces the co-occurrence variance of the microcanonical ensemble across all datasets. Our results suggest that null models should be compared not only according to the constraints they preserve but also according to the statistical consequences that these constraints induce on the distribution of the test statistic.

</details>

### [77] [SH-SAW Acousto-Electric Amplifier in Epitaxial InGaAs on Lithium Niobate on Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28888)
*Chuan Tian,Christopher Heidelberger,Siddhartha Ghosh*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文在InGaAs/绝缘体上铌酸锂（LNOI）异质结构上演示了剪切水平声表面波（SH-SAW）声电（AE）放大。钝化器件在1.11 GHz下实现32 dB/mm的基模非互易增益，未钝化器件在1.1-2.8 GHz范围内达到174 dB/mm的上界增益。Hall测量与解析模型结合量化了载流子密度对增益的决定作用，并识别出InGaAs表面氧化是主要的退化机制，InP或ALD Al2O3钝化层可有效抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频（RF）前端对非互易器件和小型化低功耗解决方案的需求日益增长，特别是在带内全双工（in-band full-duplex, IBFD）收发机等频谱高效应用场景中。现有非互易元件的尺寸、功耗和带宽限制促使研究者探索基于声电相互作用的紧凑型替代方案。

Method: 采用Al2O3介导的晶圆键合工艺，在X切铌酸锂薄膜上外延生长并转移InGaAs层，形成InGaAs/LNOI异质结构。通过Hall效应测量提取转移InGaAs中的载流子密度，将其代入解析模型计算绝对声电增益；同时通过钝化处理（InP或ALD Al2O3）对比研究表面氧化与钝化对器件性能及退化的影响。

Result: 钝化器件在1.11 GHz、30 V偏置和64 mW功耗下实现32 dB/mm的稳定基模非互易性；未钝化器件在1.1-2.8 GHz范围内达到174 dB/mm增益上界。研究发现模式相关的机电耦合系数K^2决定可实现增益，外延期间非有意硅掺杂抬高了载流子密度，解释了绝对增益水平。裸InGaAs表面环境氧化是导致声电响应数周内消失的退化机制，InP或ALD Al2O3钝化可以抑制但会重新分配压电场。

Conclusion: InGaAs-on-LNOI被确立为一种紧凑、低功耗的非互易RF器件和声电延迟线平台，适用于IBFD收发机和频谱高效无线前端。外延工艺控制（特别是载流子密度调控）被识别为进一步提升增益的关键杠杆。

Abstract: This work demonstrates shear-horizontal surface acoustic wave (SH-SAW) acoustoelectric (AE) amplification on an epitaxial InGaAs / X-cut lithium niobate on insulator (LNOI) heterostructure formed by Al2O3-mediated wafer bonding. Deployable passivated devices show a stable fundamental-mode non-reciprocity of 32 dB/mm at 1.11 GHz (30 V bias, 64 mW consumed), while unpassivated devices reach 174 dB/mm across 1.1-2.8 GHz, reported as upper bounds. Device characterization establishes the role of mode-dependent K^2 in determining the achievable gain. Hall-effect measurements of the transferred InGaAs serve as a quantitative diagnostic: the extracted carrier density, elevated by unintentional silicon doping during epitaxy, accounts for the absolute AE gain when inserted into the analytical model and identifies epitaxial process control as a clear lever for further enhancement. We further identify ambient oxidation of the bare InGaAs surface as a distinct aging mechanism that extinguishes the AE response within weeks, and show that an InP or ALD Al2O3 passivation layer suppresses it, at the cost of redistributing the piezoelectric field away from the channel. These results establish InGaAs-on-LNOI as a compact, low-power platform for non-reciprocal RF components and acoustoelectric delay lines, with strong relevance to in-band full-duplex (IBFD) transceivers and spectrum-efficient wireless front ends.

</details>

### [78] [Large deviations for the maximum of the generalized TAP free energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29330)
*Jeanne Boursier*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究Ising混合p自旋模型中广义TAP自由能最大值的指数随机大偏差（large deviations）。通过新的Guerra型插值法得到上界，并采用Huang-Sellke策略证明下界，最终将物理学家提出的超对称（supersymmetric）公式识别为TAP极大值存在的大偏差指数，而非普通的退火复杂度（annealed complexity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自旋玻璃（spin-glass）模型中TAP自由能极大值的大偏差行为，是对Chen-Panchenko-Subag提出的广义TAP自由能的进一步定量刻画，同时也试图为Parisi公式提供构造性证明路径。

Method: 上界利用一种新的Guerra型插值（Guerra-type interpolation）方法；下界遵循Huang和Sellke的策略。关键在于证明超对称临界点在正重叠（positive overlap）条件下在其自身球面上无竞争者，从而构成球面码（spherical code），使退火计数（annealed count）转化为存在概率的下界。

Result: 将超对称公式识别为TAP极大值存在的大偏差指数，而非普通退火复杂度。得到的率函数（rate function）是受限于底部原子质量的约束Parisi值的Legendre变换。在严格稳定性（strict stability）技术假设下，将同一论证在TAP祖先上方的能带中迭代，可给出Ising模型Parisi公式的构造性证明。

Conclusion: 这项工作连接了大偏差理论、TAP自由能分析与Parisi公式，提出了新的插值技术，并展示了超对称临界点结构在自旋玻璃大偏差问题中的核心作用，为进一步的构造性证明提供了可行框架。

Abstract: We study large deviations of the maximum of the generalized TAP free energy introduced by Chen, Panchenko, and Subag for the Ising mixed $p$-spin model. The upper bound uses a new Guerra-type interpolation that may also be useful for other spin-glass models. The lower bound follows the strategy of Huang and Sellke. We show that supersymmetric critical points have no competitor at positive overlap on their own sphere. As such, they form a spherical code, which turns the annealed count into a lower bound on the probability of existence. This identifies the supersymmetric formula proposed by physicists with the large-deviation exponent for the existence of TAP maxima, rather than with the ordinary annealed complexity. The resulting rate function is the Legendre transform of a constrained Parisi value in the bottom-atom mass. The same argument, iterated in bands above TAP ancestors, would give a constructive proof of the Parisi formula for the Ising model under a technical strict stability assumption.

</details>

*Ilya Losev*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzian场论（Schwarzian Field Theory）概率测度的高温极限，证明了大尺度下极限集中于跳跃过程（jump processes），而小尺度行为由实数线上的Schwarzian场论支配，该理论可视为Schwarzian场论的无穷体积版本。除证明局部收敛外，还系统处理了实数线上的Schwarzian理论，包括关联函数的计算及相应的唯一性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Schwarzian场论概率测度在高温极限下的渐近行为，特别是其在不同尺度上的极限结构，并建立与无穷体积版本的严格联系。

Method: 研究Schwarzian场论概率测度的高温极限，分别分析大尺度与小尺度行为；对大尺度极限证明其集中于跳跃过程，对小尺度极限识别为实数线上的Schwarzian场论；通过计算关联函数和建立唯一性定理系统处理该无穷体积版本理论。

Result: 证明了大尺度下概率测度极限集中于跳跃过程；建立小尺度局域收敛于实数线上的Schwarzian场论；计算了该理论的关联函数，并给出了相应的唯一性定理。

Conclusion: 实数线上的Schwarzian场论可作为Schwarzian场论的无穷体积版本，其高温极限在不同尺度下展现出截然不同的行为，该理论结构由关联函数和唯一性定理得到完整刻画。

Abstract: In this work we study the high-temperature limit of the Schwarzian Field Theory probability measure. We show that on large scales this limit concentrates on jump processes, while its small-scale behaviour is governed by a process which we call Schwarzian Field Theory on the real line. This Schwarzian Theory on the real line can be viewed as the infinite-volume version of the Schwarzian Field Theory. In addition to showing this local convergence, we also provide a systematic treatment of the Schwarzian Theory on the real line. This includes the calculation of the correlation functions and a corresponding uniqueness theorem.

</details>

### [80] [Why a mid-depth stress-free boundary condition is incorrect for Ekman flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29261)
*Christian Puntini,Luigi Roberti*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文证明在风驱动海洋流动分析中，假设在有限中间深度（埃克曼层底部）处速度导数为零（自由滑移边界）必然导致非物理的流速剖面；同时通过WKB方法论证，深海底部采用无滑移条件时，埃克曼输运与风应力仍近似正交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在风驱动海洋流动（wind-driven ocean flows）的经典分析中，研究者常在有限中间深度处（即埃克曼层底部）假设应力自由边界条件。本文旨在检验该假设的物理合理性，并厘清不同底部边界条件对埃克曼输运与风应力关系的影响。

Method: 首先通过严格的数学推导，分析流体速度的z-导数在给定深度为零时对应的流速剖面几何特征；随后基于海洋深层水密度存在小变化的前提，采用WKB近似（WKB ansatz）方法分析深海底部的无滑移条件对埃克曼输运与风应力正交性的影响。

Result: 证明若流体速度的z-导数在某一深度消失，则该深度必然是流速剖面的极小值点，且流速在其下方随深度增大而增大，这与物理预期不符；进一步表明，只要海底足够深，无滑移条件在极小误差范围内仍等效于埃克曼输运（Ekman transport）与风应力（wind-stress）正交。

Conclusion: 在有限中间深度处施加应力自由边界条件会导致非物理的流速剖面，应予以避免；而足够深处的无滑移条件对埃克曼输运与风应力正交性的偏离可以忽略不计，因此合理的底部边界条件选择不影响经典埃克曼理论的成立。

Abstract: We show that the assumption of a stress-free boundary condition at a finite intermediate depth, namely, at the bottom of the Ekman layer, in the analysis of wind-driven ocean flows necessarily leads to an unphysical current profile. Indeed, if the $z$-derivative of the fluid velocity vanishes at a given depth, then this depth necessarily corresponds to a minimum of the velocity profile, with the velocity increasing beneath it. Using a WKB ansatz based on the small variations of the ocean's water density at great depths, we also argue that a no-slip condition at the bottom of the ocean, if sufficiently deep, still effectively implies (up to a very small error) the orthogonality of the Ekman transport and the wind-stress.

</details>

*Satoshi Takada,Shintaro Hokada*

Main category: physics.class-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为准确理解和预测弹性空心球体在单轴加载下的力学行为，需建立可靠的分析框架，而现有研究对该系统应力分布的动力学来源及局部集中机制尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 采用弹性动力学框架，通过对位移的标量势和矢量势进行展开，并运用拉普拉斯变换将时间域问题转化为可求解的形式，进而在长时极限下提取静态解。

Result: 在长时极限下获得了位移和应力分布的静态解析解；内表面上某些物理量在压缩力与表面点的连线角度垂直处出现峰值，表明该位置存在显著的局部应力集中。

Conclusion: 该工作为单轴加载条件下弹性空心球体的行为分析提供了一种稳健的解析方法，对材料科学与结构工程领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: The stress analysis of a three-dimensional elastic hollow sphere subjected to uniaxial compression is revisited, employing an elastodynamic framework. Through the application of the Laplace transform, the scalar and vector potentials of displacement are expanded, facilitating a detailed exploration of the system's mechanical behavior. The static solutions for displacement and stress distributions are derived in the long-time limit, which reveal key insights into the response of the elastic hollow sphere. Notably, on the inner surface, certain quantities exhibit a peak at the point where the angle between the compressive force and the point on the surface becomes perpendicular, indicating localized stress concentration. These findings provide a robust analytical approach for understanding and predicting the behavior of elastic hollow spheres under uniaxial loading, with implications for material science and structural engineering.

</details>

### [82] [Nontrivial solutions for a class of semilinear elliptic equations with a nonlinear Goldstein-Wentzell boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28709)
*Enzo Vitillaro*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个涉及体内拉普拉斯算子（Laplacian）和边界上拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米算子（Laplace-Beltrami operator）的双重椭圆问题（doubly elliptic problem），在适当的假设下证明了该问题在势阱能级（potential well energy level）处存在非平凡解，并在更高能级处存在无穷多个解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重椭圆问题结合了区域内部和边界上的非线性偏微分方程结构，其解的存在性和多重性是偏微分方程（partial differential equations）领域的重要课题，而目前对这类问题的系统性结果仍然有限，尤其是关于最小能级解的存在性尚待解决。

Method: 作者利用Sobolev嵌入（Sobolev embeddings）下的次临界（subcritical）条件，结合变分方法（variational methods）和势阱理论（potential well theory），在合适的假设下构造非平凡解，并通过能量层级分析证明解的无穷多重性。

Result: 证明了在给定条件下，该双重椭圆问题在势阱能级（即非平凡解的最小能量水平）处存在非平凡解；同时证明了在更高能级处问题存在无穷多个解。

Conclusion: 该工作为双重椭圆问题的解的存在性和多重性提供了新的理论保证，明确了非平凡解存在的最小能量阈值，并揭示了高能级解的结构丰富性。

Abstract: The paper deals with the existence and multiplicity of nontrivial solutions for the doubly elliptic problem $$\begin{cases} -Δu=f(u) \qquad &\text{in $Ω$,}\\ \phantom{-}u=0 &\text{on $Γ_0$,}\\ -Δ_Γu +\partial_νu =g(u)\qquad &\text{on $Γ_1$,} \end{cases} $$ where $Ω$ is a bounded open domain of $\mathbb{R}^N$ ($N\ge 2$) with $C^1$ boundary $Γ=\partialΩ$, with $Γ=Γ_0\cupΓ_1$, $Γ_0\capΓ_1=\emptyset$, $Γ_1$ being nonempty and relatively open on $Γ$, $\mathcal{H}^{N-1}(Γ_0)>0$. The terms $f$ and $g$ are subcritical with respect to Sobolev embeddings, respectively in $Ω$ and on $\partialΩ$.
  We prove that, under suitable assumptions, the problem admits nontrivial solutions at the depth of the potential well energy level, which is the minimum energy level for nontrivial solutions. We also prove that the problem has infinitely many solutions at higher energy levels.

</details>

*Ralph Saxton,Katarzyna Saxton*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对形如 $U_t + (\Phi(U)U)_x = 0$ 的守恒律系统施加结构条件，建立了依据 $\phi(U)$ 形式对解的类别进行分类的框架，并据此将黎曼问题的解分为经典解与涉及 delta-激波和/或真空态的奇异解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 $n\geq 2$ 的高维守恒律系统，需要通过施加结构条件来系统分类解的演化行为，特别是区分经典解与奇异解（如 delta-激波和真空态）的类别。

Method: 通过规定特定特征值的演化过程，将满足该演化的函数类 $\phi$ 按其依赖的场分为两类：依赖标量场 $z=rK(\Theta)$（其中 $K: S^{n-1}\rightarrow R$）或依赖向量场 $\Theta$，并据此分析解的振幅是否可能在有限时间内爆裂（blow up）。

Result: 据此，相应黎曼问题（Riemann problem）的解可分为经典解与涉及 delta-激波（delta-shocks）和/或真空态（vacuum states）的奇异解两类，同时识别出哪些函数形式会导致解的振幅在有限时间内爆裂。

Conclusion: 通过结构条件建立了一个分类框架，能够依据 $\phi$ 的函数形式系统地区分经典解与奇异解（delta-激波和真空态），为守恒律系统在该类结构下的应用提供了分类基础。

Abstract: Several applications for systems of conservation laws of the form $U_t + (Φ(U) U)_x =0$, $U: R_t\times R_x\rightarrow R^n$ , $n\geq 2$, with $Φ(U) = φ(r, Θ): R^n\rightarrow R$, $r = |U|$, and $Θ= U/|U|\in S^{n-1}$, are obtained by imposing structural conditions to provide a classification framework for solutions dependent on the form of $φ(U)$. By prescribing the evolution of a particular eigenvalue, we can categorize classes of such functions, $φ$, for which this evolution is met, into depending on either a scalar field $z=rK(Θ)$, where $K: S^{n-1}\rightarrow R$, or on the vector field $Θ$, and find for which the amplitude of solutions may blow up in finite time. As a consequence, solutions to the corresponding Riemann problems can be divided into those with are classical and those which involve delta-shocks and/or vacuum states.

</details>


### [84] [Recovery of a Null Form in the Wave Equation from Scattering Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28917)
*Joel Nathe*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用高振荡几何光学解（highly oscillatory geometric optics solutions）研究一类半线性波动方程系统的反问题。在线性区域中，h^3阶系数由可单射的光线变换确定；在弱非线性区域中，h阶系数与非阿贝尔光线变换相关，在系数不依赖时间变量时可由单射的非阿贝尔X射线变换确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对包含对称与反对称零形式（null forms）耦合的半线性波动方程系统，作者旨在通过反散射方法重建系统内耦合系数，并在线性与弱非线性两种范围内考察可识别性。

Method: 采用高振荡几何光学解来构造解的渐近展开，分别在线性区域和弱非线性区域进行分析；通过提取展开系数的高阶项（h^3阶与h阶），将其与光线变换（light-ray transform）及非阿贝尔X射线变换（non-abelian X-ray transform）建立联系。

Result: 线性区域中，h^3阶系数确定了一个依赖于系数q_{rkl}与p_{rijkl}的向量场的光线变换，且该变换是单射的；弱非线性区域中，h阶系数确定了与非阿贝尔矩阵相关的光线变换，并仅在系数不依赖时间变量x_0时证明了单射性，此时对应为非阿贝尔X射线变换。

Conclusion: 本文给出了该耦合波动系统在线性与弱非线性框架下的反问题求解方案，并明确了不同区域下系数可唯一识别的条件。

Abstract: We use highly oscillatory geometric optics solutions to solve the inverse problem for the system \begin{equation} \square \begin{bmatrix} u^{(1)}\\ u^{(2)}\\ \vdots\\ u^{(n)}\end{bmatrix} = \sum_{\substack{k,l=1\\k\geq l}}^n \left(Q_0(u^{(k)},u^{(l)}) \begin{bmatrix} q_{1kl}\\q_{2kl}\\\vdots\\q_{nkl} \end{bmatrix} + \sum_{\substack{i,j=1\ı>j}}^d Q_{ij}(u^{(k)},u^{(l)})\begin{bmatrix} p_{1ijkl} \\ p_{2ijkl} \\ \vdots \\ p_{nijkl} \end{bmatrix}\right), \end{equation} where $Q_0$ and $Q_{ij}$ are the symmetric and anti-symmetric null forms. We present solutions in both the linear and weakly nonlinear regimes. In the linear regime, we show that the coefficients of order $h^3$ determine an injective light-ray transform of a vector field which depends on the coefficients $q_{rkl}, p_{rijkl}$. In the weakly nonlinear regime, we see that the coefficients of order $h$ determine the non-abelian light ray transform for matrices associated with the coefficients. While we do not have an injectivity result for this case, we do have one if we assume the coefficients do not depend on the time variable $x_0$, as our coefficients instead determine an injective non-abelian X-ray transform.

</details>

### [85] [Generalized parton distributions from lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29574)
*Krzysztof Cichy,Martha Constantinou*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本章综述了从格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）提取广义部分子分布（generalized parton distributions, GPDs）的最新进展，回顾了Ji准分布（quasi-distributions）与Radyushkin伪分布（pseudo-distributions）两种主流格点方法及其结果，并展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义部分子分布（GPDs）是描述核子内部三维结构的重要物理量，但无法直接从实验或解析方法中获得，需要借助格点QCD的数值计算来提取。该章节旨在系统梳理近年来格点QCD提取GPDs的理论框架与计算进展。

Method: 本文为综述性章节，首先简要回顾GPDs的理论原理，随后解释两种最常用的格点方法——Ji提出的准分布方法（quasi-distributions）与Radyushkin提出的伪分布方法（pseudo-distributions），并在此基础上汇总两种框架下已获得的格点计算结果。

Result: 章节整理了来自上述两种格点QCD框架的GPDs提取结果，展示了两类方法各自获得的数值成果及其在描述核子结构方面的应用表现。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了未来格点QCD提取GPDs的发展前景与潜在研究方向，指出该领域仍有进一步拓展的空间。

Abstract: This chapter gives an overview of the recent progress in extracting generalized parton distributions from lattice QCD. We briefly recall the theoretical principles of GPDs and explain the two most common lattice approaches, Ji's quasi-distributions and Radyushkin's pseudo-distributions. In the second part of the chapter, we review the lattice results obtained from these two frameworks. Finally, we offer a discussion of future prospects of lattice extractions of GPDs.

</details>
