### [86] [Anomalous Boundary Modes in a Floquet Hyperbolic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28719)
*Ali Fahimniya,Hossein Dehghani,Alicia J. Kollár,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在负曲率的双曲晶格上构造了一种反常Floquet拓扑相，通过周期驱动的四色边跃迁序列和交错势，在零/π准能隙中实现了手性边界模，并提出一种基于穿孔周期晶格的小边界谱流诊断方法以规避有限斑块外边界带来的歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将反常Floquet拓扑相推广到负曲率空间（双曲晶格）是该领域尚未充分探索的方向。同时，传统有限双曲斑块拥有巨大的外边界，给谱流诊断带来实质困难，亟需更可靠的判定工具。

Method: 模型采用紧束缚哈密顿量（tight-binding Hamiltonian），包含周期性重复的四色边跃迁序列和亚晶格交错站位势。利用紧凑周期晶格映射体态0和π准能隙，通过实空间动力学诊断手性边界模，并引入基于穿孔（punctured）周期双曲晶格的小边界谱流（spectral-flow）诊断方法。

Result: 在拓扑区，有限开放斑块的体态准能隙在0和π处被隙内态填充；在平庸区这些隙保持为空。体态0和π准能隙的映射识别出与平庸和反常开放边界谱相连的带隙区域，隙内态被识别为手性边界模。

Conclusion: 穿孔周期双曲晶格的小边界谱流诊断有效避免了有限双曲斑块大外边界带来的模糊性，该方法同样适用于其他拓扑双曲系统的研究。

Abstract: We construct an anomalous Floquet topological phase on a negatively curved hyperbolic lattice. The model is a tight-binding Hamiltonian with a periodically repeated four-color edge-hopping sequence and a sublattice-staggered onsite potential step. The topological regime is reached near the limit in which a single hopping step transfers amplitude completely across an active edge, while the trivial regime is reached near the point where two full hops occur along an active edge during a single hopping step, returning the amplitude to its starting site. In finite open patches, the topological regime is characterized by bulk quasienergy gaps at $0$ and $π$ that are populated by in-gap states, in contrast to a trivial regime where these gaps remain empty. Using compact periodic lattices, we map the bulk $0$ and $π$ quasienergy gaps and identify the gapped regions connected to the trivial and anomalous open-boundary spectra. We diagnose the in-gap states as chiral boundary modes by their real-space dynamics. Finally, we introduce a small-boundary spectral-flow diagnostic based on punctured periodic hyperbolic lattices, which avoids the ambiguity associated with the extensive outer boundary of finite hyperbolic patches. This puncture-based diagnostic should be useful for studying other topological hyperbolic systems.

</details>


### [87] [Enhancement of exciton radius near a band-gap closing through quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28731)
*Jin-Hyung Choi,Sang-Hoon Han,Young-Kwon Han,Sun-Woo Kim,Jun-Won Rhim,Joshua J. P. Thompson*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发现，在拓扑能带闭合附近，布洛赫态的量子几何（quantum geometry）会显著增强，导致电子-空穴库仑矩阵元被抑制，从而削弱激子（exciton）束缚能并扩大其在实空间的半径，最终通过弱场抗磁响应（diamagnetic response）增强这一效应得以实验观测。该机制为激子工程提供了除传统半经典参数调控之外的量子几何新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统激子工程主要聚焦于修饰半经典材料性质（如有效质量和介电屏蔽），而忽视了底层电子与空穴布洛赫态的量子几何效应。作者指出，在拓扑能带闭合附近，这种近似失效——量子度量（quantum metric）在带极值处强烈增强，使得不同动量处布洛赫态相似度降低，进而影响激子束缚。为此，需要揭示量子几何如何重塑激子性质。

Method: 作者在自旋-轨道耦合的Lieb晶格模型（spin-orbit-coupled Lieb-lattice model）中，将体系调谐至拓扑相变（topological phase transition）附近，通过计算投影后的电子-空穴库仑矩阵元，分析其如何受量子度量增强的影响；进而从动量空间激子波函数的收窄推导实空间激子半径的扩大，并计算对应的弱场抗磁响应。

Result: 研究结果表明，拓扑相变附近量子度量的增强显著抑制了投影库仑矩阵元，使激子波函数在动量空间中收窄，从而在实空间中导致激子半径增大；这种半径的增大产生实验上可观测的弱场抗磁响应增强。

Conclusion: 量子几何能够在拓扑相变附近从根本上重塑激子性质，揭示了此前未被充分探索的激子态工程新途径。

Abstract: Exciton engineering traditionally focuses on modifying semiclassical material properties, such as the effective mass and dielectric screening, while largely overlooking the quantum geometry of the underlying electron and hole Bloch states. This approximation is adequate for many materials but breaks down near a topological band-gap closing, where the quantum metric around the band extrema becomes strongly enhanced. In this regime, Bloch states at different momenta become less similar, reducing the projected electron--hole Coulomb matrix elements and consequently weakening exciton binding. We demonstrate this mechanism in a spin--orbit-coupled Lieb-lattice model tuned toward a topological phase transition. The suppressed Coulomb matrix elements narrow the exciton wavefunction in momentum space, leading to an enlarged exciton radius in real space. This increase in exciton size produces an experimentally accessible enhancement of the weak-field diamagnetic response. Our results show that quantum geometry can fundamentally reshape exciton properties near a topological phase transition, revealing a previously underexplored route for engineering excitonic states.

</details>


### [88] [Intrinsic Orbital Hall Effect in Degenerate Spin-3/2 Systems driven by the quantum metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28744)
*Rhonald Burgos Atencia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转不变的自旋-3/2系统中轨道霍尔效应的起源，证明其源于轨道磁矩的带间矩阵元，并由量子度规（quantum metric）而非贝里曲率（Berry curvature）控制，揭示了一种纯几何的输运机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在旋转不变的自旋-3/2系统中，轨道霍尔效应（orbital Hall effect）的微观起源尚不清楚，需要明确其几何输运的驱动因素。

Method: 通过分析旋转不变自旋-3/2系统中的轨道霍尔响应，分解带内与带间贡献，考察轨道磁矩矩阵元与几何量（量子度规与贝里曲率）之间的关系。

Result: 发现轨道霍尔效应源于轨道磁矩的带间矩阵元，霍尔响应由量子度规而非贝里曲率主导；在简并系统中，与轨道磁矩和贝里曲率相关的传统带内贡献恒为零。

Conclusion: 量子度规是控制简并多带系统中轨道霍尔输运的关键几何量，揭示了与贝里曲率不同的纯几何输运机制。

Abstract: We show that in rotationally invariant spin-$3/2$ systems the orbital Hall effect originates from interband matrix elements of the orbital magnetic moment. The resulting Hall response is governed by the quantum metric, rather than by the Berry curvature, revealing a purely geometric transport mechanism. Conventional intraband contributions associated with the orbital magnetic moment and Berry curvature are shown to vanish identically in degenerate systems. These results identify the quantum metric as the key geometric quantity controlling orbital Hall transport in degenerate multiband systems.

</details>


### [89] [Symmetry-assisted computation of the magnetic field at the site of a point dipole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28805)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用对称性论证作为核心分析工具，强调数学严谨性与直觉可理解性的兼顾，从而推导磁偶极子处磁场的性质，并延伸至二维物理中的相关基础问题。

Result: 获得了超越标准文献所述范围的对称性结果，这些结果与二维物理中的基础问题直接相关。

Conclusion: 对称性论证能够为高年级本科生课程提供严格且直观的磁场概念分析方法，同时其扩展结果可能在二维物理的基础问题中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we revisit this concept of the magnetic field at the site of a magnetic dipole using symmetry arguments that are mathematically rigorous yet intuitive enough to be well-suited for upper-level undergraduate courses in electrodynamics and quantum mechanics. Furthermore, we present symmetry-based results that go beyond those discussed in the standard literature, which are directly relevant to fundamental problems in two-dimensional physics.

</details>


### [90] [Engineering micro-disorder for macro-performance in magnetic nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28812)
*Jonathan Leliaert,Elizabeth M Jefremovas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文是一篇Perspective综述，提出将磁性纳米颗粒中固有的自旋无序（spin disorder）从“有害缺陷”重新定义为可工程化的设计参数，系统回顾了无序工程（disorder engineering）这一新兴概念，指出从宏自旋近似（macrospin approximation）向微磁模拟（micromagnetic modelling）转变的理论必要性，并强调建立可证伪、可量化、结构-织构-功能三者关系的研究框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上自旋无序被视为磁性纳米颗粒的有害特征，材料工程努力追求“完美颗粒”，但近年涌现出将颗粒内无序视为潜在功能来源的替代框架。作者希望通过系统综述无序工程的现状、挑战与未来方向，推动该领域从定性探索走向定量建模与实验验证。

Method: 方法层面，作者对比了宏自旋近似的局限性——严重限制颗粒内自由度的描述，与微磁模拟的优势——将磁化视为连续矢量场，能够显式表征颗粒内自由度、连接微结构特征与内部磁化织构，并与极化小角中子散射（polarized small-angle neutron scattering，PSANS）建立直接对应关系以获得纳米尺度系综平均磁关联的实验数据。

Result: 作者识别出该领域当前的核心挑战：需要推进可证伪的、具有不确定性意识的模型，其参数应具备结构物理动机，预测须能通过独立实验可观测量加以检验。

Conclusion: 领域总目标是在颗粒结构、颗粒内磁化织构与宏观功能之间建立定量关系，从而将自旋无序从难以捉摸的隐变量转化为可工程化的设计参数。

Abstract: Spin disorder, inherent to magnetic nanoparticles, has traditionally been regarded as a detrimental feature, with materials-engineering efforts largely focused on producing ''perfect particles'' containing as few defects as possible. Alongside this pursuit of perfection, however, an alternative framework has emerged in recent years that reframes intra-particle disorder as an ''ugly duckling'' whose functional potential remains to be unlocked. In this Perspective, we review the emerging concept of disorder engineering in magnetic nanoparticles, identify its current challenges, and outline promising future directions. From a theoretical standpoint, progress requires moving beyond the widely used macrospin approximation, which severely restricts the description of intra-particle degrees of freedom. Micromagnetic modelling, in contrast, treats magnetisation as a continuous vector field and thereby enables (i) the explicit representation of intra-particle degrees of freedom, linking microstructural features to internal magnetisation textures, and (ii) direct correspondence with polarized small-angle neutron scattering, an experimental technique that provides quantitative access to ensemble-averaged magnetic correlations on nanometre length scales. The field must now advance towards falsifiable and uncertainty-aware models with structurally motivated parameters and predictions that can be tested against independent experimental observables. The overarching goal is to establish quantitative relationships between particle structure, intra-particle magnetisation textures, and macroscopic functionality, thereby transforming spin disorder from an elusive hidden variable into an engineerable design parameter.

</details>


### [91] [Symmetry selection rule for the band-edge shift current in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28927)
*Felipe Pérez Riffo,Matías Castro,Leonor Chico,L. E. F. Foa Torres. Eric Suárez Morell*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多层石墨烯和多层材料中带边位移电流（band-edge shift current）在栅压或位移场下符号反转的对称性起源。作者发现这与量子度量（quantum metric）和贝里曲率（Berry curvature）无关，而是源于低能涌现旋转对称性的破缺，并提出了一个精确的角选择定则（angular selection rule）将多层石墨烯和kagome晶格的带边响应统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期计算表明，多层石墨烯的带边位移电流会在栅压或位移场下发生符号反转，但这一行为既无法用量子度量解释，也无法用贝里曲率捕获，需要寻找其背后的深层物理机制。

Method: 作者提出一个对称性论证：在无反演对称的二维有能隙类狄拉克系统中，带边处涌现的低能旋转对称性会禁止位移电流响应；三阶翘曲（trigonal warping）将该涌现对称性降至晶格的三个旋转对称，从而以线性强度开启电流。该机制被形式化为精确的角选择定则，并以带符号的、失谐加权三点Bargmann不变量刻画多带结构中的电流行为。

Result: 该角选择定则统一了多层石墨烯与kagome晶格的带边响应；光振幅保持不变，而Bargmann三角形的符号对齐随翘曲强度增强，表现为一个有界相干因子（bounded coherence factor）所捕获的相位相干效应，这一效应在任何正定优值（包括量子度量）中都不可见。在双层和三层石墨烯中，仅凭栅压即可驱动符号反转。

Conclusion: 带边位移电流的符号反转源于对称性原理而非量子几何量，揭示了一种新型的相位相干机制。双层和三层石墨烯中的带边位移电流可作为一种无参数、栅压可切换的体光伏（bulk photovoltaic）响应，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: The shift current is the intrinsic bulk photovoltaic response of a crystal without an inversion center. In graphene multilayers, recent calculations report large band-edge shift currents that reverse sign under a gate or displacement field, a behavior that neither the quantum metric nor the Berry curvature captures. We show that this behavior follows from a symmetry principle: at the absorption edge of an inversion-broken, two-dimensional gapped Dirac-like system, an emergent low-energy rotational symmetry forbids the response, and the trigonal warping, which reduces this emergent symmetry to the lattice's three-fold rotation, switches the current on linearly in its strength. We formulate this as an exact angular selection rule that unifies the band-edge responses of multilayer graphene and of the kagome lattice. In the multiband structures, the released current is governed by a signed, detuning-weighted three-point Bargmann invariant of the Bloch states: the optical amplitude remains fixed while the sign alignment of the Bargmann triangles grows with the warping, a phase-coherence effect captured by a bounded coherence factor and invisible to any positive-definite figure of merit, the quantum metric included. In bilayer and trilayer graphene, a gate voltage alone drives the sign reversal, making the band-edge shift current a parameter-free, gate-switchable bulk photovoltaic response.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索石墨烯莫尔体系中具有不同有效维度（effective dimensionality）的电子态（局域态与一维传播模式）如何共存并形成混合网络，为构建新型混合电子网络提供理论基础。

Method: 首先在最小模型（minimal model）中证明空间变化的标量势可以限制局域态，而交错势（staggered potential）的符号变化在畴壁（domain walls）上产生一维模式。随后通过扭转单层-菱面体N层石墨烯（twisted monolayer-rhombohedral N-layer graphene）实现微观层面的机制验证，计算能带结构、能量等高线和布洛赫波函数。

Result: 在最小模型中，局域态与一维模式可在同一有限能量窗口中共存并形成混合网络；在真实参数区间的扭转单层-菱面体石墨烯体系中，能带结构等计算结果揭示了局域近平带态与传播准一维模式的共存。

Conclusion: 扭转单层-菱面体石墨烯是构建具有不同有效维度共存的混合电子网络（hybrid electronic networks）的有前景平台，为相关实验与应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We investigate the coexistence of localized states and propagating one-dimensional (1D) modes in graphene moiré systems. We first show within a minimal model that a spatially varying scalar potential can confine localized states, while sign changes of a staggered potential generate 1D modes along the resulting domain walls. These two types of states can coexist within the same finite energy window and form a hybrid network. We then demonstrate a microscopic realization of this mechanism in twisted monolayer-rhombohedral N-layer graphene. Band structures, energy contours, and Bloch wave functions obtained in a realistic parameter regime reveal the coexistence of localized nearly flat-band states and propagating quasi-1D modes. Our results establish twisted monolayer-rhombohedral graphene as a promising platform for realizing hybrid electronic networks with coexisting states of distinct effective dimensionalities.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯谐振器兼具独特的机械与电学性质，是下一代传感应用的有力候选材料，但对其非线性动态响应的灵活调控仍缺乏通用手段。

Method: 利用光热加热（photothermal heating）以及机械振荡与由悬挂于反射衬底上方的石墨烯膜构成的光腔之间的光机械相互作用，对谐振器的线性恢复力与非线性动态响应进行动态控制。

Result: 通过调节光探针功率与腔深，可连续调控Duffing非线性（Duffing nonlinearity）的强度与符号，实现硬化与软化行为的转变以及几何非线性的近完全抑制。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了按需工程化石墨烯谐振器非线性动力学的多功能平台，为二维纳机电系统（nanoelectromechanical systems）机械响应的主动控制提供了通用策略。

Abstract: Graphene resonators exhibit unique mechanical and electrical properties, making them promising candidates for next-generation sensing applications. Here, we introduce an optomechanical approach that enables active, reversible, in situ tuning of the nonlinear nanomechanical response of graphene resonators. By exploiting photothermal heating and the resulting optomechanical interaction between the mechanical oscillations and the optical cavity formed by a graphene membrane suspended over a reflective substrate, we achieve dynamic control over both the linear restoring force and the nonlinear dynamic response of the resonator. We show that the strength and sign of the Duffing nonlinearity can be continuously tuned through the optical probe power and cavity depth, enabling transitions between hardening and softening behavior as well as near-complete suppression of geometric nonlinearities. Our results establish a versatile platform for on-demand engineering of nonlinear dynamics in graphene resonators and provide a general strategy for active control of the mechanical response of two-dimensional nanoelectromechanical systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实验系统难以在同一平台上同时进行原子尺度表面表征与宏观电输运测量，限制了二维器件中局域结构特征与整体输运行为之间因果关系的直接建立。作者旨在构建一套兼具两种互补技术的联合平台，以弥合这一实验鸿沟。

Result: 该系统在石墨烯（graphene）器件上完成性能演示；利用体相Pb（bulk Pb）确定能量分辨率。采用超导针尖进行扫描隧道谱（scanning tunneling spectroscopy）测量时，超导能隙的能量分辨率达30微伏（μV）；输运测量可追踪原位制备（in-situ prepared）的Pb纳米线在超导转变过程中的电阻率（resistivity）与临界电流（critical current）随温度的变化。

Conclusion: 该一体化系统实现了原子尺度局部操控与器件尺度性能实时监测的结合，为二维材料器件中局域结构与宏观行为（macroscopic behavior）的关联研究提供了新平台。

Abstract: We have developed a low temperature (LT), ultra-high-vacuum (UHV) system that combines two complementary techniques, scanning probe microscopy (SPM) and electrical transport measurements, within a single platform. By providing simultaneous access to the atomic-scale surface landscape and the macroscopic device response of the same sample, the setup enables direct correlations between local structural/spectroscopic signatures and global electronic transport behavior in two-dimensional (2D) devices. The system allows experiments where atomic-scale modifications or controlled manipulations are performed while continuously monitoring their impact on device-scale performance. The setup consists of two interconnected UHV chambers: a dedicated preparation chamber and a separate measurement chamber that houses a liquid-helium cryostat and the SPM/transport stage. Base pressure is 1x10^-11 Torr. A key feature is direct optical access to the sample, enabling rapid and reliable tip positioning with an accuracy of 5 microns x 5 microns within 10 minutes. The SPM, operated using custom-built electronics, can track the exact same sample region across a temperature range from 2.9 K to 400 K, with mechanical stability below 1 pm. System performance is demonstrated on graphene devices, and bulk Pb is used to determine energy resolution. Using superconducting tips, scanning tunneling spectroscopy measures the superconducting gap with an energy resolution of 30 microV. Transport measurements track the temperature dependence of both resistivity and critical current across the superconducting transition of an in-situ prepared Pb nanowire.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子泊肃叶流动已在石墨烯中被实验观测到，但这一宏观流动本身的线性稳定性问题尚未被系统研究。作者希望从理论上澄清在何种实验条件下这种流动会发生不稳定性，从而为实验探测该现象提供指导。

Method: 采用修正的奥尔-索末菲方程对二维材料中平面泊肃叶电子流进行线性稳定性分析，将临界电流作为实验参数的函数进行求解，并扫描参数空间以寻找需要最低电流密度即可失稳的最有利情形。

Result: 给出了使流动失稳所需的临界电流随实验参数的依赖关系，识别出最有利于观测不稳定性的参数区间，并预测了失稳发生时的流向波数（streamwise wavenumber）与频率。

Conclusion: 电子泊肃叶流可以在实验中达到失稳条件，但探测该不稳定性存在实验上的挑战；论文据此讨论了设计相关实验的难点与建议。

Abstract: Motivated by the experimental observation of electronic Poiseuille flow in graphene [J.A. Sulpizio et al. Nature 576, 75 (2019)] we analyze the linear stability of plane-Poiseuille flow of electrons in a two-dimensional material using a modified Orr-Sommerfeld equation. We calculate the critical current needed to make the flow unstable as a function of the experimental parameters and characterize the most favorable situation, that is the one needing the lowest current density, to observe flow instability. We predict the streamwise wavenumber and frequency at which the instability occurs and discuss the difficulties of an experiment aimed at probing this phenomenon.

</details>


### [96] [Origins of microwave losses in superconducting circuits made with silicon-on-insulator substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29154)
*Simon Messelot,Nicolas Aparicio,Kazi Rafsanjani Amin,Eric Eyraud,Bruno Fain,Mikaël Cassé,Guillaume Jourdan,Fabrice Nemouchi,Sébastien Hentz,Frédéric Gustavo,François Lefloch,Nicolas Roch,Jérémie J. Viennot,Julien Renard*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过超导微波谐振器实验表明，在低温下，标准电阻率的绝缘体上硅（silicon-on-insulator, SOI）衬底比高电阻率SOI衬底性能更优。高电阻率衬底中体硅与氧化硅界面处的寄生片层导电是主要损耗机制，可通过引入陷阱的衬底加以抑制，此时损耗最终受限于氧化硅层的介电损耗。此类衬底为超导纳米机电系统（nanoelectromechanical systems）的发展提供了新前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝缘体上硅技术广泛用于制造硅基器件，将低损耗超导量子电路与SOI衬底集成可将成熟的硅工艺优势与超导电路的高灵敏度相结合。然而，研究界传统上认为高电阻率硅的低微波损耗特性必然优于标准电阻率硅，本文旨在挑战这一直觉假设并深入探究低温下的实际损耗机制。

Method: 本文采用超导微波谐振器（superconducting microwave resonators）作为表征手段，对比标准电阻率与高电阻率SOI衬底在低温下的微波损耗性能，并通过在衬底中引入陷阱（intentionally induced traps）来探究寄生片层导电损耗的抑制效果及其对整体损耗上限的影响。

Result: 实验发现标准电阻率SOI衬底在低温下反而优于高电阻率SOI衬底。高电阻率衬底的损耗主要由体硅与氧化硅界面处的寄生片层导电主导；引入陷阱后该寄生片层可被有效抑制，此时损耗的最终限制因素变为氧化硅层的介电损耗。

Conclusion: 具有陷阱的SOI衬底为超导纳米机电系统提供了重要优势：其释放（release）氧化硅的步骤可仅限于可动部件，从而保持器件其余部分的机械完整性；同时该结构可增强向衬底体部的热传导（heat evacuation），解决当前如微波-光转换器（microwave-to-optics converters）等器件中的散热难题。

Abstract: Silicon-on-insulator technology is widely used to fabricate silicon based devices, from advanced transistors to photonic circuits or nanomechanical systems. Integrating low loss superconducting quantum circuits with silicon-on-insulator substrates enables to couple the advantages offered by the mature silicon technology to the exquisite sensitivity of superconducting circuits. The natural approach, inherited from research in superconducting microwave devices, is to use a substrate made with highly resistive silicon, known for its low level of microwave losses. In this work, using superconducting microwave resonators, we show that counterintuitively, standard resistivity silicon-on-insulator substrates perform better than high resistivity silicon-on-insulator substrates at cryogenic temperatures. In the latter case, the presence of a parasitic sheet conduction at the interface between bulk silicon and silicon oxide acts as the dominant loss mechanism. This parasitic sheet can be suppressed using substrates with intentionally induced traps. In such substrates, losses are ultimately limited by the dielectric losses of the silicon oxide layer. These substrates offer interesting perspectives for the development of superconducting nanoelectromechanical systems. First, the release, i.e. the removal of the silicon oxide, could be limited to the moving parts, thereby maintaining the mechanical integrity of the rest of the device. Additionally, such structure would enhance heat evacuation into the bulk of the substrate which is an issue in current devices such as microwave-to-optics converters.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强光-物质耦合（strong light-matter coupling）对电子输运的影响，并将腔量子电动力学（cavity quantum electrodynamics）与半导体电输运研究相结合，寻找实验中可观测的腔诱导输运效应。

Method: 采用非平衡格林函数形式（nonequilibrium Green's function formalism），对掺杂量子阱与双金属腔中光子模式相互作用的系统进行建模，推导出适用于任意集体耦合强度（超过相关损耗率）的电流紧凑表达式。

Result: 当载流子注入速率小于腔诱导电子展宽时，腔极化激元（cavity polaritons）在电流-电压特性中留下可观测的输运特征，表现为与共振和反共振极化激元发射相关的非弹性边带（inelastic sidebands），并在共振光照下显著增强。

Conclusion: 该研究为在半导体异质结构中实验探测腔诱导的电荷输运修正提供了一条现实可行的路径，展示了强耦合腔系统作为输运测量探针的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate electronic transport through a doped quantum well strongly interacting with a photonic mode confined in a double-metal cavity. Using a nonequilibrium Green's function formalism, we derive compact expressions for the current valid for arbitrary collective light--matter coupling strengths exceeding the relevant loss rates. We show that cavity polaritons leave observable transport signatures when the carrier injection rate is smaller than the cavity-induced electronic broadening. In this regime, the current--voltage characteristics exhibit inelastic sidebands associated with resonant and anti-resonant polariton emission, which are strongly enhanced under resonant illumination. Our results provide a realistic route to detecting cavity-induced modifications of charge transport in semiconductor heterostructures.

</details>


### [98] [Emergence of Propagating Exciton-Polaritons in Hybrid Waveguide-van der Waals Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29289)
*Alina Schubert,Karoline Becker,Andreas Thies,Rico Schwartz,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Henije Stolz,Alexander Szameit,Matthias Heinrich,Tobias Korn*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种将过渡金属二硫属化物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）单层集成到飞秒激光写入的熔融石英表面波导中的混合光子平台，并通过低温微光致发光（micro-photoluminescence, μPL）光谱观察到了波导模式与激子强耦合下传播型激子-极化激元（exciton-polariton）的形成，表现为A激子的能量红移或劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将少数层材料集成到光子电路中是有望实现新型片上光子应用的关键方案，而如何在波导结构中研究激子与光子模式的耦合行为是重要的科学问题。

Method: 将TMD材料与飞秒激光写入的熔融石英芯片内嵌表面波导相结合，搭建低温光学光谱装置，实现从器件顶部聚焦激发TMD层的同时通过波导耦合收集微光致发光信号。实验中改变六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）封装层厚度，并采用转移矩阵法（transfer matrix method）对简化平板波导模型进行计算以支持实验解释。

Result: 实验观察到封装后的TMD单层A激子在波导传播的μPL信号中出现光谱变化：随hBN封装厚度不同，A激子表现为能量红移或劈裂为两个分量，与波导模式和激子的强耦合导致传播型激子-极化激元形成的预测一致。

Conclusion: 该实验平台被证明是研究传播型极化激元的高度适应性框架，有望用于在集成光子电路中利用激子-极化激元的独特性质。

Abstract: Integrating few-layer materials into photonic circuits is a promising concept for novel on-chip photonic applications. We incorporate transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) with femtosecond-laser-written surface waveguides, which are embedded in fused silica chips. Our novel low-temperature optical spectroscopy setup enables coupling to the waveguide and simultaneous focus from the top to the TMD layer for a distinct excitation and collection of micro-photoluminescence ($μ$PL) signals in several measurement geometries. Along these lines, we observe spectral changes of the A exciton for encapsulated TMD monolayers when capturing the $μ$PL signal propagating through the waveguide. Depending on the thickness of the encapsulation with hexagonal boron nitride (hBN), these changes manifest as energetic redshifts of the A exciton, or even a splitting of the A exciton into two components. We attribute this behavior to strong coupling of the waveguide mode and the exciton in the sample, giving rise to the formation of propagating exciton-polaritons. Our interpretation is supported by calculations for a simplified model of a slab waveguide in the vicinity of an exciton by using the transfer matrix method. Having proven to be a highly adaptable framework for the study of propagating polaritons, our experimental platform likewise holds great promise for harnessing the unique properties of exciton-polaristons in integrated photonic circuits.

</details>


### [99] [Diode Effect in Nonlinear High-Kinetic-Inductance Transmission Line Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29301)
*Niklas Gaiser,Ciprian Padurariu,Björn Kubala,Nadav Katz,Joachim Ankerhold*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文针对高动态电感（HKI）传输线中的强非线性微波器件，提出了一种保留电磁场全空间依赖性的边值问题（boundary value problem）理论框架，取代传统慢变包络近似（slowly varying envelope approximation）方法。该框架成功预测了功率相关的共振频移、双稳态传输解以及高达93%的方向依赖传输对比度，并扩展至三端口短截线几何结构，揭示了非线性干涉导致的反对共振频移和Duffing型谱畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高动态电感传输线是紧凑型非线性超导微波器件的重要平台，但现有理论基于慢变包络近似，该近似在具有显著空间场变化的强非线性共振结构中失效，因此需要一种能够保留全空间场依赖性的更精确理论框架。

Method: 作者将单频非线性波传播问题重新表述为边值问题，完整保留电磁场的空间依赖性。该方法应用于共振传输线几何结构和三端口短截线（stub）几何结构，通过解析和数值手段分析非线性波传播特性。

Result: 该方法揭示了功率依赖的共振频移、双稳态传输解，以及由不对称阻抗势垒引起的强方向依赖传输，在不施加磁场偏置的情况下实现了高达93%的传输对比度。在三端口短截线几何中，非线性干涉产生了移动的反对共振（anti-resonance）和Duffing型谱畸变。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为强非线性超导微波器件的分析与设计提供了通用工具，能够捕捉传统近似方法无法描述的空间场变化效应和非线性干涉行为。

Abstract: High-kinetic-inductance (HKI) transmission lines provide a promising platform for compact nonlinear superconducting microwave devices. Existing theoretical descriptions are typically based on the slowly varying envelope approximation, which becomes inadequate for strongly nonlinear resonant structures with pronounced spatial field variations. Here, we develop a theoretical framework for single-frequency nonlinear wave propagation in HKI transmission lines by formulating the problem as a boundary value problem that retains the full spatial dependence of the electromagnetic fields. Applying the method to resonant transmission-line geometries, we demonstrate power-dependent resonance shifts, bistable transmission solutions, and strongly direction-dependent transport arising from asymmetric impedance barriers, yielding transmission contrasts of up to $93\%$ without magnetic bias fields. The framework is further extended to a three-port stub geometry, where nonlinear interference produces shifted anti-resonances and Duffing-like spectral distortions. Our approach provides a versatile tool for the analysis and design of strongly nonlinear superconducting microwave devices.

</details>


### [100] [Controlling Electron-Beam-Induced Charging in Colloidal Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29387)
*Sven Ebel,Alina Myslovska,Stefano Vezzoli,Iwan Moreels,Sergii Morozov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究通过原位阴极发光（cathodoluminescence, CL）与光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）测量，揭示了胶体量子点（colloidal quantum dots, QDs）在电子束激发下的CL响应受电子束诱导充电（electron-beam-induced charging）控制，并证明了通过缩短绝缘配体长度可改善电荷排出、抑制双激子（biexciton）形成，从而提供了一条实现稳定CL的实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体量子点是极具吸引力的纳米发光体，但其阴极发光响应机理尚不清楚，且在电子束激发下往往不稳定，限制了CL光谱学和基于电子束的器件加工的发展。

Method: 采用高度光稳定、结构均一的巨壳层CdSe/CdS量子点，结合原位CL和PL测量；通过寿命和光谱测量识别激发态的不同特征，并采用间接激发达到亚皮安（sub-pA）电流范围，同时通过更换更短的配体改善电荷排出。

Result: CL响应由电子束诱导充电主导；即使在较低电流下，电荷积累也会驱动多激子（multiexciton）生成，导致显著蓝移、平均寿命缩短和快速阴极漂白（cathodobleaching）。间接激发可部分恢复中性激子发射并减轻阴极漂白，但无法完全抑制充电；缩短绝缘配体可改善电荷排水并强烈抑制双激子形成。

Conclusion: 双激子形成可通过限制电荷积累来加以控制，这为光谱学、成像和兼容电子束的光子器件提供了实现稳定CL的实用途径。

Abstract: Colloidal quantum dots (QDs) are attractive nanoscale emitters, yet their cathodoluminescence (CL) response remains poorly understood and often unstable under electron-beam excitation, limiting CL spectroscopy and electron-beam-based device processing. Here, we investigate the CL mechanism and strategies to improve its stability using highly photostable, structurally homogeneous giant-shell CdSe/CdS QDs combined with in situ CL and photoluminescence (PL) measurements. By identifying distinct signatures of excited states in both lifetime and spectral measurements, we demonstrate that the CL response is governed by electron-beam-induced charging. Charge accumulation drives multiexciton generation even at relatively low currents, leading to a pronounced blueshift, shorter average lifetimes, and rapid cathodobleaching. To test this further, we employ indirect excitation to reach sub-pA currents beyond the limits of typical electron beams, showing that neutral-exciton emission can be partially recovered and cathodobleaching mitigated, although charging cannot be fully suppressed. Furthermore, by replacing long insulating ligands with shorter ones, we improve charge drainage and strongly suppress biexciton formation. Together, these results show that biexciton formation can be controlled by limiting charge accumulation, providing a practical route toward stable CL for spectroscopy, imaging, and electron-beam-compatible photonic devices.

</details>


### [101] [Delayed Formation of Landau Polaritons in Phase-Resolved THz Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29501)
*Noureddine Charrouj,Yurii Ivonyak,Dmitriy Yavorskiy,Vladimir Y. Umansky,Jerzy Lusakowski,Wojciech Knap,Marcin Bialek*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过相位分辨太赫兹时域光谱（phase-resolved terahertz time-domain spectroscopy）直接观测了朗道极化激元（Landau polaritons）的拉比振荡（Rabi oscillations），发现拉比振荡并非在激发回旋共振后立即开始，而是在延迟一个腔往返时间（cavity round-trip time）后才建立，揭示了强耦合（strong coupling）态的动态形成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强光-物质耦合通过电磁腔场与材料激发之间的相干周期性能量交换产生极化激元，该相互作用通常通过光谱模式劈裂（spectral mode splitting）间接推断，但其动力学过程（dynamics）此前 largely unexplored，本文旨在直接观察并理解其时间演化特性。

Method: 采用相位分辨太赫兹时域光谱，将GaAs/Al₀.₃₆Ga₀.₆₄As二维电子气中的回旋共振（cyclotron resonance）与法布里-珀罗腔（Fabry-Perot cavity）模式耦合形成朗道极化激元；结合交叉极化光谱（cross-polarized spectroscopy）与磁场差分（magnetic-field differential）技术，解析回旋共振振荡的时间拍频（temporal beating）。

Result: 实验观察到朗道极化激元的拉比振荡，并发现其并非在回旋共振激发后立即启动，而是延迟约一个腔往返时间后才出现，表明强耦合态仅在腔模式场形成之后才建立。

Conclusion: 该结果直接揭示了太赫兹（THz）波段混合光-物质态的形成动力学，证明强耦合机制的建立依赖于腔模式场的先行形成，为理解极化激元动态行为提供了直接实验证据。

Abstract: Strong light-matter coupling gives rise to polaritons through coherent and periodic energy exchange between electromagnetic cavity fields and material excitations. While this interaction is typically inferred from spectral mode splitting, its dynamics remain largely unexplored. Here, using phase-resolved terahertz time-domain spectroscopy, we observe Rabi oscillations of Landau polaritons formed by coupling the cyclotron resonance in a GaAs/Al$_{0.36}$Ga$_{0.64}$As two-dimensional electron gas with Fabry-Perot cavity modes. By employing cross-polarized spectroscopy and magnetic-field differential, we resolve the temporal beating of the cyclotron resonance oscillations. Remarkably, we find that the Rabi oscillations do not start immediately after excitation of the cyclotron resonance, but after a delay corresponding to one cavity round-trip time. This demonstrates that the strong-coupling regime sets up only after the formation of the cavity mode field. Our results provide direct insight into the dynamics of hybrid light-matter states in the THz regime.

</details>


### [102] [Temperature-driven transition between momentum-resolved and disordered averaged Coulomb drag in 1D systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29630)
*Mingyang Zheng,Rebika Makaju,Rasul Gazizulin,Alex Levchenko,Sadhvikas J. Addamane,Dominique Laroche*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过一维库仑拖曳（1D Coulomb drag）测量，对垂直耦合的GaAs-AlGaAs量子线（quantum wires）实现了Luttinger液体相互作用参数的自洽实验测定，并与理论预测定量吻合。研究揭示了磁场下子带的逐级退布居（magnetic depopulation）及无序抑制效应，并识别出两个不同温度区域，界定了动量分辨与无序平均化库仑拖曳的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维系统中电子-电子相互作用的理解仍是一维物理领域的核心挑战，尤其是对库仑耦合的Tomonaga-Luttinger液体。一维系统参数难以可靠提取，加之无序效应，阻碍了1D库仑拖曳实验的合理解释。

Method: 利用垂直耦合的GaAs-AlGaAs量子线，通过磁退布居（magnetic depopulation）完整表征一维参数，并进行1D库仑拖曳测量，实现自洽的Luttinger液体相对相互作用参数的实验测定。

Result: 库仑拖曳随磁场呈现系统性演化，反映一维子带的逐级退布居与无序效应的抑制。识别出两个不同温度区域，并观测到标度行为（scaling）、峰展宽（peak broadening）及非线性电流-电压特性。

Conclusion: 实验结果与理论预测达到定量一致，为探测一维系统中的电子-电子相互作用建立了统一且定量的框架。

Abstract: Advancing the understanding of electron-electron interactions in one-dimensional systems remains one of the central challenges in low-dimensional physics, especially for Coulomb-coupled Tomonaga-Luttinger liquids. Notably, the difficulty of reliably extracting one-dimensional system parameters, combined with the presence of disorder, has hindered the interpretation of 1D Coulomb drag experiments. Here, we present a self-consistent experimental determination of the relative Luttinger liquid interaction parameters through 1D Coulomb drag measurements, and achieve quantitative agreement with theoretical predictions. Utilizing vertically coupled GaAs-AlGaAs quantum wires, we fully characterize the one-dimensional parameters through magnetic depopulation. Coulomb drag exhibits a systematic evolution with magnetic field, reflecting the successive depopulation of 1D subbands and the suppression of disorder effects. Two distinct temperature regimes are identified, marking the boundary between momentum-resolved and disordered-averaged Coulomb drag. The observed scaling, peak broadening, and nonlinear current-voltage characteristics establish a unified and quantitative framework for probing electron-electron interactions in 1D systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Representations from Pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials as Coarse Coordinates for Material Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28776)
*Paul Hagemann,Katharina Ueltzen,Simon Müller,Janine George,Philipp Benner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原子平均特征（atom-averaged features）的材料生成模型评估与引导框架。作者利用预训练机器学习原子间势能（Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials, MLIPs）如MACE的特征，提出了粗细输运距离（Coarse-Fine Transport Distance, CFTD），在统一的基于分布的评估框架中同时衡量材料生成输出的质量与新颖性，并展示了该特征可用于引导生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无机晶体结构生成模型及其评估方法大多依赖简单的晶体结构表示形式，难以全面捕捉材料的结构质量与新颖性，需要一个更强大且统一的评估框架。

Method: 首先从预训练的MLIPs（如MACE）中提取原子平均特征作为晶体结构的表示。在此基础上，提出CFTD作为距离度量，采用两种不同的特征化器（featurizers）：质量分量基于粗粒度MACE特征，从而在单一基于分布的评估框架中同时度量生成输出的质量与新颖性。此外，将粗粒度MACE特征用作材料生成模型的引导信号。

Result: CFTD能够有效捕捉晶体结构质量，同时检测模型的记忆化（memorization）现象；与近期提出的连续SUN指标进行了比较，展示了其通用性与优势。

Conclusion: 预训练MLIPs的粗粒度特征在材料生成模型的评估和引导两方面均具有显著潜力，为无机晶体结构生成任务提供了更强大且统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative machine learning is increasingly used for inorganic crystal structure generation. Most models and the corresponding evaluation approaches rely on simple forms of crystal structure representation. In this paper, we showcase the power of atom-averaged features from pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials (MLIPs), such as MACE, for such tasks. We first introduce a distance measure that assesses the output of material generative models by capturing both quality and novelty in a single distribution-based evaluation framework. In particular, we introduce the Coarse-Fine Transport Distance (CFTD) using two different featurizers, where the quality component is based on coarse MACE features. We showcase CFTD's versatility in capturing crystal-structure quality while also detecting memorization, and compare it with the recently introduced continuous SUN metrics. We further show that coarse MACE features can be used as guidance for a material generative model.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Gapped Parent Hamiltonians for the Strongly Deformed Toric Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28740)
*Nandagopal Manoj,Zack Weinstein,Jason Alicea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文严格构造了强变形拓扑序（topological order）态——特别是强变形toric code态——的局域gapped父哈密顿量。这些哈密顿量不是严格有限程的，而是包含系数按指数衰减的Wilson环算子求和。在该局域性（locality）类别下，这些态实现平凡的gapped相，表明Wilson环的perimeter律标度并不必然意味着1-form对称性的自发破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强变形的拓扑序波函数（如toric code）表现出m任意子凝聚和Wilson环perimeter律标度等看似矛盾的性质，这些性质通常不会在gapped基态中共存。作者旨在澄清这些“反常”态在何种局域性假设下属于何种gapped相，并检验现有gapped相定义的边界。

Method: 作者严格构造了这些强变形toric code态的局域gapped父哈密顿量。构造的关键在于允许哈密顿量包含直径按指数衰减系数的Wilson环算子求和，而非严格有限程相互作用；这种局域性放宽符合标准gapped相定义的locality bound。

Result: 在该局域性类别下，这些态实现平凡的gapped相；Wilson环的perimeter律标度不暗示1-form对称性自发破缺；长程铁磁序与perimeter律失序参数关联可共存于2D gapped基态。通过放宽近期no-go定理[Sahay et al., arXiv:2503.01977]的假设（作者量化其在热力学极限下是温和的），回避了该定理的约束。

Conclusion: 结果突显了禁止gapped相内这些反直觉性质需要更强局域性概念（stronger notions of locality）。现有基于指数衰减相互作用的gapped相定义过于宽松，无法排除perimeter律标度等反常行为的共存。

Abstract: Local non-unitary deformations of topologically ordered wavefunctions can drive transitions into peculiar states that challenge modern perspectives on gapped quantum matter. The strongly deformed toric code offers a curious case, hosting $m$ anyon condensation alongside perimeter-law scaling of Wilson loops charged under an exact 1-form symmetry---properties that typically do not coexist in gapped ground states. Nevertheless, we rigorously construct local gapped parent Hamiltonians for these strongly deformed toric code states. The Hamiltonians we construct are not strictly finite-range, but contain sums of Wilson loop operators whose coefficients decay exponentially in their diameter. If one adopts standard locality bounds used to define gapped phases---which allow for such exponentially decaying terms---our construction shows that these states realize a trivial gapped phase. Within this locality class, we demonstrate that perimeter-law scaling of Wilson loops does not imply a spontaneously broken 1-form symmetry, and from a dual perspective, that long-range ferromagnetic order and perimeter-law disorder parameter correlations can coexist in a 2D gapped ground state. We evade a recent no-go theorem [Sahay et al., arXiv:2503.01977] by relaxing its assumptions in a manner that we quantify as benign in the thermodynamic limit. More broadly, our results highlight that stronger notions of locality are necessary for prohibiting these counterintuitive properties within a gapped phase.

</details>


### [105] [Generalized Holstein-Primakoff transformation with optimizable bosonic truncation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28983)
*Yang Liu,Jia-Wei Mei,Rong Yu,Z. Y. Xie,Hong-Gang Luo,Jize Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种广义Holstein-Primakoff变换，通过单一参数在传统Holstein-Primakoff与Dyson-Maleev变换之间连续插值，可自然推广至SU(N)代数，为优化玻色子截断提供灵活框架。在方形晶格自旋-1双线性-双二次模型上的应用表明，结合连续相似变换后，激发谱与张量网络计算结果高度一致，显著降低了截断误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自旋波理论中，自旋算符通常通过Holstein-Primakoff或Dyson-Maleev变换玻色子化，但实际计算中玻色子化哈密顿量必须截断，导致激发谱依赖于玻色子表示的选择。作者希望消除这种表示依赖性，提供一种可优化的统一框架。

Method: 引入一个广义Holstein-Primakoff变换，通过单一参数连续插值于传统Holstein-Primakoff变换与Dyson-Maleev变换之间，并自然推广到SU(N)代数。该方法为超越谐振阶的玻色子截断优化提供灵活框架；应用中将广义变换与连续相似变换（continuous similarity transformations）结合。

Result: 以方形晶格上的自旋-1双线性-双二次模型为应用对象，获得的激发谱与张量网络计算（tensor-network calculations）结果高度吻合。结果表明，优化玻色子表示能大幅降低截断误差，并对准粒子色散（quasiparticle dispersions）与多玻色子连续谱（multi-boson continua）提供定量可靠描述。

Conclusion: 优化玻色子表示能显著减小截断误差，为量子自旋系统的准粒子激发和多体连续谱提供定量可靠的描述框架。

Abstract: Spin-wave theory provides a quasiparticle description of excitation spectra in quantum spin systems. In this theory, the spin operators are usually bosonized by the Holstein-Primakoff transformation or the Dyson-Maleev transformation. In practical calculations, the bosonized Hamiltonian has to be truncated, and thus the resulting excitation spectra depend on the choice of bosonic representation. Here, we introduce a generalized Holstein-Primakoff transformation that continuously interpolates between the conventional Holstein-Primakoff and Dyson-Maleev transformations through a single parameter. The formulation naturally extends to the SU(N) algebra and provides a flexible framework for optimizing bosonic truncations beyond harmonic order. As an application, we investigate the spin-1 bilinear-biquadratic model on the square lattice. By combining the generalized Holstein-Primakoff transformation with continuous similarity transformations, we obtain excitation spectra in excellent agreement with tensor-network calculations. Our results demonstrate that optimizing the bosonic representation substantially reduces truncation errors and provides quantitatively reliable descriptions of both quasiparticle dispersions and multi-boson continua.

</details>


### [106] [Unwithered Majorana fermions in the bulk of a quantum chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29615)
*Gennady Y. Chitov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出在系统参数被调谐至无序线（disorder lines, DL）上的解纠缠基态（disentangled ground state）区域来探测马约拉纳模（Majorana modes）。在横场中的 XY 自旋链（即马约拉纳表示下的 Kitaev 链）上给出了精确解：单马约拉纳模局域在链端附近，而解纠缠的多粒子马约拉纳模（n≥2）可无衰减地穿透体态，这种体-边效应可在特制光学链和晶格中被观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注马约拉纳模在非无序线上的行为，本文希望探索当系统被调谐至无序线形成的解纠缠基态时，马约拉纳模的性质是否发生变化，并揭示新的体-边对应关系。

Method: 以横场中的 XY 自旋链为模型（在马约拉纳表示下等价于 Kitaev 链），将系统参数调谐至无序线上以形成解纠缠基态，从而对相关函数与马约拉纳模的性质进行精确求解。

Result: 在解纠缠态中，某些关联函数不依赖于分离距离。单马约拉纳模仍局域于链端附近（与无序线外的态相同），而解纠缠的 n 粒子马约拉纳模（n≥2）则无衰减地穿透进入体态内部。

Conclusion: 解纠缠基态下的马约拉纳模展现出显著的体-边效应（bulk-edge effects），这些效应可通过特殊设计的光学链和光学晶格实验加以探测。

Abstract: The proposal is to probe Majorana modes in the system when its parameters are tuned to bring it into a disentangled ground state, which occur on the parametric curves known as disorder lines (DL). In such state certain correlation functions do not depend on separation. The exact results are presented for the XY spin chain in transverse field, also called the Kitaev chain in the Majorana representation. The single Majorana modes are shown to be localized near the ends of the chain, as in the states off the DL, while the disentangled $n$-particle Majorana modes ($n \geq 2$) penetrate into the bulk without attenuation. The predicted bulk-edge effects can be detected in the specially engineered optical chains and lattices.

</details>


### [107] [Critical Phases of the extended isotropic $XY$ chain with four-spin interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29006)
*Nika Kurdadze,Giorgi Gogabedashvili,G. I. Japaridze*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过约旦-维格纳变换（Jordan-Wigner transformation）精确求解了含四自旋相互作用的自旋S=1/2各向同性XX链的基态和低温热力学性质，发现其等价于具有近邻和次次次近邻跳迁的格点费米气体。系统在J*_c = 4J/3处发生利夫希茨型（Lifshitz type）拓扑相变，伴随费米点三倍化，并在交变磁场下展现出丰富的基态相图，包含全极化铁磁相、有能隙反铁磁（AFM）相和自旋液体相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究含多体自旋相互作用（beyond nearest-neighbor interactions）的自旋链模型，探索四自旋耦合对系统相图和临界行为的影响，特别是拓扑相变和量子相变（quantum phase transition, QPT）的新特征。

Method: 采用约旦-维格纳变换将自旋S=1/2 XX链映射为无自旋费米子（spinless fermion, SF）表示，精确计算等效格点费米气体模型的基态和低温热力学性质。通过分析费米点结构、色散关系、态密度（density of states）及热力学变量在临界点的标度行为来刻画相变。

Result: 当四自旋耦合增强至J*_c = 4J/3时，系统经历利夫希茨型拓扑相变，费米点数三倍化。相变点处自由费米子色散关系在费米点处平坦化，导致态密度奇异ρ(ω)∼(ω/J)^(-2/3)，比热C∼(T/J*)^(1/3)及磁化率的奇异行为χ(H)∼(H/J*)^(-2/3)。在交变磁场情形下，相图包含全极化铁磁相、有能隙反铁磁相和自旋液体相；从有能隙AFM相到无能隙极化自旋液体相的转变点，磁化强度快速增长m∼(H-Hc)^(1/6)，磁化率呈现χ(H)∼(H-Hc)^(-5/6)的奇异行为。

Conclusion: 四自旋耦合能在标准XX链的基础上引发新的利夫希茨型拓扑量子相变，从不自旋液体相转变到另一类具有不同幂律衰减特征的自旋液体相，并在交变磁场下产生丰富的相图结构及临界标度行为。

Abstract: Using the Jordan-Wigner transformation we calculate exactly the ground state and low-temperature thermodynamic properties of the spin $S=1/2$ isotropic $XX$ chain with four spin interaction. In terms of the equivalent spinless fermion (SF) representation the system is viewed as a lattice fermion gas with nearest-neighbor ($J$) and next-next-next-neighbor ($J^{\ast}/4$) hopping. It is shown that with the increase of four spin coupling, at $J^{\ast}_{c} = 4J/3$ the system experiences the Lifshitz type topological phase transition characterized by the tripling of Fermi points. The quantum phase transition (QPT) point marks transition from a gapless spin-liquid phase of standard $XX$ chain into again a gapless spin-liquid phase with different character of power-low decay of spin correlations. At the transition point the free fermion dispersion relation shows flattering at Fermi points, what determines singular character of density of states $ρ(ω)\sim (ω/J)^{-2/3}$ and as a consequence unconventional temperature dependence of heat capacity of the system $C\sim (T/J^{\ast})^{1/3}$, and singular magnetic susceptibility of the system $χ(H)\sim (H/J^{\ast})^{-2/3}$.
  In the case of alternating magnetic field the system is characterized by the rich ground state phase diagram which contains fully polarized (ferromagnetic), gapped antiferromagnetic (AFM) and spin liquid phases. At the transition point from the gapped AFM phase into the gapless polarized spin liquid phase the system shows rapid increase of magnetization $m\sim(H-H_c)^{1/6}$ and magnetic susceptibility a singular behavior as $χ(H)\sim (H-H_c)^{-5/6}$.

</details>


### [108] [Successive Electronic Topological Transitions in the Antiferromagnet UPd$_2$Al$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29151)
*Adrien Gourgout,Gaël Bastien,Dai Aoki,Gabriel Seyfarth,Ilya Sheikin,Andrey Varlamov,Gertrud Zwicknagl,Jacques Flouquet,Georg Knebel,Alexandre Pourret*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报告了重费米子化合物UPd₂Al₃在反铁磁态内、直至18 T亚磁转变（metamagnetic transition）之前，热电信号随磁场变化出现的连续异常，并将其归因于塞曼效应驱动的费米面拓扑变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重费米子体系在高磁场下费米面重构的微观机制，尤其是亚磁转变附近电子结构变化与热电输运性质的内在联系。

Method: 基于重整化微扰理论（renormalisation perturbation theory），并将f轨道划分为局域化与退局域化两个部分进行分析。

Result: 在H_M=18 T处观察到热电功率与霍尔系数符号的突然翻转，且在H_M之上热电功率出现大幅量子振荡，表明亚磁转变处发生强烈的费米面重构。

Conclusion: 上述热电与霍尔信号异常由塞曼效应驱动的费米面复杂拓扑变化引起，亚磁转变处的电子带展开（unfolding）导致费米面强烈重构。

Abstract: We report successive anomalies at low temperature in the magnetic field dependence of the thermoelectric signal in the heavy fermion compound UPd$_{2}$Al$_{3}$ inside the antiferromagnetic state up to the metamagnetic transition at $H_\text{M} =18$~T. Based on renormalisation perturbation theory and the partitioning of the $f$ orbitals into localized and delocalized parts, our analysis attributes these anomalies to complex topological changes of the Fermi surface driven by Zeeman effect. The observation of a sudden change of sign both in the thermoelectric power and in the Hall coefficient at $H_\text{M}$ in addition to the appearance of large quantum oscillations in the thermoelectric power above $H_\text{M}$ indicate a strong Fermi surface reconstruction at the metamagnetic transition due to the unfolding of the electronic bands.

</details>


### [109] [Photoemission insights into lanthanide-based crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29166)
*Dmitry Yu. Usachov,Georg Poelchen,Vasily S. Stolyarov,Kristin Kliemt,Cornelius Krellner,Denis V. Vyalikh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述了光电子能谱学（photoelectron spectroscopy）与晶体生长技术进步如何共同深化人们对镧系（lanthanide）材料体相与表面4$f$电子相关现象的理解，涵盖$k$分辨的$f$-$spd$杂化、Kondo晶格中$f$衍生费米面的温度演化、层依赖的4$f$磁各向异性以及非磁体相中表面铁磁序等系统研究成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镧系材料中强局域化的4$f$电子与巡游的spd-价态电子相互作用，产生了丰富的关联现象与性质，使其成为广泛研究的焦点。与体相相比，表面因配位减少、晶体电场改变、反演对称破缺以及表面态的产生，显著重塑了4$f$驱动的电子和磁性质，从而催生了低维系统的新功能，亟需系统梳理相关研究进展。

Method: 该综述采用文献梳理与作者团队系统研究成果整合的方式，先概述1970至1990年代的关键发展，再重点讨论最近借助光电子能谱学（包括$k$分辨测量）与改进的晶体生长技术所获得的体相与表面现象的详细洞察，并集中于作者及合作者形成连贯研究线的系统工作。

Result: 综述展示了若干突破性发现，包括揭示$k$分辨的$f$-$spd$杂化、Kondo晶格中$f$衍生费米面随温度的形成与演化、层依赖的4$f$磁各向异性，以及在体相基态为非磁的体系中表面出现铁磁有序等关键现象。

Conclusion: 该连贯研究线回应了4$f$系统物理中的核心问题，并为工程化设计新型镧系基架构（如异质结构heterostructures和超分子复合物supramolecular complexes）以获取新颖物理性质与功能开辟了机会。

Abstract: The interplay of strongly localized 4$f$ electrons with itinerant spd-valence states gives rise to a wide range of correlated phenomena and properties that place lanthanide materials at the focus of considerable research efforts. Beyond the bulk, their surfaces are of particular interest, where the reduced coordination, a modified crystal electric field, broken inversion symmetry in combination with strong spin-orbit coupling, and the emergence of surface states and resonances considerably reshape 4$f$-driven electronic and magnetic properties. This, in turn, enables novel functionalities of particular relevance for low-dimensional systems and their applications. This review summarizes how advances in photoelectron spectroscopies, together with improved crystal growth, have enabled detailed insights into bulk and surface phenomena of lanthanide-based crystals. After a brief overview of key developments from the 1970s to the 1990s, we discuss recent progress, focusing on systematic studies by the authors and collaborators that form a coherent line of research. These include the unveiling of $k$-resolved $f-spd$ hybridization, the formation and evolution with temperature of $f$-derived Fermi surface in Kondo lattices, layer-dependent 4$f$ magnetic anisotropy, the emergence of ferromagnetically ordered surfaces in systems with non-magnetic bulk ground state. This coherent line of research addresses core questions in the physics of 4$f$ systems and opens opportunities for engineering novel lanthanide-based architectures, including heterostructures and supramolecular complexes with novel physical properties and functionalities.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Stoner-Wohlfarth: Machine-Learning Models and Symbolic Regression of Hard-Magnet Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29249)
*Samuel J. R. Holt,Christina Winkler,Timoteo Colnaghi,Martin Lang,Swapneel A. Pathak,Andrea Petrocchi,Michael Adams,Thomas Schrefl,Andreas Marek,Hans Fangohr*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用12012个微观磁学（micromagnetic）模拟数据训练机器学习模型，用于从磁颗粒的内在微观磁学参数预测三个外在磁性能（矫顽力、剩磁和最大磁能积），相较于传统解析模型显著降低误差；符号回归恢复出Kronmüller形式的矫顽力表达式，并发现剩磁和最大磁能积的新闭合形式表达式；还探究了由外在性质反推内在参数的逆问题，并通过mammos-ai Python包开源了训练好的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从磁性颗粒的内在微观磁学参数预测其外在磁性（矫顽力、剩磁、最大磁能积）是永磁体建模的核心问题。已有的解析模型虽提供有用估计，但常忽略非均匀磁化过程；直接微观磁学模拟虽准确但计算成本高昂。本文旨在克服这两类方法的局限，为快速且准确地预测磁性作出贡献。

Method: 作者对理想化立方体磁性颗粒进行了12012次微观磁学（micromagnetic）模拟，参数覆盖饱和磁化强度、交换常数和单轴各向异性常数的广泛范围。在此模拟数据上训练机器学习模型，并在相同的保留测试数据上与解析模型进行基准对比；同时使用符号回归（symbolic regression）从数据中提取闭合形式的解析表达式。此外还研究了从外在性质反推内在参数的逆问题。

Result: 机器学习模型在预测所有三个外在性质时误差显著低于解析模型。符号回归恢复出Kronmüller形式的矫顽力表达式（包含与材料相关的有效退磁因子），并为剩磁和最大磁能积找到了新的闭合形式表达式，每个定律最多仅含两个拟合常数，却能达到接近机器学习模型的精度。逆问题研究中，饱和磁化强度和各向异性常数可被准确恢复，但交换常数因对外在性质影响较弱而无法准确反推。

Conclusion: 本研究证明机器学习方法能有效替代计算昂贵的微观磁学模拟，在推理速度与准确性之间取得良好平衡。通过发布mammos-ai Python包，使数千组候选参数集的筛选可在数秒内完成，而非直接模拟所需的数小时或数天，为永磁体材料的快速筛选与优化提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: Predicting the extrinsic properties from hysteresis loops of a magnetic grain, namely the coercive field, remanent magnetisation, and maximum energy product, from its intrinsic micromagnetic parameters is a central problem in permanent-magnet modelling. Established analytical models provide useful estimates but often neglect nonuniform magnetisation processes, whereas direct micromagnetic simulations are computationally expensive. In this work, we train machine-learning models on 12012 micromagnetic simulations of an idealised cubic grain, spanning broad ranges of the saturation magnetisation, exchange constant, and uniaxial anisotropy constant. Benchmarked against the analytical models on identical held-out data, the machine-learning models predict all three extrinsic properties with substantially lower errors. Symbolic regression recovers the Kronmüller form of the coercive field, with an effective demagnetising factor that depends on the material, and finds new closed-form expressions for the remanence and maximum energy product. Each law contains at most two fitted constants yet approaches the accuracy of the machine-learning models. We also investigate the inverse problem of recovering the intrinsic parameters from the three extrinsic properties. The saturation magnetisation and anisotropy constant are recovered accurately, whereas the exchange constant is not, because it influences the extrinsic properties only weakly. The trained models are released through the mammos-ai Python package, enabling thousands of candidate parameter sets to be screened in seconds rather than the hours or days required by direct micromagnetic simulation.

</details>


### [111] [Sound attenuation and velocity shift in antiferromagnetic spin-1/2 chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29392)
*Edmond Orignac,Emeric Caprani,Roberta Citro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了反铁磁自旋1/2 XXZ链在外磁场中的超声衰减与声速移动。将声速移动与自由能对交换相互作用的导数关联，从而利用可积性（integrability）技术计算任意温度下的声速移动。通过玻色化（bosonization）方法预测低温Tomonaga-Luttinger液体相中声速移动呈二次温度修正；在完全极化相附近发现z=2量子临界性的普适行为；Tomonaga-Luttinger液体相中超声衰减满足关于波长和温度的标度律，并在完全极化相附近出现增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨低维量子自旋系统中声子与磁性自旋激发的耦合机制，理解超声声速移动和衰减的温度与磁场依赖关系，特别是接近量子临界点时的普适行为。

Method: 将声速移动与自由能关于交换相互作用的导数建立联系，借助可积性技术计算任意温度的声速；采用玻色化方法分析Tomonaga-Luttinger液体相中的低温行为；针对完全极化相附近的量子临界区域分析其普适标度行为。

Result: 在Tomonaga-Luttinger液体相中声速移动呈现二次温度修正；完全极化相附近发现与z=2量子临界性相关的普适行为；Tomonaga-Luttinger液体相中超声衰减满足波长与温度的标度律，并且在完全极化相附近观测到衰减增强。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一套利用可积性和玻色化方法计算反铁磁自旋1/2 XXZ链中声速移动与超声衰减的理论框架，揭示了低温量子液体相和量子临界点附近声学响应的普适特征。

Abstract: We investigate ultrasound attenuation and sound velocity shift in antiferromagnetic spin-1/2 XXZ chains in magnetic field. We relate the sound velocity shift to derivatives of the free energy with respect to exchange interactions, permitting its calculation with integrability techniques at any temperature. Using bosonization, we predict the sound velocity shift exhibits a quadratic temperature correction at low temperatures in the Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid phase. Close to the fully polarized phase, a universal behavior associated with z=2 quantum criticality is found. In the Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid phase, ultrasound attenuation obeys a scaling law as a function of wavelength and temperature. An enhancement of attenuation is obtained near the fully polarized phase.

</details>


### [112] [Study of the Anomalous Hall effect by tuning the spin orientation in the Altermagnetic material CrSb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29646)
*Sreedevi Chintalapudi,Upasana Agrawal,Suvadip Das*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文以第一性原理计算结合最大局域瓦尼尔态（maximally localized Wannier states）方法，系统研究了交变磁体（altermagnet）CrSb的自旋与轨道分辨电子谱、声子色散、几何贝里曲率（Berry curvature）及拓扑表面态，并探讨了它们对反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity）的影响，发现反常霍尔电导随磁化强度线性变化的关系并不适用于交变磁体这类磁性类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁学（altermagnetism）领域的新进展以及对反常霍尔效应应用探索的需求增长，推动了人们重新审视现有磁性材料分类和对反常霍尔效应的既有认知，特别是在拓扑非平凡物相（topologically nontrivial phases）的背景下。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first principles）计算并结合计算效率较高的最大局域瓦尼尔态方法，对CrSb这一具有前景的交变磁体化合物在多种磁构型下进行模拟，考察外场效应，并解析自旋与轨道分辨电子谱、模分解声子色散关系、几何贝里曲率及拓扑表面态。

Result: 研究结果表明，反常霍尔电导随磁化强度呈线性行为的假设并不适用于所有磁性类别，例如交变磁体；CrSb中与反常霍尔电导相关的电子谱、贝里曲率与拓扑表面态特征被系统揭示。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解交变磁体中的拓扑性质与反常霍尔效应提供了新的视角，并暗示现有关于反常霍尔效应的普遍性关系需要根据具体磁性类别加以修正。

Abstract: Recent development in the field of altermagnetism, and increased demand for the search of applications of anomalous hall effect have ushered in a new era for novel quantum phases in materials. Quantum materials previously anticipated to be scientifically predictable have unfolded novel properties that brought them into the spotlight. These manifestations have led us to rethink our understanding of existing classification of magnetic materials and preexisting notions about anomalous hall effect in the light of topologically nontrivial phases of matter. One such recent de- velopment lies in the novel class of alter-magnetic materials with prospect for quantum computing. In this article, we delineate the spin and orbital resolved electronic spectrum, mode-decomposed phonon dispersion relations, geometrical berry curvature and topological surface states and their implications on anomalous Hall conductivity in the promising altermagnetic compound CrSb. We further utilize first principles calculations coupled with computationally efficient maximally localized wannier states of numerous magnetic configurations of the altermagnet to simulate the effect of external fields and elucidate the fact that the linear behaviour of anomalous hall conductivity with magnetization does not necessarily hold true for all magnetic classes, such as altermagnets.

</details>


### [113] [Magnetic properties of a quasi-two-dimensional spin-1/2 antiferromagnet Y2CuGe4O12](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29676)
*J. Khatua,Changhyun Koo,Suyoung Kim,Eundeok Mun,Yugo Oshima,V. K. Sahu,Heung-Sik Kim,B. Koteswararao,Kwang-Yong Choi,P. Khuntia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过磁性 susceptibility、比热（specific heat）、电子自旋共振（ESR）测量以及密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，研究了Y₂CuGe₄O₁₂多晶样品，发现其呈现扭曲三角晶格磁体特性，其中最近邻及次近邻交换相互作用主导磁性行为，在极低温下仍未出现长程磁有序，而是在外加磁场下进入场极化态，为探索阻挫驱动的量子现象提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间各向异性低维磁体中的竞争磁性相互作用与阻挫诱导的量子涨落往往产生奇异磁现象（如场诱导相），因此需要寻找并表征具有此类特性的新型化合物，以深入理解阻挫磁体中的量子行为。

Method: 对Y₂CuGe₄O₁₂多晶样品进行了晶体结构、磁化率、比热和电子自旋共振（ESR）测量，并用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算进行佐证；此外，通过测量外加磁场下比热的变化来分析场依赖的磁行为。

Result: Cu²⁺离子形成扭曲三角晶格，包含竞争的面内铁磁（J₁≈0.138 K，J₂≈0.01 K）与反铁磁（J₃≈-3.22 K）相互作用及较弱的面间反铁磁耦合（J₄≈-1.56 K），导致小的居里-外斯温度θ_CW=-1.8 K；在低至0.4 K温度下无长程磁有序，仅出现短程自旋关联；外加磁场在饱和场μ₀H_s=2.6 T以上将系统驱动至场极化态，其中比热呈现指数温度依赖，对应带隙磁振子（magnon）激发。

Conclusion: Y₂CuGe₄O₁₂是一种罕见的扭曲三角晶格磁体，其磁性行为由次近邻交换相互作用主导，为探索阻挫驱动的量子现象提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: Competing magnetic interactions and frustration-induced quantum fluctuations in spatially anisotropic low-dimensional magnets often give rise to exotic magnetic phenomena, including field-induced phases. Here, we present crystal structure, magnetic susceptibility, specific heat, and electron spin resonance (ESR) measurements on polycrystalline Y$2$CuGe$4$O${12}$, supported by density functional theory (DFT) calculations. In this compound, the Cu$^{2+}$ ions form a distorted triangular lattice with competing intraplanar ferromagnetic ($J_1 \approx 0.138$ K and $J_2 \approx 0.01$ K) and antiferromagnetic ($J_3 \approx -3.22$ K) exchange interactions, together with a weaker interplanar antiferromagnetic coupling ($J_4 \approx -1.56$ K). These interactions account for the small Curie--Weiss temperature, $θ{\rm CW}=-1.8$ K. Despite the dominant antiferromagnetic interactions, no signature of long-range magnetic ordering is observed down to 0.4 K. Instead, broad maxima in both the magnetic susceptibility and magnetic specific heat reveal the development of short-range spin correlations, further supported by the critical ESR linewidth broadening characteristic of low-dimensional frustrated magnets. Application of an external magnetic field progressively suppresses the broad maximum in the magnetic specific heat, reflecting competition between the Zeeman and exchange energy scales, and drives the system into a field-polarized state above the saturation field, $μ_0H_{\rm s}=2.6$ T. In this regime, the magnetic specific heat exhibits an exponential temperature dependence, consistent with gapped magnon excitations. These results establish Y$_2$CuGe$4$O${12}$ as a rare distorted triangular-lattice magnet in which further-neighbor exchange interactions dominate the magnetic behavior, providing a promising platform for exploring frustration-driven quantum phenomena.

</details>

### [114] [Transcorrelated Random-Phase Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28845)
*Abdallah Ammar,Enzo Monino,Anthony Scemama,Emmanuel Giner,Pierre-François Loos*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文将随机相位近似（random-phase approximation, RPA）扩展到非厄米变换关联（transcorrelated, TC）哈密顿量，提出了TC-dRPA和TC-RPAx两种方法。对原子和分子基态相关能的计算精度较传统RPA提升近一个数量级，且加速了基组收敛；但对垂直激发能的改进有限，归因于Jastrow因子仅针对基态优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RPA方法在计算基态相关能时存在精度不足和基组收敛缓慢的问题，且RPA尚未与非厄米变换关联哈密顿量相结合，后者通过Jastrow相关因子显式包含三体相互作用，有望提升电子关联描述的准确性。

Method: 将RPA扩展至包含Jastrow相关因子产生的三体相互作用的非厄米TC哈密顿量上，分别实现了直接RPA（dRPA）和含交换的RPA（RPAx）两种形式，并应用于原子（He、Ne）和分子（H2O、NH3、CH4、H2CO）的基态相关能和垂直激发能计算。

Result: 对于基态相关能，TC处理显著提高了精度并加速了基组收敛，误差较传统RPA降低近一个数量级；对于垂直激发能，TC处理仅带来边际性的改进。

Conclusion: TC-RPA是一种精确且计算高效的基态能量方法，但Jastrow因子的基态优化不足以捕捉激发态的电子特性，需要发展的态特定（state-specific）Jastrow优化方案才能可靠描述激发态。

Abstract: We extend the random-phase approximation (RPA) to the non-Hermitian transcorrelated (TC) Hamiltonian, which explicitly includes three-body interactions generated by a Jastrow correlation factor. We consider both the direct RPA (dRPA) and RPA with exchange (RPAx). We apply the resulting TC-dRPA and TC-RPAx methods to calculate ground-state correlation energies and vertical excitation energies for atoms (\ce{He} and \ce{Ne}) and small molecules (\ce{H2O}, \ce{NH3}, \ce{CH4}, and \ce{H2CO}). For ground-state correlation energies, the TC treatment substantially improves accuracy and accelerates basis set convergence, reducing errors by nearly an order of magnitude relative to conventional RPA calculations. By contrast, it yields only marginal improvements in vertical excitation energies. We attribute this limited effect to the ground-state optimization of the Jastrow factor, which does not adequately capture the distinct electronic character of excited states. These results establish TC-RPA as an accurate and computationally efficient approach to ground-state energetics, while highlighting the need for state-specific Jastrow optimization to achieve reliable descriptions of excited states.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Potential-defect-driven collective modes of one-dimensional two-component quantum droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29292)
*Harsimranjit Kaur,Kuldeep Suthar*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维超稀释量子液滴（quantum droplets）二元混合物在中心势缺陷存在下的性质。研究发现势阱中自束缚量子液滴的形成源于李-黄-杨（Lee-Huang-Yang）量子涨落与排斥平均场相互作用的平衡；势垒则引发随原子数变化的极化转变（polarization transition），表现为准粒子模式的间断与软化；临界原子数随组分间吸引相互作用增强而减小；相互作用淬火动力学显示两分量系统中的局域化与扩散碎裂液滴行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子液滴二元混合物在一维系统中受中心势缺陷影响时所展现的极化转变和集体激发谱特征，以理解外部势对量子液滴结构和动力学行为的调控机制。

Method: 对含中心势缺陷的一维二元超稀释量子液滴混合物进行理论分析，考察势强度和原子数性质，研究其极化转变与低能集体激发谱，并通过相互作用淬火时间动力学分析势垒和势阱中的液滴演化行为。

Result: 证实吸引的李-黄-杨量子涨落与排斥平均场相互作用平衡可在势阱中形成自束缚量子液滴；势垒引起随原子数变化的极化转变，反映为激发谱上准粒子模式的间断和软化；转变的临界原子数随组分间吸引相互作用增强而减小；相互作用淬火动力学分别显示出两分量系统的局域化和扩散碎裂液滴现象。

Conclusion: 中心势缺陷的强度显著影响二元量子液滴混合物的结构转变与激发性质，极化转变的临界条件与组分间相互作用强度密切相关，为量子液滴系统的势调控提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We examine a one-dimensional binary mixture of ultradilute quantum droplets in the presence of a central potential defect. The properties of the potential strength and the number of atoms exhibit distinct polarization transitions and low-lying ollective excitation spectra. The balance of (attractive) Lee-Huang-Yang quantum fluctuations and repulsive mean-field interactions results in a self-bound quantum droplet in a potential well. However, the potential barrier causes the polarization transition with the number of atoms, which is reflected in the excitation spectrum as a discontinuity and softening of the quasiparticle modes. We reveal that the critical number of atoms for the transition decreases as the attractive intercomponent interaction increases. Finally, the quench time dynamics of the interaction in potential barrier and well show the localization and diffusive fragmented droplets of two-component systems.

</details>


### [116] [Quasiparticle modes across soliton transition with density-dependent gauge field in optical lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29309)
*Poorava Kumar Meena,Kuldeep Suthar*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了弱相互作用极限下一维光学晶格中超冷玻色子由密度依赖隧穿（density-dependent tunneling，DDT）引起的一阶量子相变，从玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate，BEC）过渡到局域孤子（localized soliton）过程中低能准粒子模的演化。通过离散Bogoliubov理论和色散曲线，揭示了孤子相中偶极及更高激发能量随DDT增大而升高且零能模保持，并进一步展示了孤子不表现出Bloch振荡、呼吸振荡被抑制等独特动力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维光学晶格中超冷玻色子的密度依赖隧穿会产生非平凡的规范场（gauge field）效应，导致BEC到局域孤子的第一阶量子相变。该研究旨在理解这一相变过程中低能准粒子模的演化规律，揭示密度依赖规范场对系统动力学性质的独特影响。

Method: 采用离散Bogoliubov理论（discrete Bogoliubov theory）和色散曲线（dispersion curves）分析弱相互作用极限下体系低能准粒子模在相变前后的演化；同时结合复规范场平面的相图（phase diagram）和准粒子模能隙来验证临界行为。

Result: 研究发现，随着DDT增强，孤子相中偶极及更高激发态的准粒子能量升高，但零能模（zero-energy mode）保持；有效隧穿降低导致移动孤子的能量代价增大，从而产生更快的偶极振荡。排斥性在位原子相互作用通过稳定晶格孤子将临界规范场移向更大值。孤子不表现出Bloch振荡（Bloch oscillations），因为有效动量变得密度依赖并使内部结构变形；势阱淬火（trap quench）的动力学响应不激发局域波包的宽度，呼吸振荡（breathing oscillations）被抑制。

Conclusion: 密度依赖规范场使孤子态具备区别于凝聚体态的两类独特动力学性质——无Bloch振荡和抑制呼吸振荡，这些特征源于有效动量的密度依赖性及系统的局域化特性。

Abstract: Tunneling of ultracold bosons confined in a one-dimensional optical lattice results in a nontrivial gauge field depending on density-difference between the sites involved. The density dependent tunneling (DDT) causes a first-order quantum phase transition from the Bose-Einstein condensate to the localized soliton. Here, we examine the low-lying quasiparticle mode evolution across the transition in the weakly-interacting limit. To this end, we employ the discrete Bogoliubov theory and dispersion curves to reveal an increase in the quasiparticle energies of dipole (and higher) excitations with DDT in the soliton phase while preserving the zero-energy mode. This is due to the decrease in effective tunneling, resulting in a larger energy cost to move the soliton, and causes faster dipole oscillations. The repulsive on-site atomic interaction further shifts the critical gauge field of DDT to a larger value by stabilizing the lattice soliton. The latter is corroborated by a phase diagram in the complex gauge field plane and mode energy gap of quasiparticles. We further show that the soliton does not exhibit Bloch oscillations as the effective momentum becomes density-dependent and deforms its internal structure. The dynamical response of the trap quench does not excite the width of localized wave-packet, and the breathing oscillations are suppressed. The latter two dynamical properties of the condensate uniquely contrast the soliton state due to the density-dependent gauge field.

</details>


### [117] [Dissipation-enhanced vortex clustering in a compressible quantum fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29475)
*P. Comaron,M. Matuszewski,D. Sanvitto,D. Ballarini,A. S. Lanotte*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该论文通过数值模拟发现，在受限的可压缩量子流体中，线性粒子损失可以在瞬态过程中增强涡旋聚集效应，使其超越保守演化时的水平，且增强效果在中等粒子损失率时最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为涡旋聚集（vortex clustering）与保守的二维量子流体动力学相关，粒子损失主要被预期通过缩短涡旋关联发展的时间来限制聚集。本文旨在反驳这一假设，探究粒子损失是否反而能增强瞬态涡旋聚集。

Method: 本文对以随机涡旋和反涡旋（vortices and antivortices）分布初始化的自由衰减、受限二维凝聚体进行数值模拟，系统改变粒子寿命（particle lifetime），研究最大聚集程度对粒子寿命的非单调依赖关系。

Result: 研究发现最大聚集程度随粒子寿命呈显著非单调变化，增强效应在中间粒子损失率处最强，此时损失诱导的背景稀疏化（background rarefaction）与不可压缩动能弛豫（incompressible kinetic energy relaxation）发生在可比时间尺度上。

Conclusion: 线性粒子损失选择出一个有限的动力学窗口，在此窗口内受限可压缩量子流体能够发展出比保守极限更强的高同号涡旋关联（same-sign vortex correlations），表明粒子损失并非单纯抑制而可能增强涡旋聚集。

Abstract: Vortex clustering is commonly associated with conservative two-dimensional quantum-fluid dynamics. Therefore, particle loss is mainly expected to limit clustering by shortening the time available for vortex correlations to develop. Here we show instead that particle loss can enhance transient vortex clustering beyond the conservative evolution. We numerically study freely decaying, confined two-dimensional condensates initialized with random distributions of vortices and antivortices, and find a pronounced nonmonotonic dependence of the maximum clustering on particle lifetime. The enhancement is strongest at intermediate particle-loss rates, where loss-induced background rarefaction and incompressible kinetic energy relaxation occur on comparable timescales. Our results identify a finite dynamical window, selected by linear particle loss, in which a confined compressible quantum fluid develops stronger same-sign vortex correlations than in the conservative limit.

</details>


### [118] [Tunable two-component ultracold molecular gases with vibrational shielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29611)
*Bijit Mukherjee,Michał Tomza*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实现超冷极性分子（ultracold polar molecules）双组分可调谐量子混合物的方法，核心机制称为“振动屏蔽”（vibrational shielding）：处于两个不同振转态（rovibrational states）的极性分子对展现出排斥性相互作用，无需外部场即可实现碰撞屏蔽；通过外加静电场或微波场可独立调谐各组分乃至组分间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双组分原子量子气体中可调谐的磁Feshbach共振（magnetic Feshbach resonances）是研究量子混合物的关键手段，但该方法在极性分子体系中难以直接适用。作者旨在寻找一种无需外部磁场、可稳定且可调谐的方法，以在极性分子体系中实现类似的量子混合物实验平台。

Method: 作者首先证明处于两个不同振转态的极性分子对之间存在排斥性相互作用，从而在无需任何外部场的情况下实现碰撞屏蔽（collisional shielding），即“振动屏蔽”。进一步，作者展示该组分间相互作用可通过外部静态电场或微波场进行调谐，其中微波场能同时稳定双组分体相混合物中的组分内与组分间相互作用；此外，两个独立微波场可分别调节单个组分的相互作用。

Result: 证明振动屏蔽机制可在无外部场条件下抑制碰撞损失；验证静电或微波场可调谐组分间相互作用；表明微波场能够稳定体相聚集体中的组分内与组分间相互作用；证明双微波场可实现各组分相互作用的独立调谐。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用极性分子实验实现可调谐量子混合物开辟了道路，其功能类似于双组分原子量子气体中的可调谐磁Feshbach共振机制。

Abstract: We propose a method to realize stable, tunable two-component quantum mixtures of ultracold polar molecules. First, we show that a pair of polar molecules in two distinct rovibrational states exhibits a repulsive interaction, thereby leading to collisional shielding without requiring any external field. We refer to this as "vibrational shielding". This intercomponent interaction is tunable by an external static electric or a microwave field, with the latter stabilizing both inter- and intracomponent interactions in a two-component bulk mixture. Additionally, we show that two microwave fields can independently tune the interactions of the individual components. Our findings thus open the door to the experimental realization of tunable quantum mixtures using polar molecules, analogous to tunable magnetic Feshbach resonances in two-component atomic quantum gases.

</details>


### [119] [Pairing near the boundary of a box-shaped trap in the BEC-BCS crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29620)
*Kelly R. Patton,Daniel E. Sheehy*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了囚禁在盒形势阱中的吸引相互作用费米子的配对行为。与无限平移不变系统不同，盒形边界附近的局域配对随耦合强度敏感变化：在弱耦合BCS区域呈现Friedel型振荡，并随耦合增强向BEC区域过渡时被抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限均匀系统中局域配对在空间上均匀分布，并随相互作用变化经历BEC-BCS渡越（crossover）；而在盒形容器约束下，局域配对预计在边界附近快速变化，这一边界效应的本质尚待阐明。

Method: 在半无限超流体（semi-infinite superfluid）极限下处理该问题，分析边界配对随耦合参数变化的行为，区分弱耦合BCS区域与强耦合BEC区域。

Result: 发现边界配对的性质对耦合强度高度敏感：弱耦合BCS区域中局域配对呈现类弗里德尔振荡（Friedel-like oscillations），而随耦合强度增强向BEC区域过渡时，此类振荡被抑制。

Conclusion: 盒形囚禁下的费米子配对在边界处偏离均匀行为，其结构由耦合强度决定，反映了有限几何对超流配对的重要调制作用。

Abstract: We study pairing of attractively interacting fermions confined to a box-shaped trap. In contrast to the infinite translationally-invariant case, where the local pairing order is spatially uniform and undergoes the Bose-Einstein Condensate to Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BEC-BCS) crossover as interactions are varied, in this case the local pairing is expected to vary rapidly near the edge of the box. We address this problem in the limit of a semi-infinite superfluid, finding that the nature of the edge pairing depends sensitively on the coupling. The local pairing exhibits Friedel-like oscillations in the weak coupling BCS regime that are suppressed with increasing coupling strength towards the BEC regime.

</details>


### [120] [Revealing the Spin Hydrodynamics of a Spin-Imbalanced Unitary Fermi Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29647)
*DeChao Zhang,Johannes Lang,J. E. Thomas*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过自旋不平衡的统一费米气体（unitary Fermi gas）在均匀光学盒中的实验，测量了自旋扩散率（spin diffusivity）和自旋塞贝克/佩尔捷系数（spin Seebeck/Peltier coefficients），为强相互作用流体中的自旋传输提供了无参数基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体力学（hydrodynamics）支配着从夸克胶子等离子体膨胀到量子材料中黏性电子流等多种自然界集体现象，但在强相互作用流体中，自旋（spin）的输运机制仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 在均匀光学盒约束的自旋不平衡统一费米气体中，通过淬灭一个同时调制密度与自旋极化的空间周期性光学势阱，观察多体系统的弛豫过程，从而同时确定自旋扩散率以及自旋塞贝克/佩尔捷系数。

Result: 实验测量成功获得了均质量子气体中的自旋输运系数，提供了无自由参数的微观理论基准。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个超冷原子平台，用于研究强关联物质中的自旋热电子学（spin caloritronics）。

Abstract: Hydrodynamics governs diverse collective phenomena in nature, from the expansion of a quarkgluon plasma to viscous electron flow in quantum materials. In strongly interacting hydrodynamic fluids, the transport of spin remains poorly understood. Here, we investigate spin transport in the hydrodynamic regime of a spin-imbalanced unitary Fermi gas confined in a uniform optical box. By quenching a spatially periodic optical potential that modulates both the density and spin polarization, we observe the relaxation of the many-body system, which determines both the spin diffusivity and the spin Seebeck/Peltier coefficients in a homogeneous quantum gas. Our measurements provide parameter-free benchmarks for microscopic theories and establish an ultracold atom platform for studying spin caloritronics in strongly correlated matter.

</details>
