### [121] [Obstructions to Traversable Wormholes in Einstein-Dirac Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28738)
*Robert J. Weinbaum*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究经典Einstein-Dirac-Maxwell系统中Dirac场能否作为可穿越虫洞的源物质。作者在固定虫洞几何上证明正频Dirac场可违反平均零能量条件（ANEC），但数值搜索发现无法构造出两端均渐近平坦的可穿越虫洞解，支持Einstein-Dirac系统不 admits 由物理上有意义的Dirac场所驱动的可穿越虫洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole）是广义相对论中最引人注目的假想解之一，但任何此类几何都需要违反平均零能量条件（averaged null energy condition, ANEC）的物质。部分近期工作声称在经典Einstein-Dirac-Maxwell系统中找到了可穿越虫洞解，但这些工作未正确处理单粒子态不自相互作用及模式函数为正频的问题，因此需要重新审视Dirac场作为此类物质候选者的可行性。

Method: 首先在固定的虫洞几何上证明经典正频Dirac场可以违反ANEC，从而确认其作为虫洞源物质的候选资格。随后数值搜索由确定频率ω>0、角动量量子数ℓ和确定宇称的Dirac场所驱动的静态、球对称、渐近平坦可穿越虫洞几何；对反射对称虫洞，额外进行不假设Dirac解具有确定宇称的搜索，并广泛扫描喉部形成渐近数据。

Result: 数值搜索发现"部分虫洞解"（partial-wormhole solutions），描述了具有正确一端渐近行为的规则虫洞喉部，但这些解无法延续到第二个渐近平坦末端。在反射对称虫洞情况下，即使不假设确定宇称，通过广泛扫描喉部形成渐近数据，也无法获得反射对称虫洞喉部所需的必要条件。

Conclusion: 综合这些结果有力支持Einstein-Dirac系统在由物理上有意义的Dirac场驱动时不 admits 可穿越虫洞解。

Abstract: Traversable wormholes are among the most striking hypothetical solutions of general relativity, but every such geometry requires matter that violates the averaged null energy condition (ANEC). A possible candidate for such matter is the Dirac field. Several recent works have claimed traversable wormhole solutions of the classical Einstein-Dirac-Maxwell system, but these works did not properly take into account that single-particle states do not self-interact electromagnetically and that the mode functions of single-particle states are of positive frequency. In this paper, we show that classical, positive-frequency Dirac fields on certain fixed wormhole geometries can violate ANEC, so they are indeed candidates for sourcing traversable wormholes. We then numerically search for static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat traversable wormhole geometries sourced by Dirac fields of a definite frequency $ω>0$, angular momentum quantum number $\ell$, and definite parity. We find "partial-wormhole solutions," describing a regular wormhole throat with correct asymptotics at one end of the wormhole, but we find that these solutions cannot be continued to a second asymptotically flat end. In the case of reflection-symmetric wormholes, we perform an additional search where we do not assume that the Dirac solution has a definite parity. In this case, after an extensive scan of throat-forming asymptotic data, we find that the conditions necessary for a reflection-symmetric wormhole throat cannot be obtained. Taken together, these results strongly support that the Einstein-Dirac system does not admit traversable wormhole solutions when sourced by a physically meaningful Dirac field.

</details>


### [122] [Extending the Comisso-Asenjo Energy Extraction Mechanism to Pure Lovelock Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28789)
*Chen Zhou,Ikhtiyor Eshtursunov,Sanjar Shaymatov,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将Comisso-Asenjo磁重联（magnetic reconnection）机制拓展到纯Lovelock/Gauss-Bonnet（GB）引力中的旋转黑洞，系统分析了自旋参数、等离子体磁化、磁场方向和重联位置对能量提取效率和功率的影响，发现该机制在某些参数空间区域比Blandford-Znajek（BZ）过程具有更高的提取功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨高维纯Lovelock引力理论中旋转黑洞的旋转能提取机制，检验磁重联作为高效能量提取过程在更高维时空中的可行性及其与爱因斯坦引力的差异。

Method: 将Comisso-Asenjo磁重联机制应用于纯Lovelock/GB引力框架下的旋转黑洞（维度范围$2N+2\leq D\leq 4N+1$），通过参数扫描系统研究自旋参数、等离子体磁化、磁场方向及重联位置对能量提取效率与功率的影响，并与BZ过程和爱因斯坦引力情形进行对比。

Result: 除$D=3N+1$特殊维度外，纯Lovelock黑洞在能量提取特征上与爱因斯坦黑洞存在显著差异；当维度从$D=6$增加到$9$时，单旋转构型的快速旋转纯Lovelock黑洞的磁重联能量提取效率逐步提高；在某些参数空间区域，Comisso-Asenjo磁重联机制的能量提取功率高于BZ过程。

Conclusion: 磁重联是从快速旋转的纯Lovelock/GB黑洞提取旋转能的高效机制，该类黑洞在高能天体物理现象中具有潜在的相关性和重要性。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend the Comisso-Asenjo magnetic reconnection (MR) mechanism to rotating black holes (BHs) in pure Lovelock/Gauss-Bonnet (GB) gravity in dimension $2N+2\leq D\leq 4N+1$ (where $N$ is the Lovelock polynomial degree of $N$th order term in the action). We perform a comprehensive analysis of the efficiency and power of extracted energy by exploring the effects of the spin parameter, plasma magnetization, magnetic field orientation, and reconnection location. Our results reveal distinctive energetic features of pure Lovelock BHs relative to their Einstein counterparts, except in the special case of $D=3N+1$, where the two theories coincide. Our results demonstrate that magnetic reconnection becomes increasingly efficient in extracting rotational energy from rapidly rotating pure Lovelock BHs with single rotation configuration as the spacetime dimension increases from $D=6$ to $9$. Furthermore, the Comisso-Asenjo MR mechanism can produce higher extraction power than the Blandford-Znajek (BZ) process in certain regions of parameter space because of its enhanced energy extraction rate. These results show that magnetic reconnection is an efficient mechanism for extracting rotational energy from rapidly rotating pure Lovelock/GB BHs, highlighting their relevance to high-energy astrophysical phenomena.

</details>


### [123] [Excitation region of Kerr black hole quasinormal modes from Stokes geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28930)
*Naritaka Oshita*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文从解析和数值两个角度研究了黑洞准正则模（QNM）的激发半径，提出激发半径由WKB解在反Stokes线上的主导性切换决定，并通过数值收敛测试验证了该图像，最终论证激发半径一般依赖于过音数（overtone number），形成有效的QNM收敛区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准正则模是研究黑洞扰动与引力波环形相（ringdown）的核心工具，但QNM激发的空间范围（激发半径）此前未被系统理解，尤其在Kerr时空中缺乏一般性的解析描述，需要建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 解析方面，提出QNM激发半径由WKB解在反Stokes线（anti-Stokes line）上的主导性切换确定，并由此推导Kerr时空中激发半径的解析条件；数值方面，采用QNM加尾项（tail）展开的收敛性测试作为独立验证手段。

Result: 在Schwarzschild极限下复现了已知值 r=2.556929M，区别于光环半径 r=3M；证明在高过音极限下激发半径与角模数 l 无关；数值收敛测试在低自旋和中等自旋的高过音极限下与Stokes几何一致；在极端极限下，激发半径趋向外视界，且由零阻尼模（zero-damping modes）而非高过音极限刻画。

Conclusion: QNM激发半径一般依赖过音数，由此形成有效的QNM收敛区域；在极端极限下环形相由零阻尼模主导，这与激发半径趋向外视界的结论自洽。

Abstract: We investigate the excitation region of black hole quasinormal modes (QNMs) from both analytical and numerical perspectives. On the analytical side, we propose that the QNM excitation radius is identified by the dominance switching of WKB solutions across an anti-Stokes line. Based on this picture, we derive the condition for the QNM excitation radius in Kerr spacetime. In the Schwarzschild limit with mass $M$, we reproduce the previously known value $r=2.556929 M$, which differs from the light ring radius $r=3M$. We also show that the excitation radius is independent of the angular mode $\ell$ in the high-overtone limit. As an independent approach, we employ the numerical convergence test of QNM-plus-tail expansion and obtain values consistent with the Stokes geometry in the high-overtone limit at low and intermediate spins. In the extremal limit, the QNM convergence radius approaches the outer horizon, which is captured by the Stokes geometry of the zero-damping modes, rather than the high-overtone limit. This is consistent with the fact that the ringdown is dominated by zero-damping modes in the extremal limit. Based on the complementary analysis of Stokes geometry and the convergence test, we argue that in general, the QNM excitation radius depends on the QNM overtone number, giving rise to an effective QNM convergence region.

</details>


### [124] [The brachistochrone problem for a constant velocity traveler in static and spherically symmetric spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29265)
*Longfei Wang,Junji Jia*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了静态球对称（spherically symmetric, SSS）时空中匀速旅行者的最速降线问题（brachistochrone problem），推导了超相对论旅行者在一般SSS度规下的最速降线方程并以积分形式给出解，随后应用于奇异等温球（singular isothermal sphere, SIS）和相对论Plummer质量分布两种代表性时空中。对SIS时空，边界内BT的解为解析且始终弯曲；对相对论Plummer模型，当起终点半径远小于核心区时BT为过原点的直线，位于核心区外时则弯曲。最后通过广义费马原理证明亚光速匀速旅行者的BT与超相对论情形相同，只是总时间乘以1/v因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最速降线问题（brachistochrone problem）是经典变分问题的相对论推广，此前在静态球对称时空中尚未对匀速旅行者进行系统研究。作者希望建立一般SSS度规下最速降线轨迹（BT）的理论框架，并通过具体天体物理质量分布模型检验BT的性质及其物理含义。

Method: 作者针对静态球对称时空中的匀速旅行者，利用变分法推导了超相对论旅行者的BT方程，并以积分形式给出形式解。随后将结果分别应用于带有限边界的SIS时空和相对论Plummer质量轮廓，进行解析或数值求解。最后通过广义费马原理（generalized Fermat's principle）将结论推广至亚光速匀速旅行者情形。

Result: SIS时空边界内BT始终弯曲；方程状态指数w增大时，BT的转折半径（turning radius）r0和总时间随之增大。边界外，中心天体越重则r0和总旅行时间越大。相对论Plummer模型中，当起终点半径与核心区尺寸相当或更小时BT为过原点的直线；当端点远在核心区外时BT弯曲，且核心越集中转折半径越大。亚光速旅行者的BT与超相对论情形相同，总时间按1/v缩放。

Conclusion: 静态球对称时空中的极速路径与时空几何和背景质量分布结构密切相关：质量分布的核心区域对BT的弯曲行为有定性影响，而速度仅通过1/v因子缩放总时间，不改变最速路径本身。该结果可为相对论性最速降线问题在引力环境中应用提供一般性框架。

Abstract: This work investigates the brachistochrone problem for a traveler with constant local velocity within static and spherically symmetric (SSS) spacetimes. The brachistochrone trajectory (BT) equations for ultra-relativistic travelers are derived for general SSS metrics, and the solution is formally obtained in an integral form. We then apply the result to two representative spacetimes corresponding to the singular isothermal sphere (SIS) with a finite boundary and the relativistic Plummer mass profile, respectively. For the SIS spacetime, the BT inside the boundary is solved analytically and found always to bend. As the equation of state index $w$ increases, the turning radius $r_0$ of the BT, and consequently the total time, also increase. For the BT outside the boundary, it is found that the heavier the central object, the larger the $r_0$ and the total travel time. For the relativistic Plummer model, the BT will be a straight line passing through the origin when the initial and final points' radii are comparable or smaller than the size of the core region of the mass distribution. When the end points lie well outside the core region, the BT bends, exhibiting a larger turning radius for a more concentrated core. We then extend the consideration to travelers with subluminal constant velocity $v$ and show through the generalized Fermat's principle that the BT will be the same geodesic in the optical metric as ultra-relativistic travelers, with the total travel time scaled by a factor of $1/v$.

</details>


### [125] [Deviation of trajectories in nonrelativistic and relativistic cases and Shirokov effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29350)
*Yu. V. Pavlov,V. P. Vandeev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了非相对论与相对论情形下圆形轨道附近的偏移（deviation）行为，获得了偏差方程的一般解，给出了静态球对称时空中近圆轨道近日点移动（pericenter shift）的显式表达式，评估了宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）对近日点移动的影响，并找到了不存在Shirokov效应的球对称度规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道偏移与近日点进动是检验引力理论的重要可观测效应，但非相对论与相对论两种情形下的完整偏差方程一般解此前尚不充分，且宇宙学常数与Shirokov效应对轨道的影响需要系统考察。

Method: 通过求解轨道偏差方程（deviation equations）获得一般解；在非相对论情形下求解使近圆轨道闭合的势函数，在广义相对论中寻找相应度规分量；针对静态球对称时空推导近圆轨道的近日点移动显式表达式，并分别估算宇宙学常数效应与Shirokov效应的影响。

Result: 获得了非相对论与相对论轨道偏差方程的一般解；确定了使近圆轨道闭合的非相对论势函数及广义相对论度规分量；给出了静态球对称时空中近圆轨道近日点移动的显式表达式；提供了宇宙学常数对近日点移动影响的估计值；并找到了不存在Shirokov效应的球对称度规形式。

Conclusion: 该研究为近圆轨道偏移与近日点移动提供了完整的理论与解析框架，并明确刻画了宇宙学常数和Shirokov效应在轨道动力学中的具体作用条件。

Abstract: We compare the deviations of circular orbits in the nonrelativistic and relativistic cases. General solutions of the deviation equations are obtained. We find the potentials for the nonrelativistic case and the metric components in general relativity for which trajectories close to circular ones are closed curves. Explicit expressions for the pericenter shift of an orbit close to a circular one in a static spherically symmetric spacetime are obtained. Estimates of the cosmological constant effect on the orbit pericenter shift are given. Finally, we find the spherically symmetric metric in which the Shirokov effect is absent.

</details>


### [126] [Resolution of Infrared Entanglement Divergences via the Extended Uncertainty Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29427)
*Subhra Mondal,S. Shankaranarayanan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明扩展不确定性原理（Extended Uncertainty Principle, EUP）可以将量子系统中纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）的红外（infrared, IR）发散问题自然解决：EUP引入的几何限制将位置方差限制在有限范围内，从而抑制零模的空间离域化，使纠缠谱保持离散且均匀间隔，纠缠熵饱和至有限值。作者通过对一维谐振子链和无质量标量场的精确求解，确立了这一几何正则化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统的纠缠熵通常同时存在紫外（ultraviolet）和红外发散。在低频极限下，红外发散与零模（zero-modes）的无界空间离域化密切相关，这一病态特征在耦合谐振子和无质量标量场中普遍存在，亟需一种自然的解决机制。

Method: 作者引入扩展不确定性原理（Extended Uncertainty Principle, EUP），该原理在规范对易关系中引入大尺度几何修正。通过精确求解EUP框架下的简单谐振子，建立内在几何束缚的存在性；随后将此正则化机制扩展至多体系统，计算一维谐振子链和无质量标量场的纠缠熵与纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）。

Result: EUP诱发的空间束缚阻止了低能长波模的累积，使得即使在严格无质量极限下纠缠谱仍保持离散和均匀间隔；该非零的模间隙（modular gap）有效限制了真空的局部纠缠温度，从而纠缠熵饱和至有限值。

Conclusion: EUP为量子场论中零模红外发散问题提供了一种稳健的几何解决方案，该方案源自背景里奇标量（Ricci scalar）相关的内禀局域化长度尺度，不依赖额外假设即可抑制空间离域化。

Abstract: The entanglement entropy of quantum systems typically exhibits both ultraviolet and infrared (IR) divergences. In the low-frequency limit, the IR divergence is intimately tied to the unbounded spatial delocalization of zero-modes, a pathological feature common to both coupled harmonic oscillators and massless scalar fields. In this work, we demonstrate that this infinite growth is naturally resolved by invoking the Extended Uncertainty Principle (EUP), which introduces large-length-scale geometric corrections to the canonical commutation relations. By exactly solving the simple harmonic oscillator under the EUP framework, we establish the existence of an intrinsic geometric confinement that enforces a strict upper bound on the position variance, limits spatial delocalization, and introduces an intrinsic localization length scale related to the background Ricci scalar. We extend this regularizing mechanism to many-body systems by evaluating the entanglement entropy and entanglement spectrum of a one-dimensional harmonic chain and a massless scalar field. We show that the EUP-induced spatial bounds prevent the accumulation of low-lying long-wavelength modes, keeping the entanglement spectrum discrete and evenly gapped even in the strictly massless limit. This non-vanishing modular gap effectively caps the local entanglement temperature of the vacuum. Consequently, the entanglement entropy saturates to a finite value, providing a robust, geometric resolution to the zero-mode IR divergence problem in quantum field theory.

</details>


### [127] [The Milgromian acceleration and the cosmological constant from precanonical quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.05525)
*Igor V. Kanatchikov,Valery A. Kholodnyi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文表明MOND的Milgrom加速度和宇宙学常数可以被理解为自旋联络（spin connection）量子涨落的效应，该效应由前规范量子引力（precanonical quantum gravity）理论描述，并在非相对论近似下自然涌现出小加速度尺度下类似MOND的动力学修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图为MOND理论中的Milgrom加速度和宇宙学常数提供一个更深层的量子引力基础解释，将它们统一归因于自旋联络的量子涨落，从而在非相对论极限下导出MOND式的牛顿动力学修正。

Method: 采用作者此前提出的前规范量子引力框架，将Milgrom加速度和宇宙学常数视为自旋联络量子涨落的效应，并在非相对论近似下推导牛顿动力学的MOND型修正。

Result: 证明Milgrom加速度和宇宙学常数可以从自旋联络的量子涨落中量化导出，并在非相对论近似下涌现出小加速度区间的MOND式动力学修改。

Conclusion: 结论表明MOND现象和宇宙学常数可能具有共同的量子引力起源，即自旋联络的量子涨落，这为统一理解这两个观测现象提供了新视角。

Abstract: We show that the Milgromian acceleration of MOND and the cosmological constant can be understood and quantified as the effects of quantum fluctuations of spin connection which are described by precanonical quantum gravity put forward by one of us earlier. We also show that a MOND-like modification of Newtonian dynamics at small accelerations emerges from this picture in the non-relativistic approximation.

</details>


### [128] [Near--extremal gravitational collapse in 4+1 dimensions: Schwarzschild--de--Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27976)
*Maciej Dunajski,Sebastian J. Szybka*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究了4+1维纯爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）加正宇宙学常数（positive cosmological constant）框架下，由径向对称引力波引力坍缩形成近极端视界（near extremal horizons）的过程。结果显示，从具有宇宙视界的正则初始数据演化可形成质量超过极端值99%的黑洞，并给出证据表明黑洞热力学第三定律（third law of black hole thermodynamics）在宇宙学背景下可能不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究近极端黑洞视界的形成机制，探讨在正宇宙学常数背景下引力坍缩是否能无限接近极端黑洞状态，以及这如何影响黑洞热力学第三定律的适用性。

Method: 在4+1维纯爱因斯坦引力加正宇宙学常数的框架下，对径向对称引力波的引力坍缩进行数值研究，跟踪从具有宇宙视界的正则初始数据出发的演化过程，并将结果与特征拼接（characteristic gluing）框架进行对比。

Result: 数值演化显示，初始数据可演化形成质量超过极端值99%的黑洞，其中极端值对应于黑洞与宇宙视界重合的情形。研究发现黑洞热力学第三定律在宇宙学背景下可能不成立，因为极端性在此对应德西特空间中史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）的最大质量。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在正宇宙学常数背景下，引力坍缩可以非常接近（超过99%）极端黑洞的质量极限，但这一过程对黑洞热力学第三定律的适用性提出了质疑，可能需要重新审视该定律在宇宙学环境中的有效性。

Abstract: We numerically study a formation of near extremal horizons from a gravitational collapse of radially symmetric gravitational waves in $4+1$ dimensions within the framework of pure Einstein gravity with positive cosmological constant. Evolution of a regular initial data with cosmological horizon leads to a formation of a black hole with mass exceeding $99\%$ of the extremal value corresponding to the black hole and cosmological horizons coinciding. We demonstrate how our results fit within the framework of characteristic gluing, and present some evidence that the third law of black hole thermodynamics may not hold in the cosmological context, where the extremality corresponds to the maximal mass of the Schwarzschild black hole in de--Sitter space.

</details>

### [129] [Symmetry Rules for Cavity Materials Engineering with Linearly Polarized Vacuum Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28745)
*Jingkai Quan,Chongxiao Fan,Benshu Fan,I-Te Lu,Dante M. Kennes,Angel Rubio*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过群论分析（group-theoretical analysis）建立了腔材料工程（cavity materials engineering）中线性极化腔光子模式（linearly polarized cavity photon modes）下对称性破缺的一般性规则，对所有晶体学点群（crystallographic point groups）给出了完整分类，并用量子电动力学密度泛函理论（QED-DFT）计算验证了这一框架的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔材料工程旨在通过腔内的真空涨落（vacuum fluctuations）调节材料性质，但此前研究多聚焦于特定材料和特定腔结构，缺乏对对称性规则的普适理解，因此有必要建立一般性的对称性原理。

Method: 作者对无光子有效量子电动力学哈密顿量（effective photon-free quantum-electrodynamics Hamiltonian）进行群论对称性分析，针对所有晶体学点群给出了腔模式诱导的对称性破缺模式（symmetry-breaking patterns）完整分类，并通过量子电动力学密度泛函理论计算加以验证。

Result: 该框架成功解释了立方相BaTiO₃在不同腔模式配置下带简并度（band degeneracies）的腔诱导性解除差异，以及单层MoS₂因对称性破缺导致的腔修饰红外与拉曼光谱（infrared and Raman spectra）变化。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了对称性在腔材料工程中的核心作用，为未来相关研究提供了普适性指导原则（general guidelines）。

Abstract: Cavity materials engineering, aiming to manipulate material properties by coupling to vacuum fluctuations inside a cavity, is a rapidly advancing field. Despite significant progress, most studies to date have focused on specific materials and cavity configurations. Here, through a comprehensive group-theoretical analysis, we establish general symmetry rules for cavity materials engineering with linearly polarized cavity photon modes. By analyzing the symmetry of the effective photon-free quantum-electrodynamics Hamiltonian, we provide a complete classification of the symmetry-breaking patterns induced by cavity modes for all crystallographic point groups. The power of this framework is then demonstrated by quantum-electrodynamical density functional theory calculations. In particular, we explain the distinct cavity-induced lifting of band degeneracies in cubic BaTiO$_3$ for different cavity mode configurations, and the cavity-modified infrared and Raman spectra of monolayer MoS$_2$ due to symmetry breaking. Our results highlight the central role of symmetry in cavity materials engineering and provide general guidelines for future studies in this field.

</details>


### [130] [Local B-site chemistry controls oxygen-vacancy energetics in Ca-Ce-Ti-Mn perovskites for thermochemical hydrogen production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28752)
*Manish Kumar,Natalia Ali,Matthew D. Witman,Shang Zhai,James E. Miller,Ivan Ermanoski,Ellen B. Stechel,Robert B. Wexler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算、覆盖约束特殊准随机结构（coverage-constrained special quasirandom structure）方法、可解释晶体特征模型和微调缺陷图神经网络，系统映射了Ca-Ce-Ti-Mn（CCTM）钙钛矿氧化物中氧空位形成能（oxygen-vacancy formation energy）的格局。研究发现B位最近邻Mn比例主导氧空位形成能（变化1.0-1.5 eV），而A位Ce贡献较小（0.2-0.6 eV），并识别出一个Ce/Mn平衡的成分区域，其预测氧化还原循环容量在1350 °C下即可达到或超过需约1600 °C的二氧化铈（ceria）基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两步式太阳能热化学分解水制氢需要氧化物在氧空位形成能上实现易还原与有利再氧化的平衡，钙钛矿固溶体可调谐这一平衡，但体相化学计量（bulk stoichiometry）与局域缺陷热力学能量学（local defect energetics）之间的关系尚不清楚，亟需系统阐明。

Method: 综合运用三种方法：（1）第一性原理计算与覆盖约束特殊准随机结构方法相结合，实现全部十五种对称性不同的氧最近邻环境；（2）可解释晶体特征模型，其拟合系数直接编码Born-Haber热化学（Born-Haber thermochemistry）；（3）微调缺陷图神经网络（defect graph neural network）用于预测氧空位形成能。

Result: 局域B位化学主导氧空位形成能E_v：改变最近邻Mn比例可使E_v偏移1.0-1.5 eV（取决于局域Ce含量），而A位Ce变化产生较小且依赖于Mn的0.2-0.6 eV偏移。成分空间图谱识别出Ce/Mn平衡区域（X_Ce=0.29-0.33，X_Mn=0.58-0.67），该区域在1350 °C下预测的氧化还原循环容量达到或超过需要约1600 °C的ceria基准。三个CCTM成分的实验测量显示循环容量随Ce含量单调增加。

Conclusion: 短程B位阳离子有序（short-range B-site cation order）若能在加工过程中建立并动力学保持，可成为不改变整体体相成分而调谐氧化还原性能的候选途径。所提出的设计规则预期可迁移至相关钙钛矿家族。

Abstract: Two-step thermochemical water splitting driven by concentrated solar heat is a scalable route to renewable hydrogen, but it requires oxides whose oxygen-vacancy formation energies balance facile reduction with favorable reoxidation. Perovskite solid solutions can tune this balance, but the relationship between bulk stoichiometry and local defect energetics remains poorly understood. Here we map oxygen-vacancy formation energetics across Ca-Ce-Ti-Mn (CCTM) perovskites by combining first-principles calculations with a coverage-constrained special quasirandom structure approach that realizes all fifteen symmetry-distinct oxygen nearest-neighbor environments, an interpretable crystal-feature model whose fitted coefficients directly encode the underlying Born-Haber thermochemistry, and a fine-tuned defect graph neural network. Local B-site chemistry dominates the oxygen-vacancy formation energy $E_\mathrm{v}$: varying the nearest-neighbor Mn fraction shifts $E_\mathrm{v}$ by 1.0-1.5 eV depending on local Ce content, whereas A-site Ce variation contributes a smaller, Mn-dependent shift of 0.2-0.6 eV. Short-range B-site cation order, if it can be established and kinetically retained through processing, is therefore a candidate means of tuning redox performance without changing bulk composition. Composition-space maps identify a Ce/Mn-balanced region ($X_\mathrm{Ce}$ = 0.29-0.33, $X_\mathrm{Mn}$ = 0.58-0.67) combining a high fraction of vacancy sites within the targeted $E_\mathrm{v}$ window with phase stability and solubility, whose predicted redox cycle capacity matches or exceeds the ceria benchmark at 1350 $^\circ$C rather than the roughly 1600 $^\circ$C ceria requires. Measurements on three CCTM compositions show cycle capacity increasing monotonically with Ce content under protocols close to the model conditions. The design rules are expected to transfer to related perovskite families.

</details>


### [131] [Electrical Control over Volatile Mott Switching through Non-Volatile Memory Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28787)
*Amihai Kronman,Gil Levi,Yoav Kalcheim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了VO2和V2O3两种Mott绝缘体在绝缘体-金属转变（Insulator-to-Metal Transition, IMT）诱导的易失性电阻开关（Volatile Resistive Switching, VRS）中表现出的记忆效应，识别了两种不同的物理机制，并提出利用保护电阻和多步写入协议来调控开关参数的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VO2和V2O3作为Mott绝缘体在特定温度下发生IMT，电阻骤降数个数量级，这使其成为阈值开关器件（threshold-switching devices）的有前景候选材料。然而，易失性开关行为可能受到与导电细丝（conducting filament）生成和湮灭相关的非易失性过程影响，产生记忆效应，理解并控制这些效应是实现可靠器件应用的关键。

Method: 作者通过实验研究两种材料在不同热区域（thermal regimes）下开关过程中的电压-电流特性，区分了相共存区（phase-coexistence regime）和磁滞区以下（below the hysteresis regime）两种温度范围中的行为。他们使用保护性内部电阻（protective internal resistor）和多步写入协议（multi-step writing protocol）来调控通常具有破坏性的电致成形（electroforming-like）过程。

Result: 实验发现两种不同的记忆效应机制：在相共存区，V2O3表现出由金属-绝缘体域空间再分布引起的记忆效应；在磁滞区以下，VO2和V2O3均表现出电致成形类记忆效应，归因于高电场和IMT驱动开关引起的电流冲击（current surge）所导致的缺陷形成和/或迁移。通过保护电阻和多步写入协议，可以在宽温度范围内调控开关电压和功率。

Conclusion: 研究证明可以通过控制非易失性过程来实现对IMT基电阻开关器件中开关参数的编程调控，为开发具有可控阈值开关性能的器件提供了新途径。

Abstract: Vanadium Dioxide (VO2) and Vanadium Sesquioxide (V2O3) are Mott insulators that undergo an Insulator-to-Metal Transition (IMT) at ~340K and ~160K, respectively, manifested as an orders-of-magnitude reduction in their electrical resistance. These transitions provide the physical basis for their Volatile Resistive Switching (VRS) behavior, making them promising candidates for threshold-switching devices. Here, we show that the volatile switching in these materials can be strongly affected by non-volatile processes associated with the creation and annihilation of the conducting filament. These processes give rise to pronounced memory effects in which the initial switching voltage substantially exceeds that of subsequent cycles. We identify two distinct mechanisms underlying this behavior in different thermal regimes. In the phase-coexistence regime, a memory effect is observed in V2O3, arising from the spatial redistribution of metallic and insulating domains. Well below the hysteresis regime, an additional electroforming-like memory effect is observed in both VO2 and V2O3 which is attributed to the formation and/or migration of defects under the influence of a high electric field and a current surge associated with the IMT-driven switching. Using a protective internal resistor and a multi-step writing protocol, this normally destructive process can be harnessed to tune the switching voltage and power over a wide temperature range. These results demonstrate a route toward controlled programming of switching parameters in IMT-based resistive switching devices.

</details>


### [132] [Open-Shell Molecules as Sensitive Probes of Spin-Orbit-Controlled Surface Electronic Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28797)
*Solange Mariel Di Napoli,Alexei Nefedov,María Andrea Barral,Gustavo E. Murgida,Ana María Llois,Christof Wöll,M. Verónica Ganduglia-Pirovano,Verónica Laura Vildosola*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过结合红外反射吸收光谱（infrared reflection-absorption spectroscopy）与包含非共线磁性和自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的杂化密度泛函理论（hybrid density functional theory），证明自旋轨道耦合对准确描述NO在UO₂(111)表面上的振动谱至关重要，并将该体系确立为描述开壳层分子-表面相互作用的电子结构方法的严格基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开壳层分子（open-shell molecules）因部分占据的前沿轨道能强烈放大吸附质-底物杂化（adsorbate-substrate hybridization）的细微变化，因此是探测相关氧化物表面（correlated oxide surfaces）的高度灵敏探针。理解自旋轨道耦合如何调控这类相互作用对于准确描述表面电子结构至关重要。

Method: 将红外反射吸收光谱（infrared reflection-absorption spectroscopy）与包含非共线磁性（non-collinear magnetism）及自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的杂化密度泛函理论（hybrid density functional theory）相结合，对NO吸附于UO₂(111)表面的振动谱进行实验测量与理论模拟。

Result: 研究表明，自旋轨道耦合的纳入对于重现NO在UO₂(111)上的振动谱是必不可少的，因为它抑制了NO前沿轨道与铀5f态之间虚假的过度杂化（artificial overhybridization）。自旋轨道耦合决定了N-O伸缩振动峰的位置，而色散驱动的分子间相互作用（dispersion-driven intermolecular interactions）则导致其在单层覆盖下出现不对称展宽。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了开壳层分子作为探测自旋轨道控制的表面电子结构的灵敏探针，并将NO/UO₂(111)体系鉴定为评价描述开壳层分子-表面相互作用电子结构方法的严格基准（stringent benchmark）。

Abstract: Open-shell molecules are highly sensitive probes of correlated oxide surfaces because their partially occupied frontier orbitals strongly amplify subtle changes in adsorbate-substrate hybridization. Combining infrared reflection-absorption spectroscopy with hybrid density functional theory including non-collinear magnetism and spin-orbit coupling, we show that inclusion of spin-orbit coupling is essential to reproduce the vibrational spectrum of NO adsorbed on UO$_2$(111) by suppressing an otherwise artificial overhybridization between the NO frontier orbitals and uranium 5f states. While spin-orbit coupling determines the position of the N-O stretching and, dispersion-driven intermolecular interactions account for its asymmetric broadening at monolayer coverage. These results establish open-shell molecules as sensitive probes of spin-orbit-controlled surface electronic structure and identify NO/UO$_2$(111) as a stringent benchmark for electronic-structure methods describing open-shell molecule-surface interactions.

</details>


### [133] [Giant Exfoliation Induced Magnetic Coercivity in Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28828)
*Lingrui Mei,PeiYu Cai,Sang-Eon Lee,Yue Li,Shyam Raj Karullithodi,Vadym Kulichenko,Charudatta Pathak,Elton J. G. Santos,Luis Balicas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过机械剥离实现对范德华铁磁体Fe₃GaTe₂的厚度调控，当材料减薄至100纳米以下时，其矫顽力显著增强，室温下平面内矫顽场接近1特斯拉，与常规硬磁材料相当，使其成为有前景的无稀土硬磁材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永磁体是现代信息与能源技术的支柱，但高各向异性、高矫顽力的无稀土替代材料仍然有限，亟需探索新型无稀土硬磁材料。

Method: 采用机械剥离对范德华铁磁体Fe₃GaTe₂进行厚度工程调控，并结合微磁学分析（micromagnetic analysis），研究厚度变化对磁化反转机制和磁硬度的调控作用。

Result: 块体Fe₃GaTe₂居里温度高于350K但室温矫顽力可忽略；当厚度减至100nm以下时，矫顽场显著增强，室温平面内矫顽场接近1T。微磁学分析显示磁化反转机制由块体的畴介导过程转变为薄片的准相干旋转，源于有效各向异性增强和畴形成受抑制。

Conclusion: Fe₃GaTe₂通过厚度调控实现无需化学改性的磁硬度可调性，结合高饱和磁化强度和稳健的室温性能，成为有前景的无稀土自旋电子学材料，其层状结构还可集成于范德华异质结构中，用于高密度非易失性存储和畴壁器件。

Abstract: Permanent magnets with strong anisotropy and high coercivity underpin modern information and energy technologies, yet rare-earth-free alternatives remain limited. Here, we show that thickness engineering via mechanical exfoliation induces hard magnetic behavior in the van der Waals ferromagnet Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$. Bulk crystals exhibit Curie temperatures above 350 K but negligible room-temperature coercivity. When thinned below 100 nm, the coercive field is dramatically enhanced, reaching nearly 1 T at room temperature for in-plane fields which is comparable to values of conventional hard magnets. Micromagnetic analysis reveals a crossover in magnetization reversal from domain-mediated processes in bulk samples to quasi-coherent rotation in thin flakes, driven by increased effective anisotropy and suppressed domain formation. This thickness-dependent transition enables tuning of magnetic hardness without chemical modification. Combined with high saturation magnetization and robust room-temperature performance, Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ emerges as a promising rare-earth-free material for spintronic applications. Its layered structure further allows integration into van der Waals heterostructures, where large in-plane coercivity can stabilize magnetic states against perturbations and interlayer coupling, offering potential for high-density nonvolatile memory and domain-wall-based devices.

</details>


### [134] [Strength-degradation phase-field regularization of cohesive fracture: the antiplane case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29157)
*Blaise Bourdin,Corrado Maurini*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种反平面（antiplane）相场断裂模型，该模型通过降解强度而非刚度来实现任意的凸强度面（convex strength surface）控制裂纹扩展，被解释为软化塑性（softening plasticity）的正则化，其中强度、刚度和韧度成为相互独立的材料数据，统一了极限分析、完美塑性、黏聚断裂和脆性断裂于单一变分框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场断裂（phase-field fracture）方法最初作为Griffith脆断裂模型的正则化设计，但现被普遍视为梯度损伤模型，其正则化长度成为驱动裂纹成核（crack nucleation）的材料属性。该方法的弱点在于强度面不能任意设定：其形状由弹性能决定，量级由正则化长度决定，这限制了模型的适用性。

Method: 本文聚焦于Bourdin、Marigo、Maurini和Zolesi提出的反平面相场模型，该模型通过降解强度而非刚度来处理沿未知路径的裂纹扩展以及由任意凸强度面控制的成核。从理论层面，该模型被解释为软化塑性的正则化，其中局部化带（localization bands）遵循由强度域和韧度设定的等效黏聚法则（cohesive law）；从数值层面，提出了结合交替最小化（alternate minimization）和锥规划（conic programming）的数值方案。通过简单剪切问题的闭式解、"冲浪"（surfing）模拟和再入V型缺口（re-entrant V-notch）算例验证模型行为。

Result: 推导了简单剪切问题的闭式解，数值验证了等效黏聚法则及其对正则化长度的独立性，并确认了由弹黏聚长度（elasto-cohesive length）控制的尺寸效应。"冲浪"模拟揭示了扩展裂纹的结构，V型缺口算例展示了模型无需先验假设即可桥接小尺度屈服、黏聚断裂和脆性断裂。

Conclusion: 当正则化长度远小于弹黏聚长度时，其作用纯粹是数值性的；强度、刚度和韧度成为独立材料参数，极限分析、完美塑性、黏聚断裂与脆性断裂统一于同一变分框架中。

Abstract: Phase-field approaches to fracture, initially designed as regularization of the Griffith model of brittle fracture, are now commonly viewed as gradient-damage models whose regularization length becomes a material property driving crack nucleation. One weakness of this approach is that the strength surface cannot be arbitrary: its shape is dictated by the elastic energy, and its magnitude by the regularization length. We focus on the antiplane version of the model introduced by Bourdin, Marigo, Maurini and Zolesi (arXiv:2506.22558), which handles crack propagation along unknown paths and nucleation governed by an arbitrary convex strength surface by degrading the strength instead of the stiffness. It can be interpreted as a regularization of softening plasticity in which localization bands obey an equivalent cohesive law set by the strength domain and the toughness, while the role of the regularization length, when small compared to the elasto-cohesive length, is purely numerical. Strength, stiffness, and toughness thus become independent material data, and limit analysis, perfect plasticity, cohesive fracture, and brittle fracture merge into a single variational framework. We derive closed-form solutions for a simple shear problem, propose a numerical scheme combining alternate minimization and conic programming, and numerically verify the equivalent cohesive law, its independence of the regularization, and the size effect governed by the elasto-cohesive length. A "surfing" simulation highlights the structure of the propagating crack while a re-entrant V-notch is used to show how the model bridges small-scale yielding, cohesive fracture, and brittle fracture without a priori hypotheses.

</details>


### [135] [Ultrafast Nonthermal Lattice Destabilization and Suppression of Polar Optical Scattering in Electronically Excited $α$-SiO$_2$ from First-Principles and Deep Neural Network Potential Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28838)
*Iyyappa Rajan Panneerselvam,Mark Yeung,Charlotte Palmer,Brendan Dromey,Lorenzo Stella*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种多尺度"第一性原理到机器学习"（first-principles-to-machine-learning）方法，用于研究电子激发态α-SiO₂中的超快晶格动力学。通过训练依赖电子温度的深度神经网络势（DNNPs），实现了对数千原子大体系的近DFT精度模拟，揭示了电子温度升高导致的晶格失稳、极性到非极性相变以及非平衡热化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子激发态下的超快晶格动力学对理解材料在强激光或高能辐射下的行为至关重要，但第一性原理分子动力学（AIMD）受限于体系规模，难以直接模拟包含数千原子的大体系。作者旨在开发一种兼具DFT精度与大规模模拟能力的方法，以系统研究电子温度升高对α-SiO₂结构和动力学性质的影响。

Method: 作者采用基于电子温度依赖的密度泛函理论（DFT）进行AIMD模拟，以此为训练数据构建电子温度依赖的深度神经网络势（DNNPs）。利用DNNPs对包含数千个原子的α-SiO₂大晶胞进行近DFT精度的原子级建模，获得声子能带结构、分子动力学轨迹以及Fröhlich耦合常数等物理量，并结合Bader电荷分析和弹性稳定性判据进行综合分析。

Result: 研究发现随着电子温度T_e升高，α-SiO₂出现显著的晶格失稳，表现为弹性稳定性判据破坏、体积大幅膨胀、体模量急剧降低以及反键态占据导致的Si-O键减弱。Fröhlich耦合常数随T_e升高而减小，提示向非极性相的交叉，Bader电荷分析对此提供佐证。此外，大晶胞DNNP-MD模拟显示，在T_e升高后的前数百飞秒内体系未达到麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布定义的热平衡，动能温度呈非单调驰豫；当T_e升至2.6 eV时，Si和O原子先分别在不同温度下各自平衡，暗示出现原子流体相。

Conclusion: 本研究为电子激发态下的大规模超快晶格动力学模拟提供了有效途径，揭示了α-SiO₂在高温电子激发下经历的极性-非极性相变和原子流体相变，并指出T_e>2 eV时极性光学声子散射被强烈抑制。这些发现与近期实验和理论结果一致，加深了对电子激发诱导材料结构演化的理解。

Abstract: We present a multiscale first-principles-to-machine-learning approach to investigate ultrafast lattice dynamics in electronically excited $α$-SiO$_2$. Ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) based on electronic-temperature-dependent density functional theory (DFT) are used to train electronic-temperature-dependent deep neural network potentials (DNNPs). The use of DNNPs enables atomistic modeling at near-DFT accuracy of large $α$-SiO$_2$ cells with thousands of atoms. In particular, DNNPs allowed us to obtain accurate phonon band structures and molecular dynamics (MD) of $α$-SiO$_2$ excited by a sudden increase in electronic temperature. With increasing electronic temperature, $T_e$, pronounced lattice destabilization of $α$-SiO$_2$ is found, as evidenced by violations of elastic stability criteria, substantial volumetric expansion, a sharp reduction of the bulk modulus, and progressive weakening of Si-O bonding due to antibonding-state occupation. From the electronic and phonon band structures, we estimated the Frohlich coupling constant, which decreases as $T_e$ increases, suggesting a crossover to a nonpolar phase of $α$-SiO$_2$ at elevated electronic temperature. This is corroborated by the Bader charge analysis. We also suggest that polar optical phonon scattering should be strongly suppressed at $T_e > 2$ eV. From large-cell DNNP-MD simulations, we show that a well-defined thermal equilibrium, as defined by the Maxwell-Boltzmann distribution, is not achieved over the first few hundred femtoseconds. This behavior explains the non-monotonic equilibration of the kinetic temperature after a sudden rise of $T_e$. After $T_e$ is raised to 2.6 eV, Si and O atoms first equilibrate separately at two different temperatures, suggesting an atomic fluid phase, in agreement with recent experimental and theoretical findings.

</details>


### [136] [An End-to-End Differentiable Forward Model for High-Energy Diffraction Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28843)
*Hemant Sharma,Nina Andrejevic,Simon Zhang,Mathew Cherukara*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文首次提出端到端可微的高能衍射显微镜（HEDM）前向模型，覆盖远场（FF）、近场（NF）和点聚焦（pf）三种几何构型，以PyTorch实现并与MIDAS参考模拟器逐像素吻合。通过联合取向-应变-位置恢复、真实α-Ti数据集的往返精修以及四面板几何参数联合精修三项演示验证了该框架，并以开源包midas-diffract形式发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FF、NF和pf-HEDM前向模型不可微，无法进行基于梯度的联合参数精修、物理信息正则化（physics-informed regularisation）和贝叶斯不确定性量化（Bayesian uncertainty quantification），限制了重建精度和可靠性。

Method: 在PyTorch中实现首个端到端可微的HEDM前向模型，覆盖FF、NF和pf三种几何构型，与已建立的MIDAS参考模拟器进行逐像素级一致性验证（含探测器倾斜扫描）。

Result: 模型与MIDAS在FF 162/162、NF 2304/2304（含非零探测器倾角扫描）及pf-HEDM 1088/1096个光斑上匹配。NF-HEDM联合恢复达到约6纳米位置精度；在真实214晶粒α-Ti FF-HEDM数据集上实现100%晶粒恢复，残差与生产拟合一致至0.3%；四面板FF-HEDM联合精修中，面板绕束轴旋转恢复至约10微弧度，全局旋转轴楔角恢复至约26微弧度。

Conclusion: 该可微框架为HEDM的梯度下降优化、正则化与不确定性量化开辟了路径，并以开源包midas-diffract（pip install midas-diffract）形式发布。

Abstract: High-Energy Diffraction Microscopy (HEDM) recovers crystallographic orientation, strain, and grain position from rotating-crystal X-ray diffraction patterns. Existing forward models in far-field (FF), near-field (NF), and point-focused (pf) HEDM are not differentiable, which forecloses gradient-based joint parameter refinement, physics-informed regularisation, and Bayesian uncertainty quantification. We present the first end-to-end differentiable HEDM forward model covering all three geometries, implemented in PyTorch with pixel-exact agreement against the established MIDAS reference simulators (162/162 FF, 2304/2304 NF including a non-zero detector-tilt sweep, and 1088/1096 pf-HEDM spots matched). Three demonstrations validate the framework: joint orientation-strain-position recovery in NF-HEDM at ~6 nm precision; round-trip refinement on a real 214-grain alpha-Ti FF-HEDM dataset reaching 100% grain recovery from a 1.5 degree initial perturbation with residuals matching the production fit to 0.3%; and joint refinement of all per-detector geometry parameters and per-grain state on a synthetic four-panel FF-HEDM setup, recovering panel rotations about the beam axis to ~10 mu-rad and a global rotation-axis wedge to ~26 mu-rad. The framework is released as the open-source midas-diffract package (pip install midas-diffract).

</details>


### [137] [Automating the analysis of micron-scale synchrotron diffraction data on inhomogeneous polycrystalline samples: a solid oxide electrolysis cell case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28859)
*Liam A. V. Nagle-Cocco,Christopher A. Crain,Michael J. Dzara,Mathias A. Kiefer,Madeline G. Port,Tolga Han Ulucan,Madeline Van Winkle,Oscar Hathaway,Nicholas A. Strange*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全自动、开源的Python分析流程，用于对微米级空间分辨X射线衍射（μ-XRD）获得的二维衍射图数据集进行物相鉴定（phase identification）和Rietveld精修（Rietveld refinement），并以固体氧化物电解池（SOEC）截面为案例验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电化学与光伏器件的失效后表征中，μ-XRD（微米级X射线衍射）虽能揭示降解产物与应变的空间分布，但数据规模庞大、物相组成随位置剧烈变化、多相共存且降解产物难以标定，导致全自动Rietveld分析在文献中极少实现，亟需自动化的数据分析方案。

Method: 作者开发了一套完全自动化的开源Python程序，用于对在微米尺度位置采集、跨越化学不均匀多晶器件截面的二维衍射图数据集执行物相鉴定与Rietveld分析，并在涵盖空气电极、阳离子扩散阻挡层、电解质和燃料电极的SOEC碎片上进行了案例验证。

Result: 该自动化分析协议成功应用于SOEC截面的μ-XRD数据，实现了多物相系统的物相识别与结构精修。该协议具有一定通用性，可通过修改推广至全固态电池、湿电解质电池电极、固体氧化物燃料电池、光伏器件及金属氧化物赝电容器等其他器件。

Conclusion: 该全自动开源Python流程有效解决了μ-XRD数据分析中数据量大、物相复杂、降解产物难标定的核心难题，为多种电化学和光伏器件的空间分辨失效表征提供了可扩展的分析框架。

Abstract: A novel approach to post mortem characterisation of electrochemical and photovoltaic devices is spatially-resolved diffraction using a hyper-focused, micron-width x-ray beam to examine the distribution of degradation products and strain, a technique called $μ$-XRD. Aside from the experimental difficulties associated with beam focusing and sample preparation, which are themselves non-trivial, the analysis of resulting data is complex and challenging, with full Rietveld analysis rarely attempted in literature. The difficulty lies in the size of the data, which may consist of hundreds or even thousands of diffraction patterns with very different crystallographic phase compositions depending on position within the device, and the difficulty in fitting the data due to the presence of many phases at the same position, including possible degradation products which may be difficult to index and assign to known phases. In this paper, we present a fully-automated open access Python routine for performing phase identification and Rietveld analysis on 2D datasets of diffraction pattern taken at micron-scale positions, measured over the cross-section of a chemically inhomogeneous device with polycrystalline phases. Solid oxide electrolyser cells are a promising technology for green hydrogen production which can utilise waste heat to split water at higher efficiencies than low-temperature electrolysis techniques such as polymer electrolyte membranes, but exhibit many degradation modes due to the high operating temperatures. We present a case study using our analysis protocol on an SOEC fragment encompassing the air electrode, cation diffusion barrier, electrolyte, and fuel electrode. With modification, this protocol could be applied to other devices such as all-solid-state batteries, wet-electrolyte battery electrodes, solid oxide fuel cells, photovoltaic devices, and metal-oxide pseudocapacitors.

</details>


### [138] [Knudsen-Controlled Switching of Thermal Conductivity Response by Targeted Phonon Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28905)
*Shixian Liu,Tianhao Li,Fei Yin,Yu He,Alexander A. Barinov,Han Meng,Nuo Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，用于预测频谱选择性非平衡声子（phonon）激发对热导率的影响方向。通过结合第一性原理三声子散射率和声子追踪蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟，研究发现块体体系中激发增强散射主导导致热导率被抑制，而纳米薄膜中低频激发可增强准弹道热导通道贡献从而提升热导率。基于归一化目标频率和克努森数（Knudsen number, Kn）构建的频率–克努森图（frequency–Knudsen map）统一组织了这些相反响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 靶向声子激发被认为是动态调控热传导的可行途径，但缺乏通用原理来预测频谱选择性非平衡声子群将增强还是抑制热输运。作者旨在建立一个能够基于系统物理参数（目标频率和克努森数）统一预测热导率响应方向的普适框架。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算三声子散射率（three-phonon scattering rates），并结合声子追踪蒙特卡洛（phonon-tracking Monte Carlo）模拟，对从块体晶体到受限纳米薄膜（nanofilm）的 Ge、Si 和 3C–SiC 体系分别进行研究，分析不同频率激励下的热导率变化。

Result: 块体体系中，激发增强的本征散射占主导，热导率主要被抑制；纳米薄膜中，低频激发可增强长平均自由程声子的准弹道（quasi-ballistic）贡献并提升热导率，而高频激发则主要起抑制作用。这些相反响应用于归一化目标频率 ωt/ωD 与克努森数 Kn 构成的频率–克努森图可系统分类。

Conclusion: 构建的频率–克努森图为预测非平衡热输运响应提供了通用的物理基础，超越了静态声子工程（static phonon engineering）的范畴，为动态调控热传导提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Targeted phonon excitation offers a route to dynamically control heat conduction, yet no general principle predicts whether a spectrally selective nonequilibrium phonon population will enhance or suppress thermal transport. A Knudsen-controlled competition between the increased contribution of long-mean-free-path phonons and excitation-enhanced intrinsic scattering governs the sign of the thermal-conductivity response. First-principles three-phonon scattering rates combined with phonon-tracking Monte Carlo simulations are used to examine Ge, Si, and 3C--SiC from bulk crystals to confined nanofilms. In bulk systems, excitation-enhanced scattering dominates and thermal conductivity is predominantly suppressed. In nanofilms, by contrast, low-frequency excitation can increase the contribution of quasi-ballistic heat-carrying channels and enhance thermal conductivity, whereas higher-frequency excitation is predominantly suppressive. At fixed background temperature and excitation strength, these opposite responses are organized in a frequency--Knudsen map based on the normalized target frequency, $ω_{\mathrm t}/ω_{\mathrm D}$, and the Knudsen number, $\mathrm{Kn}$. The resulting framework provides a general physical basis for controlling nonequilibrium heat transport beyond static phonon engineering.

</details>


### [139] [Molecular beam epitaxy synthesis of ternary nitride PrTaN$_2$ and its crystal structure determination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28909)
*Kosuke Takiguchi,Yoshiharu Krockenberger,Eisuke Magome,Yoji Kunihashi,Hideki Yamamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）技术合成新型三元氮化物PrTaN₂薄膜，并通过基于结构因子的拟合方法确定了其正交晶系P222空间群结构，展示了薄膜生长途径在探索新型复杂氮化物材料方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元氮化物在块体合成中难以获得，尚未被充分探索。作者旨在利用薄膜外延技术稳定此前未知的三元氮化物相，并解决从有限薄膜衍射数据中确定晶体结构的难题。

Method: 采用分子束外延技术，结合电子束蒸发难熔元素与射频氮自由基源实现高氮化环境生长氮化物薄膜；通过X射线衍射和高角环形暗场扫描透射电子显微镜进行结构表征；开发了基于结构因子的拟合程序，结合消光规则与Wyckoff位置约束减少拟合参数，从而确定晶体结构。

Result: 成功合成PrTaN₂薄膜，该化合物结晶为正交晶系，在YAlO₃衬底上生长取向明确且基本无应变；通过系统排除Pr-Ta-N体系中其他候选相并结合结构因子拟合，确定其空间群为P222并得到了原子坐标。

Conclusion: 分子束外延薄膜生长可以稳定此前未探索的三元氮化物相，并建立了一种实用的结构确定方法，为新型复杂氮化物材料的探索提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We report the discovery of a novel ternary nitride PrTaN$_2$ synthesized as a thin film using molecular beam epitaxy. The combination of e-beam evaporation for refractory elements and a radio-frequency nitrogen radical source enables growth under a highly nitriding environment, providing access to phases not readily obtained in bulk synthesis. Structural characterization by X-ray diffraction and high-angle annular dark-field scanning transmission electron microscopy reveals that the compound crystallizes in an orthorhombic structure and grows with a well-defined orientation on YAlO$_3$ substrates, while remaining essentially strain-free. To determine the crystal structure from limited thin-film diffraction data, we developed a fitting procedure based on structure factors. By combining extinction rules with constraints from Wyckoff positions, the number of fitting parameters is significantly reduced, enabling reliable structure determination. Systematic exclusion of alternative candidate phases in the Pr-Ta-N system, together with structure factor fitting, identifies the space group as $P222$ and determines the atomic coordinates. The present results demonstrate that thin-film growth with molecular beam epitaxy can stabilize previously unexplored ternary nitrides, and establish a practical approach for structural determination in such systems. This work provides a pathway for the exploration of new complex nitride materials.

</details>


### [140] [Mechanism-Dependent Descriptors Enable Predictive Design of Oxygen Capacity in Perovskite Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29014)
*Shuiping Gong,Yi Li,Jie Su,Zhenhao Zhou,Xiaobo Liao,Jian Deng,Chris Wolverton,Tao Deng,Jiangang He,Runxia Cai,Chaochao Dun,Zhenpeng Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合实验与第一性原理计算，在模型钙钛矿系列LnxSr1-xCoO3中建立了组分与氧容量之间的关系。研究发现，Sr2+含量的增加通过Co4+/Co3+/Co2+阳离子氧化还原增强氧容量；然而在稀土系列中，氧容量从La到Lu递减，尽管氧空位形成能逐步降低。这一反直觉行为源于氧释放过程中晶格氧被部分氧化为O1-类物种的替代电荷补偿路径。当阴离子氧化还原占主导时，由积分晶体轨道哈密顿布居量（ICOHP）量化的平均金属-氧键强度成为氧容量的物理意义描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿氧化物（perovskite oxides）可逆地容纳氧化学计量比变化的能力使其对于化学链（chemical looping）和储氧等清洁能源技术具有吸引力，但跨多样组分预测氧容量的描述符仍处于发展阶段，亟需建立组成-氧容量关系的普适性预测框架。

Method: 结合实验测量与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），系统研究模型钙钛矿系列LnxSr1-xCoO3中组分变化对氧容量及电荷补偿机制的影响，并通过积分晶体轨道哈密顿布居量（integrated crystal orbital Hamilton population, ICOHP）量化平均金属-氧键强度。

Result: Sr2+含量增加促进高价位Co4+形成，扩大氧释放时可用的阳离子氧化还原库从而增强氧容量；但在稀土系列中，氧容量从La到Lu递减。重稀土组分（Tb-Lu）通过独特的局域键合环境优先稳定氧空穴（oxygen-hole）物种，电荷补偿同时涉及被氧化的晶格氧以及被还原的稀土和钴阳离子，从而抑制净氧释放。

Conclusion: 氧空位形成能仅在阳离子氧化还原主导的机制下能捕捉氧容量趋势；当阴离子氧化还原（anionic redox）占主导时，平均金属-氧键强度成为更具物理意义的氧容量描述符，为钙钛矿储氧材料的合理设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Perovskite oxides can reversibly accommodate substantial changes in oxygen stoichiometry, making them attractive for clean-energy technologies including chemical looping and oxygen storage. Despite extensive efforts to optimize their redox properties, predictive descriptors capable of assessing oxygen capacity across diverse compositions remain under development. Here, we combine experiments and first-principles calculations to establish composition and oxygen-capacity relationships in the model perovskite series LnxSr1-xCoO3. We confirm that increasing Sr2+ content promotes the formation of high-valence Co4+, expanding the cationic redox reservoir available during oxygen release and thereby enhancing oxygen capacity. In this regime, oxygen-vacancy formation energy captures the observed trend because oxygen release is primarily compensated by Co4+/Co3+/Co2+ redox. Across the rare-earth series, however, oxygen capacity decreases from La to Lu despite progressively lower oxygen-vacancy formation energies. We reveal that this counterintuitive behavior originates from an alternative charge-compensation pathway, in which lattice oxygen is partially oxidized to O1- -like species during oxygen removal. Heavy rare-earth compositions (Tb-Lu) preferentially stabilize these oxygen-hole species through distinct local bonding environments, with charge compensation involving both oxidized lattice oxygen and reduced rare-earth and cobalt cations, thereby suppressing net oxygen release despite favorable vacancy thermodynamics. We further identify average metal-oxygen bond strength, quantified by integrated crystal orbital Hamilton population, as a physically meaningful descriptor for oxygen capacity when anionic redox becomes dominant.

</details>


### [141] [One-Step Epitaxial Access to Rhombohedral Graphene Flat-Band States on Step-Bunched SiC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29022)
*Hao Zhong,Xingzhe Wang,Hanbin Deng,Tianyu Yang,Haixuan Cao,Renzhe Li,Qiang Wan,Shangkun Mo,Keming Zhao,Shuming Yu,Dingkun Qin,Guang Zhu,Yifan Zhou,Jianping Shi,Shuangfeng Jia,He Zheng,Jia-Xin Yin,Nan Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种在4°偏轴4H-SiC衬底上通过一步石墨化工艺制备菱方（rhombohedral）多层石墨烯的方法，利用高温闪蒸退火同步驱动台阶聚束和石墨烯生长，厚度可从双层调谐至二十多层，并在厚膜中观测到近费米能级的平带（flat band）态及相关电子态特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱方石墨烯多层结构提供了无摩尔纹（moiré-free）的相关性和拓扑平带物理平台，但缺乏直接、免转移的外延途径来获得厚度可调的薄膜。作者希望开发一种简单的一步法方案，在无需转移的情况下实现厚度可调的菱方石墨烯外延生长。

Method: 在4°偏轴4H-SiC衬底上进行高温闪蒸退火，一步实现自组织的台阶聚束与多层石墨烯形成；利用原子分辨截面扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）确认局部ABC堆叠并区分菱方与Bernal堆叠；通过改变退火温度这一单一参数调控薄膜厚度；结合角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）、低温扫描隧道显微镜（STM）和扫描隧道谱（STS）表征电子结构。

Result: 石墨烯厚度可通过退火温度从双层调谐至二十层以上；ARPES显示厚度依赖的电子结构从界面主导的低能态向厚膜中近费米能级显著的平带谱权重演化；17层薄膜的低温STM/STS观测到13.4 meV的低能谱重构及√3×√3 Kekulé型调制，与谷间混合电子织构的一致微观特征相符。

Conclusion: 这一一步式、免转移的方法确立了台阶聚束SiC作为外延平台的地位，将堆叠工程与无摩尔纹的相关性平带电子态联系起来，为堆叠可调的相关物理研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: Rhombohedral graphene multilayers provide a moiré-free platform for correlated and topological flat-band physics, but direct, transfer-free epitaxial access to thickness-tunable multilayers remains limited. Here we report a one-step graphitization route on 4$^\circ$ off-axis 4H-SiC, in which high-temperature flash annealing simultaneously drives self-organized step bunching and multilayer graphene formation. Atomic-resolution cross-sectional scanning transmission electron microscopy identify local ABC registry and distinguish rhombohedral from Bernal stacking. The thickness is tuned from bilayer to more than twenty layers by varying single parameter, the annealing temperature. Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy directly tracks the thickness-dependent evolution from interface-dominated low-energy states toward pronounced near-Fermi-level flat-band spectral weight in thick multilayers. Low-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy on a 17-layer film further reveal a 13.4 meV low-energy spectral reconstruction and a $\sqrt{3} \times \sqrt{3}$ Kekulé-like modulation, providing microscopic signatures consistent with an intervalley-mixed electronic texture. This one-step, transfer-free approach establishes step-bunched SiC as an epitaxial platform that links stacking engineering with moiré-free correlated flat-band electronic states.

</details>


### [142] [First-principles carrier mobility and optical absorption of strained ZnO with self-consistent Hubbard interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29030)
*Hong-Guk Min,Wooil Yang,Sabyasachi Tiwari,Feliciano Giustino,Young-Woo Son*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用新开发的自洽哈伯德校正密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT+U），系统研究了应变氧化锌（ZnO）中声子限制的电子输运与声子辅助光学吸收。研究发现沿[\bar110]方向施加最高4.8%的单轴拉伸应变可使室温电子迁移率提升19%，同时可见光区吸收几乎不变，表明中度应变可选择性增强载流子输运而不损害光学透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 载流子迁移率与光学吸收是氧化物半导体在透明柔性显示领域的关键性能参数，现有方法难以同时准确描述电子-声子相互作用和局域关联效应，亟需一种无参数的理论框架来预测应变调控下的输运与光学性质。

Method: 采用新开发的自洽哈伯德校正密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT+U），该无参数方法同时包含电子-声子相互作用和原位关联效应；在三种不同单轴应变方向上计算了电子结构和声子色散，并在此基础上分析声子限制的电子输运与声子辅助光学吸收。

Result: 沿[\bar110]方向施加高达4.8%的单轴拉伸应变使室温电子迁移率提高19%，同时可见光范围的光学吸收基本保持不变。

Conclusion: 中度应变可选择性增强载流子输运而不损害光学透明度，DFPT+U被确立为预测宽禁带氧化物应变依赖输运与光学性质的有效框架，对应变工程化显示和光电子应用具有参考意义。

Abstract: Carrier mobility and optical absorption are key performance parameters of oxide semiconductors in transparent and flexible displays. We use a newly developed density-functional perturbation theory with a self-consistent Hubbard correction (DFPT+U) to study phonon-limited electron transport and phonon-assisted optical absorption in strained zinc oxide (ZnO). This parameter-free approach accounts for electron-phonon interactions and on-site correlation effects simultaneously. Electronic structures and phonon dispersions are computed under three distinct uniaxial strain directions. Uniaxial tensile strain up to 4.8% along [\bar110] is found to increase the room-temperature electron mobility by 19% while leaving visible-range optical absorption essentially unchanged. These results demonstrate that moderate strain can selectively enhance carrier transport without degrading optical transparency, and establish DFPT+U as an effective framework for predicting strain-dependent transport and optical properties in wide-band-gap oxides with implications for strain-engineered display and optoelectronic applications.

</details>


### [143] [Charge-Density-Wave Phase Selection by Janus-Induced Intrinsic Strain in Monolayer NbSSiAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29084)
*Chun-Jie Zhang,Bing Zhang,Dongliang Mao,Yapeng Wu,Xiao-Ping Li,Lei Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理计算发现，Janus结构导致的内部张应变可将NbS₂中偏离M点的软模趋势重定向至M点，从而在NbSSiAs₂中选择2×2电荷密度波（charge-density-wave, CDW）重构，并进一步通过外加应变调控拓扑与超导电性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维材料中，竞争性的电荷密度波不稳定性之间的相选择仍具挑战性，需要探索内部结构机制来实现对CDW相的有效调控。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算方法，结合电-声耦合（electron-phonon coupling）分析，研究NbSSiAs₂中的晶格不稳定性与CDW重构，并通过双轴应变调控相对构型稳定性。

Result: 研究发现Janus诱导的内部张应变使CDW不稳定点从偏离M点转移至M点，导致2×2 CDW重构；该重构相包含两个近简并的Nb三聚化构型，均可维持约6–7 K尺度的声子介导超导电性；压应变倾向1+3-中空构型并诱导能带反转的Z₂拓扑非平庸态。

Conclusion: Janus诱导的内部应变提供了一条内在的结构路径用于CDW相选择，而外部应变则可在同一材料中实现CDW序、拓扑序与超导电性的共存。

Abstract: Controlling phase selection among competing charge-density-wave (CDW) instabilities remains challenging in two-dimensional materials. Here, first-principles calculations show that Janus-induced intrinsic tensile strain redirects the off-M soft-mode tendency of NbS$_2$ to the M point in NbSSiAs$_2$, selecting a $2\times2$ CDW reconstruction. Electron-phonon coupling analysis identifies momentum-selective coupling between Nb-derived states and a longitudinal acoustic mode as the origin of the M-point instability. The reconstructed phase hosts two nearly degenerate Nb-trimerized configurations whose relative stability is tuned by biaxial strain. Both configurations retain phonon-mediated superconductivity on the 6-7 K scale, indicating the coexistence of CDW order and superconductivity. Compressive strain favors the 1+3-hollow configuration and induces a band-inverted, $Z_2$-nontrivial state while preserving superconductivity. Together, these results identify Janus-induced intrinsic strain as an internal structural route for CDW phase selection, whereas external strain provides access to a regime in which CDW order, topology, and superconductivity coexist.

</details>


### [144] [Domain-Selective Enhancement of Second Harmonic Generation in Monolayer MoS$_2$ via Ferroelectricity-Controlled Photodoping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29152)
*David Hernández-Pinilla,Line Jelver,María Jesús Martínez-Morillo,César Hernando-Fuente,Miquel Cherta,Guillermo López-Polin,Joel D. Cox,Luisa E. Bausá,Mariola O Ramírez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了在单层二硫化钼（MoS₂）与周期性极化铌酸锂（LiNbO₃）集成的混合异质结构中，通过全光、光诱导的铁电畴选择性控制实现二次谐波产生（SHG）的主动调控。研究发现铁电极化控制的界面光掺杂可将有效二阶磁化率χ⁽²⁾增强，使共振激发条件下的SHG强度提升高达约70%，并通过从头算计算为实验结果提供了微观理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维半导体与铁电材料构成的混合异质结构为实现纳米尺度的光-物质相互作用主动控制提供了多功能途径，但如何在无需外部电学偏置的条件下实现对非线性光学响应的可重构调控仍是一个关键挑战。该研究旨在探索利用铁电畴工程与光照相结合的方式，实现二维材料非线性光学响应的可编程调制。

Method: 将单层MoS₂集成到周期性极化LiNbO₃衬底上，利用空间分辨的SHG成像技术对非线性光学响应进行逐域表征；系统考察激发波长和入射光功率对SHG对比度的影响，并通过对比具有和不具有光驱动光掺杂的畴来评估载流子密度对非线性响应的作用；同时结合从头算（ab initio）计算验证电荷掺杂对MoS₂电子能带结构和χ⁽²⁾的影响。

Result: 空间分辨SHG成像揭示了由铁电畴图案调控的单层MoS₂非线性光学响应的显著调制；相反极化畴之间呈现强SHG对比度，且依赖于激发波长和入射光功率；铁电极化控制的界面光掺杂使有效χ⁽²⁾增强，在共振激发条件下SHG强度提升最高约70%。从头算计算证实电荷掺杂改变MoS₂电子能带结构并强烈影响共振区χ⁽²⁾。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，光强与铁电性的结合可作为二维材料能带结构调控的有力手段，实现可重构的非线性光学响应，为可编程频率转换、智能光调制器以及集成混合平台中的先进非线性光子功能开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Hybrid heterostructures combining two-dimensional semiconductors with ferroelectric materials offer a versatile route to actively control light-matter interactions at the nanoscale. Here, we report all-optical, light-induced domain-selective control of second-harmonic generation (SHG) in monolayer molybdenum disulfide (MoS$_2$) integrated with periodically poled lithium niobate (LiNbO$_3$). Spatially resolved SHG imaging reveals a pronounced modulation of the nonlinear optical response of monolayer MoS$_2$ governed by the ferroelectric domain pattern of the underlying substrate. A strong SHG contrast is observed between domains of opposite polarization, with a marked dependence on both the excitation wavelength and the incident optical power. The comparison between ferroelectric domains that either enable or do not exhibit light-driven photodoping in the MoS$_2$ monolayer provides a direct assessment of the role of carrier density in the nonlinear optical response. We find that ferroelectric-polarization-controlled photodoping at the MoS$_2$/LiNbO$_3$ interface enhances the effective second-order susceptibility, $χ^2$, producing an increase in SHG intensity of up to ~70% under resonant excitation conditions. Ab initio calculations corroborate that charge doping modifies the electronic band structure of MoS$_2$ and strongly affects $χ^2$ in the resonant regime, providing microscopic support for the experimentally observed modulation. The results highlight the combination of light intensity and ferroelectricity as a powerful knob for band-structure modulation in 2D materials and reconfigurable nonlinear optical responses, opening pathways toward programmable frequency conversion, smart light modulators, and advanced nonlinear photonic functionalities in integrated hybrid platforms.

</details>


### [145] [Quadratic piezoelectricity from stacking-engineered interference in multilayer sliding ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29214)
*Jiaxuan Fan,Yuexing Zhao,Xiao-Ping Li,Yurong Yang,Xing-Qiu Chen,Lei Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用堆叠工程实现压电干涉（piezoelectric interference）的方法，在多层滑动铁电材料中系统性地压制线性压电系数同时保留高阶（二次）机电响应，并通过第一性原理计算和广义Ginzburg–Landau框架验证了这一可调控的设计原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计非线性压电材料需要在不消除高阶机电响应的前提下抑制线性压电系数，但此前缺乏通用且可重构的调控方案。

Method: 结合第一性原理（first-principles）计算与广义Ginzburg–Landau框架（generalized Ginzburg–Landau framework），将每个层间间隙（interlayer gap）建模为局域压电通道，其符号与大小由堆叠方式决定；通过同号通道的相长干涉（constructive interference）产生线性主导响应，通过异号通道的相消干涉（destructive interference）抑制线性系数并保留有限二次响应。

Result: 代表性MoS₂和NiTe₂多层结构接近抛物线极限；BAAC堆叠的MoS₂相比CBA堆叠的MoS₂，将线性到二次交叉应变（crossover strain）降低了25倍。实验上可达的四层MoS₂滑动路径可以在线性主导、二次主导和符号反转（sign-inverted）状态之间切换。

Conclusion: 堆叠工程干涉被确立为层状材料中可编程非线性电机电学（programmable nonlinear electromechanics）的设计原则。

Abstract: Designing nonlinear piezoelectricity requires suppressing the linear piezoelectric coefficient without extinguishing higher-order electromechanical response, yet a general and reconfigurable route remains lacking. Here we introduce stacking-engineered piezoelectric interference as such a mechanism in multilayer sliding ferroelectrics. Combining first-principles calculations with a generalized Ginzburg--Landau framework, we show that each interlayer gap acts as a local piezoelectric channel whose sign and magnitude are determined by stacking. Constructive interference between same-signed channels produces a linear-dominated response, whereas destructive interference between oppositely signed channels suppresses the linear coefficient while preserving a finite quadratic response. Representative MoS$_2$ and NiTe$_2$ multilayers approach the parabolic limit, with BAAC-stacked MoS$_2$ reducing the linear-to-quadratic crossover strain by a factor of 25 relative to CBA-stacked MoS$_2$. Experimentally accessible tetralayer MoS$_2$ sliding pathways further connect linear-dominated, quadratic-dominated and sign-inverted states. Here, we identify stacking-engineered interference as a design principle for programmable nonlinear electromechanics in layered materials.

</details>


### [146] [Savi-Bhransha: Graph-Theoretic Dislocation-Loop Characterization in Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29328)
*Utkarsh Bhardwaj*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出Savi-Bhransha，一种基于图论的理论方法，直接从局部缺陷位移图案中重构晶体中的间隙与空位位错环，无需构建全局界面网格。该方法适用于BCC、FCC和HCP晶体，能识别Burgers矢量族、惯习面、环尺寸、分段边/螺位错特征及边界与体缺陷群，并在钨、锆及FeNiCr合金的级联模拟中展现出优于传统DXA算法的稳定性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位错环决定晶体材料的性质，但当环碎裂或嵌入致密缺陷碎片时，从原子模拟中提取其详细特征非常困难。现有方法（如DXA）在复杂环境下会产生碎片化、重叠的开放段，难以得到稳定的环级对象。

Method: 提出Savi-Bhransha，一种图论（graph-theoretic）方法，直接从局部缺陷位移图案重建间隙和空位位错环，无需构建全局界面网格。方法可识别Burgers矢量族、惯习面、环尺寸、分段边/螺位错（edge/screw dislocation）特征以及边界和体缺陷群体。

Result: 在单次级联模拟（BCC W和HCP Zr）及连续碰撞级联（BCC W和FCC FeNiCr）中进行了验证。总位错长度与DXA强相关，但Savi-Bhransha在复杂环境中返回更稳定的环级对象。中位运行速度提升6.34倍（BCC W）和8.86倍（HCP Zr），峰值内存最多降低7.77倍。在连续W级联中，边界缺陷浓度与瞬态光栅光谱测量吻合，预测的Burgers矢量分数与室温透射电镜一致。在FCC FeNiCr中解析了Heidenreich-Shockley解离，约91%的幸存<110>族间隙团簇具有Shockley对特征。

Conclusion: Savi-Bhransha能够实现对大型辐照损伤模拟的高效、拓扑分辨分析，并与实验可观测量的直接比较，为辐射损伤研究提供了一种更稳定高效的分析工具。

Abstract: Dislocation loops govern the properties of crystalline materials, but extracting their detailed characteristics from atomistic simulations is difficult when loops are fragmented or embedded in compact defect debris. We present Savi-Bhransha, a graph-theoretic method that reconstructs interstitial and vacancy loops directly from local defect-displacement motifs, without constructing a global interface mesh. The method identifies Burgers-vector family, habit plane, loop size, segment-wise edge/screw character, and boundary and bulk defect populations for BCC, FCC, and HCP crystals. We apply it to single-cascade simulations over a range of energies in BCC W and HCP Zr, and to successive collision cascades in BCC W and FCC FeNiCr. We benchmark the method against the Dislocation Extraction Algorithm (DXA). Total dislocation lengths remain strongly correlated between the two methods, while Savi-Bhransha returns more stable loop-level objects in complex environments where DXA returns fragmented, overlapping open segments. Savi-Bhransha also better resolves mixed-morphology defects and dislocations near other defects, including vacancy clusters. Median runtime speedups are 6.34x for BCC W and 8.86x for HCP Zr, with peak-memory reductions up to 7.77x. In successive W cascades, the resolved boundary-defect concentration brackets transient-grating-spectroscopy measurements and the predicted Burgers-vector fraction agrees with room-temperature TEM. In FCC FeNiCr, the method resolves Heidenreich-Shockley dissociation, with a Shockley-pair signature in about 91% of surviving <110>-family interstitial clusters. Savi-Bhransha therefore enables efficient, topology-resolved analysis of large radiation-damage simulations and direct comparison with experimentally accessible observables.

</details>


### [147] [Resolving competing distortions in Ca0.4Sr0.6TiO3 using complementary electron and X-ray techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29346)
*Robin Sjökvist,Catriona A. Crawford,Richard Beanland,Mark S. Senn*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用变温透射电子显微镜（TEM）和粉末X射线衍射（PXRD）系统研究了钙钛矿Ca0.4Sr0.6TiO3的结构相变机制，发现其在380 K以上从正交Pbcm相转变为四方I4/mcm相，相变过程通过TiO6八面体沿c轴倾斜相干性的丧失实现，形成的纳米级片状微结构在相变温度以上仍持续存在，这可能解释了该材料体系中拉曼光谱与衍射测量长期存在分歧的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该材料体系中拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）与衍射测量之间存在长期分歧，研究者希望通过高分辨率的变温结构表征手段揭示其微观起源，深入理解Ca0.4Sr0.6TiO3的结构相变动力学与微结构演化。

Method: 采用变温透射电子显微镜（variable temperature TEM）和粉末X射线衍射（powder X-ray diffraction, PXRD）对Ca0.4Sr0.6TiO3进行系统表征；利用Δ点和R点暗场TEM成像追踪相变过程中的结构演化，并结合布里渊区M、T、Δ和R点的模式与序参量分析描述结构畸变。

Result: 室温及以下为类似NaNbO3 P相的正交Pbcm结构，380 K以上转变为四方I4/mcm相。PXRD仅观测到R点反射，而电子衍射同时观测到M和R点反射，表明局部对称性与宏观结构存在差异。暗场TEM成像显示相变通过TiO6八面体沿c轴倾斜相干性的丧失进行，形成局部对称性不同于宏观结构的纳米级薄片状微结构，且这些微结构在相变温度以上仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 纳米级片状微结构在相变温度以上的持久存在可能是造成该材料体系拉曼光谱与衍射测量结果长期不一致的根本原因，同时可能还导致了其他次级效应；这一发现强调了相变动力学中局部微结构与宏观平均结构之间差异的重要性。

Abstract: We present a study of the perovskite Ca0.4Sr0.6TiO3 using variable temperature transmission electron microscopy (TEM) and powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD). At room temperature and below, PXRD shows that the material adopts an orthorhombic Pbcm structure analogous to the P-phase of NaNbO3. Above 380 K the material transforms to a tetragonal I4/mcm phase. The structural distortions of these phases can be described as a combination of modes and order parameters associated with the M, T, $Δ$ and R-points of the Brillouin zone, each of which can be associated with a different set of superstructure reflections visible in X-ray and electron diffraction patterns. For the I4/mcm phase only the expected R-point reflections are observed in PXRD while both M and R- reflections are observed in electron diffraction. Using $Δ$ and R dark field TEM images we show that the phase transition proceeds by a loss of coherence of TiO6 octahedral tilting along the c-axis, leading to a microstructure of thin nanoscale platelets with a different local symmetry to the macroscopic structure. These persist well above the phase transition temperature and are probably responsible for the long-standing discrepancy between Raman spectroscopy and diffraction measurements in this materials system, as well as other secondary effects.

</details>


### [148] [Layer- and Field-Dependent Magnetic Order in 2D CrSBr Revealed by Pulsed Nanocalorimetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29395)
*Hugo Gomez-Torres,Roop K. Mech,Alessandra Canetta,Llibertat Abad,Carles Navau,Daniel G. Chica,Xavier Roy,Pascal Gehring,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Aitor Lopeandia,Javier Rodriguez-Viejo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用微秒脉冲加热纳米量热法（nanocalorimetry）测量CrSBr薄片从体相到单层极限的热容和磁熵，揭示了层间反铁磁向层内铁磁关联的维度跨越（dimensional crossover）、层数奇偶效应以及随厚度变化的临界场，确立了纳米量热法作为低维磁性热力学探针的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维极限下磁有序的演化是范德瓦尔斯磁体（van der Waals magnets）研究的核心挑战，但由于剥离薄片质量仅有飞克（femtogram）量级，传统热力学测量受到严格限制，亟需适用于原子级薄材料的敏感热力学探测手段。

Method: 使用微秒脉冲加热纳米量热法测量CrSBr薄片在单层极限下的热容（heat capacity）与磁熵（magnetic entropy），并结合面内易轴方向磁场施加，系统探测不同厚度的热力学响应。

Result: 实验揭示了磁有序相关的熵图景：层间转变温度降低、接近单层极限时熵导出的有效磁矩减少；热力学异常显示从体相层间反铁磁到层内铁磁关联主导的跨越；奇数层样品出现与未补偿磁层相关的额外高温贡献；面内磁场逐步抑制反铁磁序，提取出随厚度减小而降低的临界场；熵分析显示磁涨落（magnetic fluctuations）在层间有序转变温度以上仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 纳米量热法为低维磁性提供了强有力的热力学探测途径，使原子级薄范德瓦尔斯磁体中的磁熵、交换相互作用和维度跨越得以直接测量，填补了该领域热力学测量的空白。

Abstract: Understanding the evolution of magnetic order in the two-dimensional limit remains a central challenge in van der Waals magnets, where thermodynamic measurements are constrained by the femtogram-scale mass of exfoliated flakes. Here, we use microsecond pulse-heating nanocalorimetry to measure the heat capacity and magnetic entropy of CrSBr flakes down to the monolayer limit. The measurements reveal the entropy landscape associated with magnetic ordering, uncovering a reduction of the interlayer transition temperature and an entropy-derived effective magnetic moment approaching the monolayer limit. Thermodynamic anomalies capture a crossover from bulk-like interlayer antiferromagnetism to a regime dominated by intralayer ferromagnetic correlations. A pronounced layer-parity effect further emerges, with odd-layer samples displaying an additional high-temperature contribution associated with uncompensated magnetic layers. Under in-plane magnetic fields applied along the easy axis, antiferromagnetic order is progressively suppressed, allowing extraction of a thickness-dependent critical field reflecting weakened interlayer exchange coupling. Entropy analysis further reveals an extended regime of magnetic fluctuations persisting well above the interlayer ordering transition. Together, these results establish nanocalorimetry as a powerful thermodynamic probe of low-dimensional magnetism, providing direct access to magnetic entropy, exchange interactions, and dimensional crossover in atomically thin van der Waals magnets.

</details>


### [149] [A versatile generalized digital twin for Electron Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29411)
*Thomas W. Pfeifer,Andrew R. Lupini,Eric R. Hoglund*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscopy）的通用数字孪生（digital twin）系统，用于模拟电子束在整个显微镜内部的轨迹，提供透镜定位校准和预测工具，为新型成像与光谱模式的搭建以及自动化显微镜和人工智能集成提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透射电子显微镜需要发展专用的成像与光谱状态以支持新型应用（如柔性的动量分辨高能量分辨率光谱），但这一任务因需对齐和配置多个透镜、以及各成像与衍射平面位置缺乏明确文献记录而变得复杂。

Method: 构建了一个多功能数字孪生代码包，包含用于建模显微镜镜筒的简单工具；提出了多种精确校准透镜位置和标定透镜模型的流程，使模型可作为预测工具；并通过自动改变透镜设置并测量结果变化的闭环程序实现自动校准。

Result: 该模型使使用者能够快速且简便地确定搭建新的聚光镜（condenser）或投影镜（projector）模式所需的正确透镜数值，且自动程序可闭环调节透镜设置并测量相应变化。

Conclusion: 作者认为，随着机器学习和人工智能（AI/ML）工具的加速发展，这种基于物理信息的显微镜模型将成为自动化显微镜和AI/ML集成中的宝贵工具。

Abstract: Development of specialized imaging and spectroscopy states in transmission electron microscopy is needed for novel applications, such as flexible momentum-resolved high energy-resolution spectroscopy. This task is complicated by the need to align and configure many different lenses, and by ambiguity as to the locations of various imaging and diffraction planes, which are often not well documented. Here we develop a versatile digital twin for simulating the electron beam trajectory throughout an electron microscope. Our code package contains simple tools for modeling the microscope column, and we present multiple procedures for dialing in precise lens locations and calibrating lens models. This enables use of the model as a predictive tool, allowing the user to quickly and easily determine the correct lens values for setting up new condenser or projector modes. Automatic procedures to change lens settings and measure the resulting changes can be used to close the loop. With the accelerating development of machine learning and artificial intelligence tools, we also believe a physically-informed model of the microscope can serve as a valuable tool for automated microscopy and AI/ML integration.

</details>


### [150] [A Unified Symmetry Framework For In-plane Anomalous Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29452)
*Xun-Jiang Luo,Yong-Ting Shi,Ding-Fu Shao,Ping Zhang,Ning Hao,Mingliang Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的对称性框架，将自旋空间群（spin space group）与磁性空间群（magnetic space group）联系起来，用于解释面内反常霍尔效应（in-plane anomalous Hall effect, IPAHE）。通过分析磁场对122个磁性点群（magnetic point group）的对称性约化，识别出54个支持IPAHE的群，并预测了包括交替磁体（altermagnet）和奇宇称磁体（odd-parity magnet）在内的多种非常规磁体中的IPAHE。基于该框架筛选MAGNDATA数据库识别候选材料，并通过第一性原理计算验证了两种代表性材料的对称性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面内反常霍尔效应由面内净磁化或外加磁场驱动，挑战了传统反常霍尔效应的范式，但迄今为止缺乏统一的对称性原理来系统理解这一现象。作者旨在建立一个连接磁几何与反常霍尔响应的综合性对称性框架，以弥合这一理论空白。

Method: 作者建立了将自旋空间群（决定磁几何）与磁性空间群（决定反常霍尔响应）联系起来的对称性框架，系统分析了外加磁场对全部122个磁性点群的对称性约化过程，并据此识别出支持IPAHE的54个点群。此外，利用该框架对MAGNDATA数据库进行材料筛选，并对代表性候选材料开展第一性原理计算验证。

Result: 研究结果表明，自发IPAHE可产生于铁磁体中，当自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）诱导的自旋群对称性破缺允许额外净磁化方向时即会出现。对称性分析揭示了面内旋转磁场下霍尔电导（Hall conductance）呈现一、二或三重角谐波（angular harmonic）特征，为非常规磁性提供了对称性分辨的指纹信号。通过数据库筛选确定了支持自发或场诱导IPAHE的候选材料，涵盖铁磁体、反铁磁体（antiferromagnet）及非常规磁体，并经由第一性原理计算确认了对称性预测。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的对称性框架，不仅系统解释了IPAHE的起源，还为预测具有IPAHE响应的新型磁性材料提供了理论工具。所识别的角谐波指纹（angular harmonic fingerprint）可用于实验上分辨非常规磁性的类型，为后续材料发现和实验验证铺平了道路。

Abstract: The in-plane anomalous Hall effect (IPAHE), driven by an in-plane net magnetization or an applied magnetic field, challenges the conventional anomalous Hall paradigm. Despite growing interest, a unified symmetry principle governing these phenomena has remained elusive. Here, we establish a comprehensive symmetry framework that bridges the spin space group, which dictates the magnetic geometry, with the magnetic space group, which governs the anomalous Hall response. We show that spontaneous IPAHE can emerge in ferromagnets when spin-orbit-coupling-induced spin-group symmetry breaking permits additional net magnetization directions. For field-induced IPAHE, we analyze how an applied magnetic field reduces the symmetries of all 122 magnetic point groups and identify 54 groups that support IPAHE. Our framework naturally predicts IPAHE in a broad class of unconventional magnets, including altermagnets and odd-parity magnets. In particular, symmetry analysis reveals characteristic one-, two-, or three-fold angular harmonics of the Hall conductance under an in-plane rotating field, providing a symmetry-resolved fingerprint for unconventional magnetism. Using this framework, we screen the MAGNDATA database and identify candidate materials supporting spontaneous or field-induced IPAHE, encompassing ferromagnets, antiferromagnets, and unconventional magnets. Finally, we validate the symmetry predictions through first-principles calculations for two representative materials.

</details>


### [151] [Tuneable magnetic behaviour, electronic structure and nitrogen vacancy formation in Gd$_{x}$Sm$_{1-x}$N](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29472)
*Omri Porat,Elma Joshy,Jackson Miller,Simon Granville,William Holmes-Hewett*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过计算与实验相结合的方法，系统研究了稀土氮化物Gd_xSm_{1-x}N的电子与磁性性质，发现阳离子取代可使内交换场改变约20%，并使矫顽力在多个数量级上变化；同时发现氮空位缺陷更倾向于在Sm离子邻近位点形成，其形成能显著低于Gd离子邻近位点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土氮化物是唯一一类兼具内禀铁磁半导体性质的系列材料，其自旋与未淬灭轨道角动量（unquenched orbital angular momentum）的相互作用可产生丰富的磁性行为，且磁性可通过多镧系离子的组合进行精细调控，因此深入理解阳离子取代对其磁性和电子结构的影响具有重要意义。

Method: 本研究采用第一性原理计算与实验测量相结合的方式，对Gd_xSm_{1-x}N的电子结构和磁性性质进行系统表征，分析阳离子取代对内交换场和能带结构的影响；同时通过计算研究氮空位缺陷在该材料中的形成行为，考察不同阳离子邻近位点对缺陷形成能的影响。

Result: 研究发现，通过阳离子取代，Gd_xSm_{1-x}N的矫顽场（coercive field）可发生多个数量级的变化，而内交换场仅改变约20%；此外，含较高Sm含量的薄膜中氮空位浓度升高，且Sm离子邻近位点的氮空位形成能显著低于Gd离子邻近位点，表明空位更易在Sm附近形成。

Conclusion: 阳离子取代对Gd_xSm_{1-x}N的磁性调控效果显著，相关材料参数的精确控制对超导自旋电子学（superconducting spintronics）领域至关重要；氮空位缺陷在Sm邻近位点的优先形成揭示了Sm掺杂对缺陷化学的显著影响，为稀土氮化物材料的设计与应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The rare earth nitrides are the only series of intrinsic ferromagnetic semiconductors where the interplay of spin and unquenched orbital angular momentum provides access to a range of magnetic behaviour. Furthermore, the magnetic properties can be finely tuned through the combination of multiple lanthanide ions in the nitride. Here we present a combined computational and experimental study on the electronic and magnetic properties of Gd$_x$Sm$_{1-x}$N and discuss the effect of cation substitution on the internal exchange field and band structure. We find that as the coercive field of Gd$_x$Sm$_{1-x}$N changes over orders of magnitude via cation substitution the internal exchange field changes by $\sim$20%. Control of these material properties is vital in the field of superconducting spintronics. Finally, motivated by an enhanced concentration of nitrogen vacancies in films with higher Sm content, we investigate computationally the formation of nitrogen vacancy defects in Gd$_x$Sm$_{1-x}$N finding that the formation energy is significantly reduced for vacancy sites adjacent to Sm ions rather than Gd ions.

</details>


### [152] [Ordered-to-disordered transfer learning with graph neural networks for formation-energy and HOMO-LUMO gap prediction in high-entropy perovskite oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29510)
*Panupol Untarabut,Narjes Jomaa,Sylvian Cadars,Olivier Masson,Samuel Bernard,Assil Bouzid,Santanu Saha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用图神经网络（graph neural networks, GNNs）研究有序到无序高熵钙钛矿氧化物（high-entropy perovskite oxides, HEPOs）的迁移学习，评估四种GNN模型在形成能和HOMO-LUMO带隙预测中的迁移性能，发现形成能预测可有效迁移而带隙预测迁移性有限，且三体几何信息编码对迁移性提升至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵钙钛矿氧化物成分空间庞大且存在化学/结构无序性，给准确性质预测带来显著挑战；GNN虽能快速探索材料空间，但受限于代表性训练数据的可得性。研究者旨在通过从有序钙钛矿迁移先验知识的方法来克服数据稀缺瓶颈。

Method: 采用有序到无序迁移学习策略，将从化学有序钙钛矿学到的知识迁移至无序HEPOs的形成能与HOMO-LUMO带隙预测；系统评估四种代表性GNN模型（CGCNN、GATGNN、ALIGNN和M3GNet），覆盖两体与三体相互作用的结构表示；并利用UMAP进行表示层面的分析。

Result: 发现强性质依赖的迁移行为：形成能预测可有效迁移至无序HEPOs，而HOMO-LUMO带隙预测因对局部化学环境敏感而迁移性有限；加入少量HEPO特有训练数据显著改善带隙预测性能；UMAP分析表明编码三体几何信息的表示（如ALIGNN）对捕获复杂结构-性质关系及提升迁移性更为重要。

Conclusion: 三体几何信息编码（如ALIGNN所采用的）对提高GNN在无序材料中的迁移性能至关重要，且结合少量目标域数据补充是一种有效的实用策略。

Abstract: High-entropy perovskite oxides (HEPOs) represent a chemically complex class of materials with promising functional properties, yet their vast compositional space and, chemical/structural disorder pose significant challenge for accurate property prediction. Graph neural networks (GNNs) enable rapid exploration of materials space but are often limited by the availability of representative training data. Here, we investigate ordered-to-disordered transfer learning using GNNs for formation-energy and HOMO-LUMO gap prediction in HEPOs by transferring knowledge learned from chemically ordered perovskites. Four representative GNN models, including CGCNN, GATGNN, ALIGNN and M3GNet are evaluated to understand the role of structural representations, spanning pairwise two-body and angular three-body interactions in transfer performance. We find strong property-dependent transfer behavior: formation-energy prediction transfers effectively to disordered HEPOs, whereas HOMO-LUMO gap prediction shows limited transferability due to its sensitivity to local chemical environments. Incorporating a small HEPO-specific training dataset substantially improves HOMO-LUMO gap prediction. Representation-level analysis using UMAP further highlights the importance of encoding three-body geometric information such as in ALIGNN for capturing complex structure-property relationships and improving transferability.

</details>


### [153] [Hidden chiral signatures in ferroaxial K2Zr(PO4)2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29573)
*Nora Taufertshöfer,Awadhesh Narayan,Nicola A. Spaldin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）和多极分析（multipole analyses），揭示了原型铁轴性材料（ferroaxial material）K₂Zr(PO₄)₂中铁轴性与手性（chirality）之间的内在联系：顺轴相（paraxial phase）是非手性的，而铁轴相（ferroaxial phase）是反铁手性的；施加电场可诱导出具有净电子手性的铁手性态（ferri-chiral state），并在动量空间中引起贝里曲率偶极张量（Berry curvature dipole tensor）的非零分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁轴性材料中是否存在隐藏手性及其在实空间和动量空间中的表现形式尚不明确。作者希望通过多极分析和第一性原理计算，阐明铁轴性与手性之间的本质关联。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算与多极分析方法，以原子位电环形单极子（atomic-site electric toroidal monopole）作为实空间中电子手性的度量，对原型铁轴性材料K₂Zr(PO₄)₂的相结构进行系统分析；并通过施加外电场诱导手性态，考察其在实空间和动量空间的响应。

Result: 研究表明，K₂Zr(PO₄)₂的顺轴相是非手性的，而铁轴相是反铁手性的（antiferro-chiral）；施加电场后可诱导出具有净电子手性的铁手性态，该手性方向与铁轴畴的取向相反，且可随电场的方向和强度进行调控；同时，该诱导手性在动量空间中对应贝里曲率偶极张量的非零分量，且该分量在相反的铁手性畴之间符号翻转。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了铁轴性材料中在实空间和动量空间中均存在的隐藏手性，表明铁轴性（ferroaxiality）可作为手性调控的平台，为相关材料的功能化应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We use first-principles calculations and multipole analyses to demonstrate the relationship between ferroaxiality and chirality in the prototypical ferroaxial material K2Zr(PO4)2. Using the atomic-site electric toroidal monopole as a measure of electronic chirality in real space, we show that, while the paraxial phase of K2Zr(PO4)2 is non-chiral, the ferroaxial phase is antiferro-chiral. By applying an electric field, we induce a ferri-chiral state with net electronic chirality which is opposite for opposite underlying ferroaxial domains and can be tuned by the direction and strength of the electric field. Associated with the real-space induced chirality, we find a distinct response in momentum space, with induced non-zero components in the Berry curvature dipole tensor that switch sign between opposite ferri-chiral domains. Our findings therefore reveal hidden chirality in ferroaxial materials in both real and momentum space.

</details>
