# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [A Group-Theoretical Framework for Local k-Space Topology and Berry Phase in 2D Photonic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29356)
*Xinyi Yuan,Grazia Salerno*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个受固体物理学中不可约表示（irreducible representation）形式启发的理论框架，专门用于分析二维光子晶体（2D photonic crystals）中的局域k空间拓扑性质，包括连续谱中的束缚态（bound states in the continuum）、光学涡旋光束（optical vortex beams）和贝里相位（Berry phase），并从群论视角探讨如何通过结构设计和对称性微扰来调控这些拓扑特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维光子晶体作为开放性系统能够操控多种布洛赫模式（Bloch modes），但缺乏一套同时纳入辐射耦合（radiative coupling）和横截条件（transverse condition）等光子特性、且可用于系统分析其对称性起源拓扑现象的理论框架。

Method: 借鉴固体物理中的不可约表示形式，构建一套同时显式纳入光子布洛赫系统关键特性的理论框架；从群论角度分析局域k空间拓扑的对称性起源，并考察不同截断方法（truncation approaches）下布洛赫模式的形式应用，均在一般的n能带（n-band）构型下展开。

Result: 在两个代表性系统中阐明了连续谱束缚态、光学涡旋光束及贝里相位的对称性来源；指出了不同截断方法各自适用的优选取参数区间；该分析可扩展至超越标量薛定谔类算符和两能级处理的广泛人工波晶体（artificial wave crystals）。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论框架为光子布洛赫系统的拓扑现象提供了系统的对称性分析工具，能够推广到更广泛的人工波晶体体系，从而为结构设计与对称性微扰对拓扑特征的操控提供理论指导。

Abstract: Two-dimensional photonic crystals (2D PhCs) enable fine-grained control over a broad set of Bloch modes without the constraints of band occupancy and natural crystal structures, and, as intrinsically open systems, serve as versatile platforms for exploring diverse topological phenomena. Here, we develop a theoretical framework inspired by the irreducible-representation formalism in solid-state physics, while explicitly incorporating key characteristics of photonic Bloch systems, such as radiative coupling and transverse condition. Within this framework, we study the symmetry origins of local k-space topology, e.g., bound-states in the continuum and optical vortex beams, and Berry phase in two representative systems. We further analyze, from a group-theory perspective, how tailored structural designs and targeted symmetry perturbations can be exploited to manipulate these topological features. In particular, we showcase the application of the formalism to Bloch modes from distinct truncation approaches and specify the preferable regimes for each, both under a generic $n$-band configuration. The analysis can thereby be readily extended to a wide range of artificial wave crystals beyond scalar Schrödinger-like operators and two-level treatment.

</details>


### [155] [Ascorbic acid mitigates oxidative structural degradation in bovine spermatozoa: a label-free quantitative phase microscopy study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29428)
*Harpreet Kaur,Shubham Tiwari,Aman Kumar,Sandeep Kumar Jha,Satish Kumar Dubey,Dalip Singh Mehta*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本研究利用定量相位显微镜（quantitative phase microscopy, QPM）作为无标记、高通量的方法，评估抗坏血酸对Sahiwal公牛精子氧化应激损伤的保护作用。研究发现急性氧化应激导致精子光学厚度和干质量显著下降，而8 mg/ml抗坏血酸处理能显著减轻这些结构损伤，提示QPM生物物理参数可作为评估精子氧化损伤的无标记结构指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧化应激（oxidative stress）是辅助生殖技术（assisted reproduction technology, ART）中低生育率的关键因素，导致精子质量下降。传统评估依赖明场显微镜，缺乏定量灵敏度且可能引入细胞毒性，因此需要一种无标记、定量且高通量的方法来表征精子在氧化应激下的亚细胞结构和生物物理变化。

Method: 实验通过过氧化氢（H2O2）诱导Sahiwal公牛精子产生严重氧化应激，并设置1 mg/ml和8 mg/ml两种剂量的抗坏血酸处理组以评估剂量依赖性抗氧化恢复效果。采用QPM量化关键生物物理参数，包括干质量（dry mass）、光学厚度（optical thickness）、体积、表面积、球形度和表面积体积比，以及细胞内纹理参数。

Result: 急性氧化应激下，牛精子表现出光学厚度和干质量的显著降低，以及细胞内结构组织的可测量变化，表明氧化应激诱导的形态学降解。抗坏血酸共处理产生部分且剂量依赖性的结构保护，其中8 mg/ml组相比1 mg/ml组显示出统计学上显著的减缓作用。

Conclusion: QPM衍生的生物物理参数可能作为无标记的结构性指标，用于表征牛精子氧化损伤。未来研究应纳入功能验证，以确定这种结构保护能否转化为ART中妊娠结局的改善。

Abstract: Oxidative stress is a key factor in low fertility outcomes during assisted reproduction technology (ART) and contributes to poor sperm quality. Conventional assessment relies on bright-field microscopy, which lacks the quantitative sensitivity to resolve subcellular structural and biophysical changes without exogenous contrast agents, a requirement that can introduce cytotoxic effects and compromise cell viability. This study uses QPM as a label-free, high-throughput method to demonstrate how ascorbic acid reduces the impact of oxidative structural degradation in Sahiwal bovine spermatozoa. To illustrate this pathology, severe oxidative stress was experimentally induced using hydrogen peroxide (H2O2), and two doses of ascorbic acid (1 mg/ml and 8 mg/ml) were tested to evaluate dose-dependent antioxidant recovery in structurally damaged sperm. QPM was used to quantify structural changes in key biophysical parameters, including dry mass, optical thickness, volume, surface area, sphericity, and surface area-to-volume ratio, as well as intracellular texture parameters. Under acute oxidative stress, bovine spermatozoa exhibited significant reductions in optical thickness and dry mass, alongside measurable changes in intracellular structural organization, collectively indicative of oxidative stress-induced morphological degradation. Co-treatment with ascorbic acid resulted in partial, dose-dependent structural preservation, with the 8 mg/ml formulation demonstrating statistically significant attenuation of these structural changes compared to the 1 mg/ml treatment group. These findings suggest that QPM-derived biophysical parameters may serve as promising, label-free structural indicators for characterizing oxidative damage in bovine spermatozoa. Future studies should incorporate functional validation to determine whether this structural preservation translates to improved outcomes in ART.

</details>


### [156] [High-NA vectorial hologram optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29505)
*Michael Wischert,Fiona Hellstern,Paul Uerlings,Tilman Pfau,Stephan Welte,Ralf Klemt*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于矢量相位（vectorial phase-only）框架的计算机生成全息图（computer-generated holograms）梯度优化方法。通过使用可微的Richards-Wolf前向模型，该方法解决了高数值孔径（NA）光学系统中标量近似的精度退化问题，在NA=0.9下实现了99.97%（平顶）和99.98%（光镊阵列）的均匀度，并额外支持依赖局部偏振的目标优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高数值孔径（high numerical aperture, NA）光学系统中，标准的标量近似（如Fraunhofer和Debye衍射模型）常面临显著的保真度退化，限制了计算机生成全息图的优化精度。摘要指出需要一种能够准确描述深非近轴（deep non-paraxial）区域中光场传播的矢量框架，以克服标量模型的固有局限。

Method: 提出一个矢量相位的梯度优化框架，采用可微的Richards-Wolf（richards-wolf）前向模型作为衍射传播的精确矢量描述。该框架针对典型目标强度类别（包括光镊阵列和扩展平顶轮廓）进行优化，并通过直接优化光学偶极势（optical-dipole-potential）目标来验证对局部偏振（local polarization）依赖目标的处理能力，其中包含了矢量和张量光移（vector and tensor light-shift）项。

Result: 在NA=0.9条件下，Richards-Wolf优化分别达到平顶和光镊阵列99.97%和99.98%的均匀度，平均光镊椭圆率（ellipticity）为ε=1.007；相比之下标量前向模型表现出目标依赖的平台保真度或焦几何退化。在偏振敏感的偶极势目标优化中，包含矢量和张量光移项的RW势直接优化将平均归一化残差降低了一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该矢量相位框架在高NA条件下显著优于标量近似的优化精度，同时因其返回完整矢量场，可以自然扩展到依赖局部偏振的复杂优化目标，在光镊和光势设计等应用中具有广阔前景。

Abstract: We present a vectorial phase-only framework for the gradient-based optimization of computer-generated holograms. Our approach addresses a critical limitation in high numerical aperture (NA) optical systems, where standard scalar approximations often experience significant fidelity degradation. We highlight the limitations of scalar diffraction models (Fraunhofer and Debye) at high NA for representative classes of target intensities, namely tweezer arrays and extended flat-top profiles. Using a differentiable Richards-Wolf forward model, we numerically demonstrate stable algorithmic convergence and the generation of highly uniform intensity profiles in the deep non-paraxial regime. At NA=0.9, Richards-Wolf (RW) optimization reaches flat-top and tweezer uniformities of 99.97 % and 99.98 %, respectively, with a mean tweezer ellipticity of $\varepsilon=$1.007, while scalar forward models show target-dependent degradation in plateau fidelity or focal geometry. Furthermore, because the RW model returns the full vectorial field, the same framework can optimize objectives that depend on local polarization. We demonstrate this for a polarization-sensitive optical-dipole-potential target, where direct RW-potential optimization reduces the mean normalized residual by more than an order of magnitude when including vector and tensor light-shift terms.

</details>

### [157] [Observation of a Charged Charmoniumlike Structure in $e^+e^-\rightarrow D^0D^-π^++c.c.$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29138)
*BESIII Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII探测器在4682 MeV质心能量下采集的$e^+e^- \rightarrow D^0D^-π^++c.c.$过程数据进行分波分析（partial wave analysis），发现在$D^0D^-$质量阈值附近存在一个显著性为5.3σ的带电结构$X(3790)^-$，其量子数为$J^P=1^-$，并测量了其质量与耦合强度；同时报告了$D_2^*(2460)$、$D_1^*(2600)$和$D_1^*(2760)$粲介子的质量与宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找和研究近阈值处可能存在的带电粲介子结构，有助于深入理解强子谱与量子色动力学（QCD）的低能行为。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在质心能量4682 MeV采集的数据，对$e^+e^- \rightarrow D^0D^-π^++c.c.$过程进行分波分析，假设该结构满足Flatté形式（Flatté description）来描述近阈值共振，并估算包括系统不确定性和look-elsewhere效应在内的显著性。

Result: 发现带电结构$X(3790)^-$，显著性为5.3σ（含系统不确定性与look-elsewhere效应）；其量子数确定为$J^P=1^-$，质量与对$D^0D^-$道的耦合强度分别为$(3794.7 \pm 6.0 \pm 8.7)$ MeV/$c^2$和$(1.6 \pm 0.2 \pm 0.2)$ GeV。此外还测量了$D_2^*(2460)$、$D_1^*(2600)$和$D_1^*(2760)$粲介子的质量与宽度。

Conclusion: 该研究为近阈值带电粲介子结构$X(3790)^-$的存在提供了显著证据，并确定了其自旋-宇称量子数，同时给出了多个激发态粲介子的质量与宽度测量值。

Abstract: We present a partial wave analysis of the process $e^+e^- \rightarrow D^0 D^- π^++c.c.$ using a data sample collected with the BESIII detector at a center-of-mass energy of 4682~MeV. A charged structure, referred to as the $X(3790)^-$ near the $D^0D^-$ mass threshold with a significance of $5.3σ$, which includes systematic uncertainties and the look-elsewhere effect. Assuming a Flatté description of the structure, the quantum numbers of the $X(3790)^-$ are found to be $J^{P}=1^{-}$, and its mass and coupling strength to the $D^0D^-$ channel are determined to be $(3794.7 \pm 6.0 \pm 8.7)$~MeV/$c^2$ and $(1.6 \pm 0.2 \pm 0.2)$~GeV, respectively, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic. In addition, we also report measurements of the masses and widths of the $D_{2}^{*}(2460)$, $D_{1}^{*}(2600)$, and $D_{1}^{*}(2760)$ charmed mesons.

</details>


### [158] [Constraining Axion-Like-Particles with germanium detector at the Kuo-Sheng Reactor Neutrino Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29205)
*TEXONO Collaboration,Greeshma C.,L. Singh,H. T. Wong,J. -W. Chen,M. Deniz,H. B. Li,S. Karadağ,S. Karmakar,J. Li,F. K. Lin,S. T. Lin,C. -P. Liu,S. K. Liu,H. Ma,D. K. Mishra,M. K. Pandey,K. Rani,R. Raj,V. Sharma,M. K. Singh,M. K. Singh,V. Singh,C. -P. Wu,L. T. Yang,Q. Yue*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究基于台湾国圣核电站TEXONO实验数据，利用高纯度锗探测器搜索轴子类粒子（ALPs），未发现显著超出，并在90%置信水平下给出了ALP与光子及电子的耦合上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子类粒子（Axion-Like Particles, ALPs）是超出标准模型的理论候选粒子，其与光子和电子的耦合参数尚未被充分约束，需要实验搜索来完善参数空间。

Method: 利用位于国圣核电站、距2.9 GW反应堆堆芯28 m处的1.06 kg高纯度锗探测器收集了278.91天反应堆开启（reactor-ON）和43.60天关闭（reactor-OFF）数据，通过Primakoff和康普顿类散射（Compton-like scattering）通道搜索ALP产生，并采用ON-OFF谱相减结合低本底探测器数据进行联合分析。

Result: 在反应堆ON与OFF数据相减后的残差谱中未观察到显著超出事件；在1 eV至3 MeV的ALP质量范围内，于90%置信水平下给出了ALP-光子耦合（g_{aγγ}）与ALP-电子耦合（g_{aee}）的上限，并通过对两个耦合通道同时作为活跃参数的联合分析获得了综合约束。

Conclusion: 该实验结果是现有ALP耦合参数空间的重要补充约束，表明在当前探测灵敏度和本底水平下，所考察质量区间内未发现ALP存在的证据。

Abstract: We present results of a search for Axion-Like Particles (ALPs) produced $via$ Primakoff and Compton-like scattering channels, using data acquired with TEXONO experiment at the Kuo-Sheng Nuclear Power Station. The analysis is based on 278.91 days of reactor-ON and 43.60 days of reactor-OFF data. These datasets were collected using a 1.06 kg high-purity germanium detector located 28 m from a 2.9 GW reactor core. No significant excess is observed in the residual spectrum from the reactor-ON and reactor-OFF data subtraction. Using data acquired with low-background germanium detectors, upper limits on both the ALP-photon ($g_{aγγ}$) and ALP-electron ($g_{aee}$) couplings are derived for ALP masses ranging from 1 eV to 3 MeV at 90\% confidence level. Since both $g_{aγγ}$ and $g_{aee}$ couplings contribute to ALP production and detection, a combined analysis is performed by treating both channels as active parameters.

</details>

### [159] [Meissner Effect and Josephson Radiation in Driven Dissipative Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28734)
*Carl Philipp Zelle,Subir Sachdev*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出"驱动—耗散凝聚"（driven-dissipative condensation）机制解释光诱导超导现象：光学泵浦产生有效增益克服配对场的固有阻尼，稳定一个相位以非公度频率旋转的非平衡凝聚态，该态在长波极限下仍呈现常规超导电磁学特征，并可通过约瑟夫森结实验进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个材料在远高于平衡临界温度的条件下被观测到光诱导超导特征（包括类似迈斯纳效应的磁场排斥），目前缺乏统一的机制解释。作者旨在提出一种理论框架，将这一现象归因于驱动—耗散超导序参量凝聚，而非平衡态超导的延续。

Method: 作者建立了一个唯象连续场论（phenomenological continuum theory），将缓慢旋转的超导序参量与守恒电荷密度及电磁规范场相耦合。该理论在三维与二维情形下分别推导了系统的长波电磁响应，并设计了一个基于约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）的直接实验检验方案。

Result: 三维体系中，驱动—耗散态呈现静态迈斯纳效应（static Meissner effect）及由 Anderson–Higgs 机制产生的带隙等离激元（gapped plasmon）；二维薄层中表现为 Pearl 屏蔽（Pearl screening）和特征的平方根等离激元色散（square-root plasmon dispersion）。约瑟夫森结在零外加电压下支持交流约瑟夫森电流，并辐射出与凝聚态本征旋转频率对应的电磁波。

Conclusion: 这种驱动—耗散超导态与平衡超导、以及锁定于驱动频率的配对态在本质上不同，其低频、随泵浦变化且通常与驱动频率不可公度（incommensurate）的辐射频率可作为关键性实验判据。

Abstract: Photo-induced superconducting signatures have been observed in several materials at temperatures far above their equilibrium critical temperatures, including magnetic field expulsion as in the Meissner effect. We propose driven-dissipative condensation of the superconducting order parameter as a mechanism for this phenomenon. In such a theory, optical pumping generates an effective gain (possibly via a parametric resonance) that overcomes the intrinsic damping of the pairing field, and stabilizes a non-equilibrium condensate whose phase rotates at an intrinsic frequency that is generally lower than, and incommensurate with, the drive frequency. We develop a phenomenological continuum theory that couples this slowly rotating order parameter to the conserved charge density and the electromagnetic gauge field. Despite its finite-frequency dynamics, the resulting state exhibits the conventional long-wavelength electromagnetic signatures of superconductivity. In three dimensions, it displays a static Meissner effect and a gapped plasmon generated by the Anderson--Higgs mechanism. In a two-dimensional sheet, it exhibits Pearl screening and the characteristic square-root plasmon dispersion. We further propose a direct experimental test based on a Josephson junction between the driven-dissipative state and an equilibrium superconductor. The junction supports an AC Josephson current at zero applied voltage and emits radiation at the intrinsic rotation frequency of the condensate. Its low, pump-dependent, and generally incommensurate frequency provides a clear signature distinguishing driven-dissipative superconductivity from equilibrium and drive-locked pairing states.

</details>


### [160] [Nonlinear Meissner States, Vortex Sheets, and Laminar Structures in Extreme Type-II Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29608)
*Eugene B. Kolomeisky*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文证明一个近来得出的极端第二类超导体非线性速度理论存在一维精确可解扇区，其解包括具有普适尾部的非线性迈斯纳态、涡旋片和周期层状结构，后者可解释为粗粒化的阿布里科索夫涡旋结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端第二类超导体的非线性速度理论此前已被推导，但尚未证明其存在可解析求解的扇区，且其解的物理结构未被系统阐明。本文旨在揭示该理论在一维情形下的可解性，并厘清解的物理意义。

Method: 文章通过构造理论的一维精确可解扇区（exactly solvable one-dimensional sector）来分析非线性迈斯纳态（nonlinear Meissner states）、涡旋片（vortex sheets）与周期层状解，并利用孤子（soliton）与半孤子（half-soliton）的概念对解的拓扑结构进行刻画。

Result: 非线性迈斯纳态表现出普适尾部（universal tails）；热力学临界场（thermodynamic critical field）由与正常-超导边界等价的边缘迈斯纳廓线涌现，可视为半孤子；涡旋片是速度场不连续、磁场连续且局域化、中心超导密度为零的孤子，对应密集阿布里科索夫涡旋行（Abrikosov vortex row）的粗粒化极限；周期解描述层状态，可解释为粗粒化的矩形涡旋晶格（rectangular vortex lattices）。

Conclusion: 该非线性速度理论在一维情形下具有丰富的精确解结构，涵盖从孤子到周期晶格的各种态，且这些态与超导体中实际存在的涡旋构型之间存在清晰的粗粒化对应关系，为理解极端第二类超导体的非线性行为提供了新的解析框架。

Abstract: A recently derived nonlinear velocity theory of extreme type-II superconductors is shown to possess an exactly solvable one-dimensional sector. Its solutions include nonlinear Meissner states exhibiting universal tails, vortex sheets, and periodic laminar structures. The thermodynamic critical field emerges from a marginal Meissner profile equivalent to the normal-superconducting boundary and may be viewed as a half-soliton. The vortex sheet is a soliton characterized by a discontinuity of the velocity field, a continuous and localized magnetic field and vanishing superconducting density at its center, and may be interpreted as the coarse-grained limit of a dense row of Abrikosov vortices. Periodic solutions describe laminar states that may be interpreted as coarse-grained rectangular vortex lattices.

</details>


### [161] [Continuous Tuning of the Charge-Phase Uncertainty in a Josephson Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29636)
*Irena Padniuk,Xianzhe Zeng,Juan Carlos Cuevas,Joachim Ankerhold,Klaus Kern,Christian R. Ast*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究利用扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscope）的可调隧穿结，在毫开尔文温度下连续调节约瑟夫森能与充电能的比值，在单个结中观测到非相干与相干库珀对流的平滑转变，从而以受控方式揭示了量子—经典转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学约束共轭可观测量无法同时以任意精度测量。在约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）中，转移电荷与超导域间的量子力学相位差互为共轭量，哪一个发生涨落决定了超电流的不同性质：一个极限是耗散性的单库珀对（Cooper pair）涓流，另一个极限则是相干的无耗散流动。然而，由于约瑟夫森能和充电能在器件制造时被固定，在单一器件中沟通这两个区域一直难以实现。

Method: 作者利用扫描隧道显微镜的可调隧穿结，在毫开尔文温度下连续改变约瑟夫森能与充电能的比值，跨越多个数量级，从而在单一结中实现了两种极限之间的连续调节。

Result: 作者在单个结中监测到了非相干与相干库珀对流动之间的平滑转变，即以受控方式揭示了量子到经典的转变过程。

Conclusion: 该工作表明利用可调隧穿结可以在同一器件中连续跨越两种超电流极限，为受控研究量子—经典转变提供了一种有效的实验平台。

Abstract: Quantum mechanics constrains conjugate observables from being simultaneously measurable with arbitrary precision. In a Josephson junction, these are the transferred electric charge and the quantum-mechanical phase difference between the superconducting domains. Which of them fluctuates determines the supercurrent: a dissipative trickle of single Cooper pairs in one limit, a coherent dissipationless flow in the other. Bridging both regimes in one device remained elusive because the Josephson and charging energies are fixed at fabrication. Here, we use the tunable tunnel junction of a scanning tunneling microscope at millikelvin temperature to vary their ratio continuously over many orders of magnitude. In this way, we monitor the smooth transition between incoherent and coherent Cooper pair flow in a single junction, revealing the quantum-to-classical transition in a controlled way.

</details>

### [162] [A new upper bound on the dimer constant of $\mathbb{Z}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28810)
*Qidong He*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明立方晶格（cubic lattice）上的二聚体常数（dimer constant）上界为0.452130，优于Lundow（2001年）通过Ciucu（1998年）传递矩阵方法得到的0.457547上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在改进立方晶格二聚体常数已知最优上界。此前的最好上界由Lundow在2001年利用传递矩阵方法得到，作者希望通过新的构造方法获得更紧的上界。

Method: 作者采用对角传递层（diagonal transfer layers）构造方法，并利用Csikvári（2017年）的不等式将所得矩阵的谱半径与二聚体常数建立联系，而非使用矩形环面特有的对称性。通过类似于Lundow的压缩方法降低传递矩阵维度，并使用由Friedland–Schneider（1980年）渐近结果派生的Collatz–Wielandt公式变体，获得一个单调收敛于真实谱半径的上界序列，每一项无需构造完整矩阵即可计算。

Result: 成功将立方晶格二聚体常数的上界从0.457547改进至0.452130。

Conclusion: 新的构造与计算方法在无需依赖矩形环面特殊对称性的条件下，实现了对二聚体常数上界的显著改进，为后续进一步优化该常数提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We prove that the dimer constant of the cubic lattice is bounded above by $0.452130$, improving the previous best upper bound of $0.457547$ obtained by Lundow (2001) using the transfer-matrix method of Ciucu (1998). Our construction instead uses diagonal transfer layers and an inequality of Csikvári (2017) to relate the spectral radius of the resulting matrix to the dimer constant, rather than exploiting symmetry special to the rectangular torus. We reduce the dimension of the transfer matrix using a compression method similar to Lundow's. Finally, we use a variant of the Collatz--Wielandt formula, derived from an asymptotic result of Friedland--Schneider (1980), to obtain a monotone sequence of upper bounds converging to the true spectral radius, each of which can be computed without forming the full matrix.

</details>
